GroupAlgebra.lean

源码 SHA-256：1c6c0899fe9bc9f7860f291ba7c5573353d18a3c5809258769d689bc39da8ad4

  1 import Mathlib.Algebra.MonoidAlgebra.Basic
  2 import Mathlib.Algebra.MonoidAlgebra.Support
  3 import Mathlib.Analysis.Complex.Basic
  4 
  5 /-!
  6 # The complex group algebra of an arbitrary group
  7 
  8 This file verifies the algebraic starting point for the reduced group C*-algebra.
  9 Multiplication is Mathlib's convolution product, not pointwise multiplication.
 10 The involution conjugates coefficients and reverses group elements.
 11 No topology, countability, regular representation, or completion is assumed here.
 12 -/
 13 
 14 noncomputable section
 15 
 16 namespace BC4lean
 17 
 18 /-- Finitely supported complex coefficients with convolution multiplication. -/
 19 abbrev GroupAlgebra (Γ : Type*) := MonoidAlgebra ℂ Γ
 20 
 21 namespace GroupAlgebra
 22 
 23 variable {Γ : Type*} [Group Γ]
 24 
 25 /-- The basis vector at a group element. -/
 26 def delta (g : Γ) : GroupAlgebra Γ := MonoidAlgebra.single g 1
 27 
 28 /-- The finite coefficient ℓ¹-norm; no normed-algebra instance is claimed. -/
 29 def l1Norm (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) : ℝ := ∑ g ∈ a.coeff.support, ‖a.coeff g‖
 30 
 31 /-- Conjugation of coefficients followed by inversion of the group index. -/
 32 def involution (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) : GroupAlgebra Γ :=
 33   .ofCoeff (Finsupp.equivMapDomain (Equiv.inv Γ)
 34     (a.coeff.mapRange star (star_zero ℂ)))
 35 
 36 @[simp]
 37 theorem coeff_involution (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) (g : Γ) :
 38     (involution a).coeff g = star (a.coeff g⁻¹) := rfl
 39 
 40 @[simp]
 41 theorem involution_zero : involution (0 : GroupAlgebra Γ) = 0 := by
 42   ext g
 43   simp
 44 
 45 theorem involution_add (a b : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 46     involution (a + b) = involution a + involution b := by
 47   ext g
 48   simp
 49 
 50 theorem involution_smul (z : ℂ) (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 51     involution (z • a) = star z • involution a := by
 52   ext g
 53   simp
 54 
 55 @[simp]
 56 theorem involution_involution (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 57     involution (involution a) = a := by
 58   ext g
 59   simp
 60 
 61 @[simp]
 62 theorem involution_single (g : Γ) (z : ℂ) :
 63     involution (MonoidAlgebra.single g z) = MonoidAlgebra.single g⁻¹ (star z) := by
 64   simp only [involution, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_single, Finsupp.mapRange_single,
 65     Finsupp.equivMapDomain_single, Equiv.inv_apply, MonoidAlgebra.ofCoeff_single]
 66 
 67 theorem involution_mul (a b : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 68     involution (a * b) = involution b * involution a := by
 69   induction a using MonoidAlgebra.induction_linear with
 70   | zero => simp
 71   | add a c ha hc => simp only [add_mul, involution_add, ha, hc, mul_add]
 72   | single g z =>
 73     induction b using MonoidAlgebra.induction_linear with
 74     | zero => simp
 75     | add b c hb hc => simp only [mul_add, involution_add, hb, hc, add_mul]
 76     | single h w => simp [MonoidAlgebra.single_mul_single, mul_inv_rev, mul_comm]
 77 
 78 /-- The group-algebra star is conjugation combined with inversion. -/
 79 instance instStar : Star (GroupAlgebra Γ) := ⟨involution⟩
 80 
 81 /-- Convolution and the group-algebra involution form a star ring. -/
 82 instance instStarRing : StarRing (GroupAlgebra Γ) where
 83   star_involutive := involution_involution
 84   star_mul := involution_mul
 85   star_add := involution_add
 86 
 87 /-- The involution is conjugate-linear over the complex scalars. -/
 88 instance instStarModule : StarModule ℂ (GroupAlgebra Γ) where
 89   star_smul := involution_smul
 90 
 91 @[simp]
 92 theorem coeff_star (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) (g : Γ) :
 93     (star a).coeff g = star (a.coeff g⁻¹) := coeff_involution a g
 94 
 95 /-- The multiplication agrees with the blueprint's convolution formula. -/
 96 theorem coeff_convolution (a b : GroupAlgebra Γ) (h : Γ) :
 97     (a * b).coeff h = ∑ g ∈ a.coeff.support, a.coeff g * b.coeff (g⁻¹ * h) :=
 98   MonoidAlgebra.coeff_mul_apply_left a b h
 99 
100 @[simp]
101 theorem delta_one : delta (1 : Γ) = 1 := rfl
102 
103 @[simp]
104 theorem delta_mul (g h : Γ) : delta g * delta h = delta (g * h) := by
105   simp [delta, MonoidAlgebra.single_mul_single]
106 
107 @[simp]
108 theorem delta_star (g : Γ) : star (delta g) = delta g⁻¹ := by
109   change involution (MonoidAlgebra.single g 1) = _
110   simp [delta]
111 
112 theorem delta_expansion (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
113     ∑ g ∈ a.coeff.support, a.coeff g • delta g = a := by
114   simpa [delta, MonoidAlgebra.smul_single, Finsupp.sum] using a.sum_coeff_single
115 
116 open scoped Pointwise in
117 theorem support_convolution [DecidableEq Γ] (a b : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
118     (a * b).coeff.support ⊆ a.coeff.support * b.coeff.support :=
119   MonoidAlgebra.support_coeff_mul_subset a b
120 
121 theorem support_star (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
122     (star a).coeff.support = a.coeff.support.map (Equiv.inv Γ).toEmbedding := by
123   classical
124   ext g
125   simp only [Finsupp.mem_support_iff, coeff_star, star_ne_zero, Finset.mem_map,
126     Equiv.toEmbedding_apply, Equiv.inv_apply]
127   constructor
128   · intro h
129     exact ⟨g⁻¹, h, inv_inv g⟩
130   · rintro ⟨h, hh, rfl⟩
131     simpa using hh
132 
133 theorem convolution_bilinear (a b c : GroupAlgebra Γ) (z : ℂ) :
134     (a + b) * c = a * c + b * c ∧ a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c ∧
135     (z • a) * b = z • (a * b) ∧ a * (z • b) = z • (a * b) :=
136   ⟨add_mul a b c, mul_add a b c, smul_mul_assoc z a b, mul_smul_comm z a b⟩
137 
138 /-- The laws appearing together in the blueprint's algebraic proposition. -/
139 theorem star_algebra_laws (a b c : GroupAlgebra Γ) (z w : ℂ) :
140     (a * b) * c = a * (b * c) ∧
141     (delta 1) * a = a ∧ a * (delta 1) = a ∧
142     star (a * b) = star b * star a ∧ star (star a) = a ∧
143     star (z • a + w • b) = star z • star a + star w • star b := by
144   simp only [mul_assoc, delta_one, one_mul, mul_one, star_mul, star_star,
145     star_add, star_smul, and_self]
146 
147 end GroupAlgebra
148 end BC4lean

L2Group.lean

源码 SHA-256：c29e90c17d29b0a55f4ca3e568c275b8246603e15b0f7ebb6b41b43d62d410d4

  1 import Mathlib.Analysis.InnerProductSpace.l2Space
  2 
  3 /-!
  4 # Square-summable functions and the delta Hilbert basis
  5 
  6 The index type need not be countable or carry a group structure.
  7 Mathlib supplies the complete complex inner product space structure on `lp`.
  8 Sums are unordered sums over finite subsets, not a chosen enumeration.
  9 -/
 10 
 11 noncomputable section
 12 
 13 namespace BC4lean
 14 
 15 open scoped ENNReal
 16 
 17 /-- Square-summable complex-valued functions, with their Hilbert space structure. -/
 18 abbrev L2Group (Γ : Type*) := lp (fun _ : Γ => ℂ) 2
 19 
 20 namespace L2Group
 21 
 22 variable {Γ : Type*}
 23 
 24 /-- Membership is exactly summability of the squared coefficient norms. -/
 25 theorem mem_iff (f : Γ → ℂ) :
 26     Memℓp f 2 ↔ Summable (fun g => ‖f g‖ ^ (2 : ℕ)) := by
 27   simpa using (memℓp_gen_iff (by norm_num : 0 < (2 : ℝ≥0∞).toReal) (f := f))
 28 
 29 /-- Completeness is inherited from Mathlib's `lp` construction. -/
 30 theorem completeSpace : CompleteSpace (L2Group Γ) := inferInstance
 31 
 32 theorem summable_norm_sq (ξ : L2Group Γ) : Summable (fun g => ‖ξ g‖ ^ (2 : ℕ)) :=
 33   (mem_iff ξ).mp (lp.memℓp ξ)
 34 
 35 theorem norm_sq (ξ : L2Group Γ) : ‖ξ‖ ^ (2 : ℕ) = ∑' g, ‖ξ g‖ ^ (2 : ℕ) := by
 36   simpa using lp.norm_rpow_eq_tsum (by norm_num : 0 < (2 : ℝ≥0∞).toReal) ξ
 37 
 38 theorem inner_eq_tsum (ξ η : L2Group Γ) :
 39     inner ℂ ξ η = ∑' g, star (ξ g) * η g := by
 40   simpa [RCLike.inner_apply, mul_comm] using lp.inner_eq_tsum (𝕜 := ℂ) ξ η
 41 
 42 theorem summable_inner (ξ η : L2Group Γ) :
 43     Summable (fun g => star (ξ g) * η g) := by
 44   simpa [RCLike.inner_apply, mul_comm] using lp.summable_inner (𝕜 := ℂ) ξ η
 45 
 46 /-- The delta vector at `g`. -/
 47 def delta (g : Γ) : L2Group Γ := by
 48   classical
 49   exact lp.single 2 g 1
 50 
 51 open scoped Classical in
 52 theorem delta_apply (g h : Γ) : delta g h = if h = g then 1 else 0 := by
 53   classical
 54   simp [delta, lp.single_apply, Pi.single_apply]
 55 
 56 @[simp] theorem delta_apply_self (g : Γ) : delta g g = 1 := by
 57   classical
 58   simp [delta_apply]
 59 
 60 @[simp] theorem norm_delta (g : Γ) : ‖delta g‖ = 1 := by
 61   classical
 62   simp only [delta, lp.norm_single (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ≥0∞) < 2), norm_one]
 63 
 64 /-- The identity coordinate representation gives the canonical Hilbert basis. -/
 65 def deltaBasis : HilbertBasis Γ ℂ (L2Group Γ) :=
 66   HilbertBasis.ofRepr (LinearIsometryEquiv.refl ℂ _)
 67 
 68 @[simp] theorem deltaBasis_apply (g : Γ) : deltaBasis g = delta g := rfl
 69 
 70 theorem delta_orthonormal : Orthonormal ℂ (delta : Γ → L2Group Γ) :=
 71   deltaBasis.orthonormal
 72 
 73 theorem delta_dense_span :
 74     (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (delta : Γ → L2Group Γ))).topologicalClosure = ⊤ :=
 75   deltaBasis.dense_span
 76 
 77 theorem hasSum_delta (ξ : L2Group Γ) : HasSum (fun g => ξ g • delta g) ξ :=
 78   deltaBasis.hasSum_repr ξ
 79 
 80 @[simp] theorem inner_delta (g : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) : inner ℂ (delta g) ξ = ξ g :=
 81   (deltaBasis.repr_apply_apply ξ g).symm
 82 
 83 theorem norm_apply_le (ξ : L2Group Γ) (g : Γ) : ‖ξ g‖ ≤ ‖ξ‖ :=
 84   lp.norm_apply_le_norm (by norm_num : (2 : ℝ≥0∞) ≠ 0) ξ g
 85 
 86 end L2Group
 87 end BC4lean

LeftRegularRepresentation.lean

源码 SHA-256：400a9225e55f140dcdca8e494397443be99304852e0d69a4cee8c43e9fd6d0f9

  1 import BC4lean.L2Group
  2 import Mathlib.Algebra.Star.Unitary
  3 import Mathlib.Analysis.InnerProductSpace.Adjoint
  4 
  5 /-!
  6 # First milestone: the left regular representation
  7 
  8 We construct translations directly on square-summable functions. No countability
  9 assumption is required. All groups in this module are regarded as discrete.
 10 -/
 11 
 12 noncomputable section
 13 
 14 namespace BC4lean
 15 
 16 open scoped ENNReal
 17 
 18 universe u
 19 
 20 variable {Γ : Type u} [Group Γ]
 21 
 22 /-- Left translation preserves square summability by reindexing the sum. -/
 23 theorem memLp_leftTranslate (g : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
 24     Memℓp (fun h => ξ (g⁻¹ * h)) 2 := by
 25   apply memℓp_gen
 26   exact (Equiv.mulLeft g⁻¹).summable_iff.mpr
 27     ((lp.memℓp ξ).summable (by norm_num))
 28 
 29 /-- Left translation, before packaging it as a linear isometry. -/
 30 def leftTranslate (g : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) : L2Group Γ :=
 31   ⟨fun h => ξ (g⁻¹ * h), memLp_leftTranslate g ξ⟩
 32 
 33 @[simp] theorem leftTranslate_apply (g h : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
 34     leftTranslate g ξ h = ξ (g⁻¹ * h) := rfl
 35 
 36 @[simp] theorem leftTranslate_one (ξ : L2Group Γ) : leftTranslate 1 ξ = ξ := by
 37   apply lp.ext
 38   funext h
 39   simp
 40 
 41 theorem leftTranslate_mul (g h : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
 42     leftTranslate (g * h) ξ = leftTranslate g (leftTranslate h ξ) := by
 43   apply lp.ext
 44   funext k
 45   simp [mul_assoc]
 46 
 47 @[simp] theorem leftTranslate_inv_left (g : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
 48     leftTranslate g⁻¹ (leftTranslate g ξ) = ξ := by
 49   rw [← leftTranslate_mul, inv_mul_cancel, leftTranslate_one]
 50 
 51 @[simp] theorem leftTranslate_inv_right (g : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
 52     leftTranslate g (leftTranslate g⁻¹ ξ) = ξ := by
 53   rw [← leftTranslate_mul, mul_inv_cancel, leftTranslate_one]
 54 
 55 /-- Translations preserve the norm, by invariance of a sum under a bijection. -/
 56 @[simp] theorem norm_leftTranslate (g : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
 57     ‖leftTranslate g ξ‖ = ‖ξ‖ := by
 58   rw [lp.norm_eq_tsum_rpow (by norm_num), lp.norm_eq_tsum_rpow (by norm_num)]
 59   congr 1
 60   exact (Equiv.mulLeft g⁻¹).tsum_eq (fun h => ‖ξ h‖ ^ (2 : ℝ≥0∞).toReal)
 61 
 62 /-- A unitary expressed as a complex-linear isometric equivalence. -/
 63 def leftRegular (g : Γ) : L2Group Γ ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] L2Group Γ where
 64   toFun := leftTranslate g
 65   invFun := leftTranslate g⁻¹
 66   left_inv := leftTranslate_inv_left g
 67   right_inv := leftTranslate_inv_right g
 68   map_add' ξ η := by
 69     apply lp.ext
 70     rfl
 71   map_smul' c ξ := by
 72     apply lp.ext
 73     rfl
 74   norm_map' := norm_leftTranslate g
 75 
 76 @[simp] theorem leftRegular_apply (g h : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
 77     leftRegular g ξ h = ξ (g⁻¹ * h) := rfl
 78 
 79 theorem leftRegular_inner (g : Γ) (ξ η : L2Group Γ) :
 80     inner ℂ (leftRegular g ξ) (leftRegular g η) = inner ℂ ξ η :=
 81   (leftRegular g).inner_map_map ξ η
 82 
 83 /-- The regular operator in the algebra of bounded complex-linear operators. -/
 84 def regularOperator (g : Γ) : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ :=
 85   (leftRegular g : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)
 86 
 87 @[simp] theorem regularOperator_apply (g h : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
 88     regularOperator g ξ h = ξ (g⁻¹ * h) := rfl
 89 
 90 @[simp] theorem regularOperator_one : regularOperator (1 : Γ) = 1 := by
 91   ext ξ h
 92   simp [regularOperator]
 93 
 94 @[simp] theorem regularOperator_mul (g h : Γ) :
 95     regularOperator (g * h) = regularOperator g * regularOperator h := by
 96   ext ξ k
 97   simp [regularOperator, mul_assoc]
 98 
 99 @[simp] theorem regularOperator_star (g : Γ) :
100     star (regularOperator g) = regularOperator g⁻¹ := by
101   change star (leftRegular g : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) = _
102   rw [LinearIsometryEquiv.star_eq_symm]
103   rfl
104 
105 /-- Both unitary identities hold in the bounded-operator algebra. -/
106 theorem regularOperator_unitary (g : Γ) :
107     star (regularOperator g) * regularOperator g = 1 ∧
108       regularOperator g * star (regularOperator g) = 1 := by
109   constructor <;> simp [← regularOperator_mul]
110 
111 /-- The left regular representation, packaged as a multiplicative map. -/
112 def operatorRepresentation : Γ →* (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) where
113   toFun := regularOperator
114   map_one' := regularOperator_one
115   map_mul' := regularOperator_mul
116 
117 /-- Translation carries the delta vector at `h` to the delta vector at `g * h`. -/
118 @[simp] theorem regularOperator_delta (g h : Γ) :
119     regularOperator g (L2Group.delta h) = L2Group.delta (g * h) := by
120   classical
121   ext k
122   simp only [regularOperator_apply, L2Group.delta_apply, inv_mul_eq_iff_eq_mul]
123 
124 @[simp] theorem norm_regularOperator (g : Γ) : ‖regularOperator g‖ = 1 := by
125   apply le_antisymm
126   · apply ContinuousLinearMap.opNorm_le_bound _ zero_le_one
127     intro ξ
128     change ‖leftTranslate g ξ‖ ≤ 1 * ‖ξ‖
129     simp only [norm_leftTranslate, one_mul, le_refl]
130   · have h := (regularOperator g).le_opNorm (L2Group.delta (1 : Γ))
131     simpa only [regularOperator_delta, L2Group.norm_delta, mul_one] using h
132 
133 /-- The left regular representation with values in the unitary group. -/
134 def regularRepresentation : Γ →* unitary (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) where
135   toFun g := ⟨regularOperator g, Unitary.mem_iff.mpr (regularOperator_unitary g)⟩
136   map_one' := Subtype.ext regularOperator_one
137   map_mul' g h := Subtype.ext (regularOperator_mul g h)
138 
139 @[simp] theorem regularRepresentation_coe (g : Γ) :
140     (regularRepresentation g : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) = regularOperator g := rfl
141 
142 @[simp] theorem regularRepresentation_apply (g h : Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) :
143     (regularRepresentation g : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) ξ h = ξ (g⁻¹ * h) := rfl
144 
145 end BC4lean

IntegratedRepresentation.lean

源码 SHA-256：6127ba4543ef7f420056752bcf1731c553a597fe59bc402b8b9af38146f48df0

  1 import BC4lean.GroupAlgebra
  2 import BC4lean.LeftRegularRepresentation
  3 import Mathlib.Analysis.CStarAlgebra.ContinuousLinearMap
  4 
  5 /-! # The integrated regular representation and reduced norm
  6 
  7 All groups are regarded as discrete. No countability assumption is used.
  8 The algebra homomorphism is Mathlib's linear extension of the regular operators;
  9 compatibility with the group-algebra involution is proved below.
 10 -/
 11 
 12 noncomputable section
 13 
 14 namespace BC4lean
 15 
 16 variable {Γ : Type*} [Group Γ]
 17 
 18 /-- The linear extension of the regular operators to the complex group algebra. -/
 19 def integratedRepresentation : GroupAlgebra Γ →ₐ[ℂ] (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) :=
 20   MonoidAlgebra.lift ℂ _ Γ operatorRepresentation
 21 
 22 theorem integratedRepresentation_sum (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 23     integratedRepresentation a = ∑ g ∈ a.coeff.support, a.coeff g • regularOperator g :=
 24   MonoidAlgebra.lift_apply operatorRepresentation a
 25 
 26 @[simp] theorem integratedRepresentation_single (g : Γ) (z : ℂ) :
 27     integratedRepresentation (MonoidAlgebra.single g z) = z • regularOperator g :=
 28   MonoidAlgebra.lift_single operatorRepresentation g z
 29 
 30 @[simp] theorem integratedRepresentation_delta (g : Γ) :
 31     integratedRepresentation (GroupAlgebra.delta g) = regularOperator g := by
 32   simp [GroupAlgebra.delta]
 33 
 34 theorem integratedRepresentation_apply (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) (ξ : L2Group Γ) (h : Γ) :
 35     integratedRepresentation a ξ h =
 36       ∑ g ∈ a.coeff.support, a.coeff g * ξ (g⁻¹ * h) := by
 37   rw [integratedRepresentation_sum]
 38   simp only [sum_apply, smul_apply, lp.coeFn_sum,
 39     lp.coeFn_smul, Finset.sum_apply, Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, regularOperator_apply]
 40 
 41 theorem norm_integratedRepresentation_le (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 42     ‖integratedRepresentation a‖ ≤ GroupAlgebra.l1Norm a := by
 43   rw [integratedRepresentation_sum]
 44   calc
 45     _ ≤ ∑ g ∈ a.coeff.support, ‖a.coeff g • regularOperator g‖ := norm_sum_le _ _
 46     _ = GroupAlgebra.l1Norm a := by simp [GroupAlgebra.l1Norm, norm_smul]
 47 
 48 @[simp] theorem integratedRepresentation_star (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 49     integratedRepresentation (star a) = star (integratedRepresentation a) := by
 50   induction a using MonoidAlgebra.induction_linear with
 51   | zero => simp
 52   | add a b ha hb => simp only [star_add, map_add, ha, hb]
 53   | single g z =>
 54     change integratedRepresentation (GroupAlgebra.involution (MonoidAlgebra.single g z)) = _
 55     simp only [GroupAlgebra.involution_single, integratedRepresentation_single,
 56       star_smul, regularOperator_star]
 57 
 58 /-- The integrated representation as a unital complex star-algebra homomorphism. -/
 59 def integratedStarHom : GroupAlgebra Γ →⋆ₐ[ℂ] (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) where
 60   toAlgHom := integratedRepresentation
 61   map_star' := integratedRepresentation_star
 62 
 63 @[simp] theorem integratedStarHom_apply (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 64     integratedStarHom a = integratedRepresentation a := rfl
 65 
 66 /-- Coefficients are recovered by applying the operator to the identity delta vector. -/
 67 @[simp] theorem integratedRepresentation_delta_one (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) (h : Γ) :
 68     integratedRepresentation a (L2Group.delta 1) h = a.coeff h := by
 69   classical
 70   induction a using MonoidAlgebra.induction_linear with
 71   | zero => simp
 72   | add a b ha hb => simpa using congrArg₂ (· + ·) ha hb
 73   | single g z =>
 74     simp [L2Group.delta_apply, Finsupp.single_apply, eq_comm]
 75 
 76 theorem integratedRepresentation_injective :
 77     Function.Injective (integratedRepresentation (Γ := Γ)) := by
 78   intro a b hab
 79   apply MonoidAlgebra.ext
 80   apply Finsupp.ext
 81   intro h
 82   simpa using congrArg (fun T : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ => T (L2Group.delta 1) h) hab
 83 
 84 /-- The coefficient function as an ℓ²-vector; the next theorem identifies it pointwise. -/
 85 def GroupAlgebra.toL2 (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) : L2Group Γ :=
 86   integratedRepresentation a (L2Group.delta 1)
 87 
 88 @[simp] theorem GroupAlgebra.toL2_apply (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) (h : Γ) :
 89     a.toL2 h = a.coeff h := integratedRepresentation_delta_one a h
 90 
 91 theorem norm_toL2_le (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) : ‖a.toL2‖ ≤ ‖integratedRepresentation a‖ := by
 92   simpa only [GroupAlgebra.toL2, L2Group.norm_delta, mul_one] using
 93     (integratedRepresentation a).le_opNorm (L2Group.delta (1 : Γ))
 94 
 95 theorem norm_coeff_le (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) (h : Γ) :
 96     ‖a.coeff h‖ ≤ ‖integratedRepresentation a‖ := by
 97   simpa only [GroupAlgebra.toL2_apply] using
 98     (L2Group.norm_apply_le a.toL2 h).trans (norm_toL2_le a)
 99 
100 /-- The algebraic image is exactly the finite complex span of the regular operators. -/
101 theorem integratedRepresentation_range :
102     (integratedRepresentation (Γ := Γ)).range.toSubmodule =
103       Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (regularOperator (Γ := Γ))) := by
104   apply le_antisymm
105   · rintro T ⟨a, rfl⟩
106     change integratedRepresentation a ∈ Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range regularOperator)
107     induction a using MonoidAlgebra.induction_linear with
108     | zero => simp
109     | add a b ha hb =>
110       rw [map_add]
111       exact Submodule.add_mem _ ha hb
112     | single g z =>
113       rw [integratedRepresentation_single]
114       exact Submodule.smul_mem _ z (Submodule.subset_span ⟨g, rfl⟩)
115   · apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
116     rintro T ⟨g, rfl⟩
117     exact ⟨GroupAlgebra.delta g, integratedRepresentation_delta g⟩
118 
119 /-- The reduced norm is the norm of the faithful regular operator. -/
120 def reducedNorm (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) : ℝ := ‖integratedRepresentation a‖
121 
122 theorem reducedNorm_nonneg (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) : 0 ≤ reducedNorm a := norm_nonneg _
123 
124 @[simp] theorem reducedNorm_eq_zero (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) : reducedNorm a = 0 ↔ a = 0 := by
125   simp only [reducedNorm, norm_eq_zero, map_eq_zero_iff _ integratedRepresentation_injective]
126 
127 theorem reducedNorm_add_le (a b : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
128     reducedNorm (a + b) ≤ reducedNorm a + reducedNorm b := by
129   simpa only [reducedNorm, map_add] using norm_add_le (integratedRepresentation a) _
130 
131 theorem reducedNorm_smul (z : ℂ) (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
132     reducedNorm (z • a) = ‖z‖ * reducedNorm a := by
133   simp only [reducedNorm, map_smul, norm_smul]
134 
135 theorem reducedNorm_mul_le (a b : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
136     reducedNorm (a * b) ≤ reducedNorm a * reducedNorm b := by
137   simpa only [reducedNorm, map_mul] using norm_mul_le (integratedRepresentation a) _
138 
139 @[simp] theorem reducedNorm_star (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) : reducedNorm (star a) = reducedNorm a := by
140   unfold reducedNorm
141   rw [integratedRepresentation_star]
142   exact norm_star (integratedRepresentation a)
143 
144 theorem reducedNorm_cstar (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
145     reducedNorm (star a * a) = reducedNorm a ^ 2 := by
146   unfold reducedNorm
147   rw [map_mul, integratedRepresentation_star]
148   simpa only [pow_two] using CStarRing.norm_star_mul_self (x := integratedRepresentation a)
149 
150 @[simp] theorem reducedNorm_delta (g : Γ) : reducedNorm (GroupAlgebra.delta g) = 1 := by
151   simp only [reducedNorm, integratedRepresentation_delta, norm_regularOperator]
152 
153 theorem reducedNorm_bounds (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
154     ‖a.toL2‖ ≤ reducedNorm a ∧ reducedNorm a ≤ GroupAlgebra.l1Norm a :=
155   ⟨norm_toL2_le a, norm_integratedRepresentation_le a⟩
156 
157 end BC4lean

ReducedGroupCStar.lean

源码 SHA-256：96917b6794dc08f3ea214d34285484632b8f7c4a1247dfd480949707cd2da173

  1 import BC4lean.IntegratedRepresentation
  2 import Mathlib.Analysis.CStarAlgebra.ContinuousLinearMap
  3 
  4 /-! # The concrete reduced group C⋆-algebra
  5 
  6 The underlying set is the operator-norm closure of the integrated regular image.
  7 The C⋆-algebra structure is inherited from bounded operators on ℓ²(Γ).
  8 -/
  9 
 10 noncomputable section
 11 
 12 namespace BC4lean
 13 
 14 variable (Γ : Type*) [Group Γ]
 15 
 16 /-- The closed star subalgebra generated by the integrated regular image. -/
 17 def reducedAlgebra : StarSubalgebra ℂ (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) :=
 18   (integratedStarHom (Γ := Γ)).range.topologicalClosure
 19 
 20 /-- The concrete reduced group C⋆-algebra. -/
 21 abbrev ReducedGroupCStar := ↥(reducedAlgebra Γ)
 22 
 23 variable {Γ}
 24 
 25 theorem reducedAlgebra_coe_eq_closure_range :
 26     (reducedAlgebra Γ : Set (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)) =
 27       closure (Set.range (integratedRepresentation (Γ := Γ))) := rfl
 28 
 29 theorem reducedAlgebra_isClosed :
 30     IsClosed (reducedAlgebra Γ : Set (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)) :=
 31   isClosed_closure
 32 
 33 instance reducedGroupCStarAlgebra : CStarAlgebra (ReducedGroupCStar Γ) :=
 34   StarSubalgebra.cstarAlgebra _ (h_closed := reducedAlgebra_isClosed)
 35 
 36 /-- The identity regular operator has norm one, also for the trivial group. -/
 37 instance regularOperatorsNormOne : NormOneClass (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) where
 38   norm_one := by
 39     simpa only [regularOperator_one] using norm_regularOperator (1 : Γ)
 40 
 41 instance reducedGroupCStarNormOne : NormOneClass (ReducedGroupCStar Γ) where
 42   norm_one := by
 43     change ‖(1 : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)‖ = 1
 44     exact norm_one
 45 
 46 /-- The canonical faithful star homomorphism with dense image. -/
 47 def integratedToReduced : GroupAlgebra Γ →⋆ₐ[ℂ] ReducedGroupCStar Γ :=
 48   integratedStarHom.codRestrict (reducedAlgebra Γ)
 49     (fun a => subset_closure ⟨a, rfl⟩)
 50 
 51 @[simp] theorem integratedToReduced_coe (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 52     (integratedToReduced a : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) =
 53       integratedRepresentation a := rfl
 54 
 55 theorem integratedToReduced_injective :
 56     Function.Injective (integratedToReduced (Γ := Γ)) := by
 57   intro a b h
 58   exact integratedRepresentation_injective (congrArg Subtype.val h)
 59 
 60 @[simp] theorem norm_integratedToReduced (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 61     ‖integratedToReduced a‖ = reducedNorm a := rfl
 62 
 63 theorem denseRange_integratedToReduced :
 64     DenseRange (integratedToReduced (Γ := Γ)) := by
 65   intro T
 66   rw [closure_subtype, ← Set.range_comp]
 67   change (T : L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ) ∈
 68     closure (Set.range (integratedRepresentation (Γ := Γ)))
 69   rw [← reducedAlgebra_coe_eq_closure_range]
 70   exact T.property
 71 
 72 theorem regularOperator_mem_reducedAlgebra (g : Γ) :
 73     regularOperator g ∈ reducedAlgebra Γ :=
 74   subset_closure ⟨GroupAlgebra.delta g, integratedRepresentation_delta g⟩
 75 
 76 theorem reducedAlgebra_coe_eq_closure_span :
 77     (reducedAlgebra Γ : Set (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)) =
 78       closure (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (regularOperator (Γ := Γ))) :
 79         Set (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)) := by
 80   rw [← integratedRepresentation_range]
 81   rfl
 82 
 83 /-- Minimality among closed complex star subalgebras containing all regular operators. -/
 84 theorem reducedAlgebra_minimal (S : StarSubalgebra ℂ (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ))
 85     (hS : IsClosed (S : Set (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)))
 86     (h : ∀ g : Γ, regularOperator g ∈ S) : reducedAlgebra Γ ≤ S := by
 87   apply StarSubalgebra.topologicalClosure_minimal _ hS
 88   rintro T ⟨a, rfl⟩
 89   change integratedRepresentation a ∈ S
 90   induction a using MonoidAlgebra.induction_linear with
 91   | zero => simp
 92   | add a b ha hb =>
 93     rw [map_add]
 94     exact S.add_mem ha hb
 95   | single g z => simpa only [integratedRepresentation_single] using S.smul_mem (h g) z
 96 
 97 /-- Countability is needed here, but not for the C⋆-algebra construction. -/
 98 instance reducedGroupCStarSeparable [Countable Γ] : TopologicalSpace.SeparableSpace (ReducedGroupCStar Γ) := by
 99   apply TopologicalSpace.IsSeparable.separableSpace
100   rw [reducedAlgebra_coe_eq_closure_span]
101   exact ((Set.countable_range (regularOperator (Γ := Γ))).isSeparable.span (R := ℂ)).closure
102 
103 end BC4lean

 11 theorem reducedAlgebra_le_scalars :
 12     reducedAlgebra Γ ≤ (StarAlgHom.ofId ℂ (L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)).range := by

 14   · exact (algebraMap_isometry (𝕜 := ℂ) (𝕜' := L2Group Γ →L[ℂ] L2Group Γ)).isClosedEmbedding.isClosed_range

 25   exact hz

ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.lean

  1 import BC4lean.ReducedGroupCStar

  3 /-! # The group algebra with its reduced norm
  4 
  5 A dedicated type synonym keeps this norm separate from possible ℓ¹ norms.
  6 The algebraic operations are precisely those of `GroupAlgebra Γ`.
  7 -/
  8 
  9 noncomputable section
 10 namespace BC4lean
 11 
 12 variable (Γ : Type*) [Group Γ]
 13 
 14 /-- The complex group algebra, reserved for the reduced-norm topology. -/
 15 def ReducedNormGroupAlgebra := GroupAlgebra Γ
 16 
 17 namespace ReducedNormGroupAlgebra
 18 
 19 instance instRing : Ring (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :=
 20   inferInstanceAs (Ring (GroupAlgebra Γ))
 21 instance instAlgebra : Algebra ℂ (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :=
 22   inferInstanceAs (Algebra ℂ (GroupAlgebra Γ))
 23 instance instStarRing : StarRing (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :=
 24   inferInstanceAs (StarRing (GroupAlgebra Γ))
 25 instance instStarModule : StarModule ℂ (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :=
 26   inferInstanceAs (StarModule ℂ (GroupAlgebra Γ))
 27 
 28 /-- Identity on coefficients; only the choice of topological structure changes. -/
 29 def ofGroupAlgebra : GroupAlgebra Γ ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ :=
 30   StarAlgEquiv.refl ℂ (GroupAlgebra Γ)
 31 
 32 def toReduced : ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ →⋆ₐ[ℂ] ReducedGroupCStar Γ :=
 33   integratedToReduced
 34 
 35 theorem toReduced_injective : Function.Injective (toReduced Γ) :=
 36   integratedToReduced_injective
 37 
 38 instance instNormedRing : NormedRing (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :=
 39   NormedRing.induced _ _ (toReduced Γ) (toReduced_injective Γ)
 40 instance instNormedAlgebra : NormedAlgebra ℂ (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :=
 41   NormedAlgebra.induced ℂ _ _ (toReduced Γ)
 42 
 43 @[simp] theorem norm_ofGroupAlgebra (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
 44     ‖ofGroupAlgebra Γ a‖ = reducedNorm a := rfl
 45 
 46 @[simp] theorem norm_toReduced (a : ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :
 47     ‖toReduced Γ a‖ = ‖a‖ := rfl
 48 
 49 instance instNormedStarGroup : NormedStarGroup (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) where
 50   norm_star_le a := by
 51     change ‖toReduced Γ (star a)‖ ≤ ‖toReduced Γ a‖
 52     rw [map_star]
 53     exact (norm_star (toReduced Γ a)).le
 54 
 55 instance instCStarRing : CStarRing (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) where
 56   norm_mul_self_le a := by
 57     change ‖toReduced Γ a‖ * ‖toReduced Γ a‖ ≤ ‖toReduced Γ (star a * a)‖
 58     rw [map_mul, map_star]
 59     exact CStarRing.norm_star_mul_self.symm.le

 62   AddMonoidHomClass.isometry_of_norm (toReduced Γ) (norm_toReduced Γ)

ReducedCompletion.lean

源码 SHA-256：27bc0c056893f22d9ed1a17462215a71356768e9d5435025630aa3cb025b2754

  1 import BC4lean.ReducedNormGroupAlgebra
  2 import Mathlib.Analysis.Normed.Module.Completion
  3 
  4 /-! # The abstract reduced-norm completion and its concrete realization
  5 
  6 The source is Mathlib's uniform completion of the reduced-norm group algebra.
  7 Its ring, complex module and algebra operations are the standard completion
  8 operations. Its star is `Completion.map star`, not a transported operation.
  9 -/
 10 
 11 noncomputable section
 12 namespace BC4lean
 13 open UniformSpace
 14 
 15 variable (Γ : Type*) [Group Γ]
 16 
 17 /-- Abstract norm completion of the complex group algebra with reduced norm. -/
 18 abbrev ReducedCompletion := Completion (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ)
 19 
 20 /-- Continuous extension of the canonical ring homomorphism. -/
 21 def completionMap : ReducedCompletion Γ →+* ReducedGroupCStar Γ :=
 22   Completion.extensionHom (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.toReduced Γ).toAlgHom.toRingHom
 23     (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.toReduced_isometry Γ).continuous
 24 
 25 @[simp] theorem completionMap_coe (a : ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :
 26     completionMap Γ (a : ReducedCompletion Γ) = ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.toReduced Γ a :=
 27   Completion.extensionHom_coe _ _ a
 28 
 29 theorem completionMap_isometry : Isometry (completionMap Γ) :=
 30   (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.toReduced_isometry Γ).completion_extension
 31 
 32 theorem completionMap_injective : Function.Injective (completionMap Γ) :=
 33   (completionMap_isometry Γ).injective
 34 
 35 @[simp] theorem norm_completionMap (x : ReducedCompletion Γ) :
 36     ‖completionMap Γ x‖ = ‖x‖ := by
 37   simpa only [map_zero, dist_zero_right] using (completionMap_isometry Γ).dist_eq x 0
 38 
 39 theorem completionMap_surjective : Function.Surjective (completionMap Γ) := by
 40   intro T
 41   have hsubset : Set.range (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.toReduced Γ) ⊆
 42       Set.range (completionMap Γ) := by
 43     rintro _ ⟨a, rfl⟩
 44     exact ⟨(a : ReducedCompletion Γ), completionMap_coe Γ a⟩
 45   have h := closure_mono hsubset (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.toReduced_denseRange Γ T)
 46   rwa [(completionMap_isometry Γ).isClosedEmbedding.isClosed_range.closure_eq] at h
 47 
 48 /-- Complex-linearity of the canonical extension. -/
 49 def completionAlgHom : ReducedCompletion Γ →ₐ[ℂ] ReducedGroupCStar Γ where
 50   toRingHom := completionMap Γ
 51   commutes' z := by
 52     change completionMap Γ ((algebraMap ℂ (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) z :
 53       ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) : ReducedCompletion Γ) = _
 54     rw [completionMap_coe]
 55     exact (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.toReduced Γ).commutes z
 56 
 57 instance completionStar : Star (ReducedCompletion Γ) :=
 58   ⟨Completion.map (star : ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ → ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ)⟩
 59 
 60 @[simp] theorem completion_star_coe (a : ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :
 61     star (a : ReducedCompletion Γ) = (star a : ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :=
 62   Completion.map_coe star_isometry.uniformContinuous a
 63 
 64 instance completionContinuousStar : ContinuousStar (ReducedCompletion Γ) :=
 65   ⟨Completion.continuous_map⟩
 66 
 67 theorem completionMap_star (x : ReducedCompletion Γ) :
 68     completionMap Γ (star x) = star (completionMap Γ x) := by
 69   induction x using Completion.induction_on with
 70   | hp =>
 71     exact isClosed_eq ((completionMap_isometry Γ).continuous.comp continuous_star)
 72       (continuous_star.comp (completionMap_isometry Γ).continuous)
 73   | ih a => simp only [completion_star_coe, completionMap_coe, map_star]
 74 
 75 instance completionStarRing : StarRing (ReducedCompletion Γ) :=
 76   (completionMap_injective Γ).starRing (completionMap Γ)
 77     (completionMap_star Γ) (map_add _) (map_mul _)
 78 
 79 instance completionStarModule : StarModule ℂ (ReducedCompletion Γ) :=
 80   Function.Injective.starModule (completionMap Γ) ℂ (completionMap_injective Γ)
 81     (completionMap_star Γ) (map_smul (completionAlgHom Γ))
 82 
 83 instance completionNormedAlgebra : NormedAlgebra ℂ (ReducedCompletion Γ) where
 84   norm_smul_le := norm_smul_le
 85 
 86 instance completionCStarRing : CStarRing (ReducedCompletion Γ) where
 87   norm_mul_self_le x := by
 88     rw [← norm_completionMap Γ x, ← norm_completionMap Γ (star x * x),
 89       map_mul, completionMap_star]
 90     exact CStarRing.norm_star_mul_self.symm.le
 91 
 92 instance completionCStarAlgebra : CStarAlgebra (ReducedCompletion Γ) where
 93 
 94 /-- The extended map as a unital complex star-algebra homomorphism. -/
 95 def completionStarHom : ReducedCompletion Γ →⋆ₐ[ℂ] ReducedGroupCStar Γ where
 96   toAlgHom := completionAlgHom Γ
 97   map_star' := completionMap_star Γ
 98 
 99 /-- The abstract norm completion is canonically the concrete reduced group C⋆-algebra. -/
100 def reducedCompletionEquiv : ReducedCompletion Γ ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] ReducedGroupCStar Γ :=
101   StarAlgEquiv.ofBijective (completionStarHom Γ)
102     ⟨completionMap_injective Γ, completionMap_surjective Γ⟩
103 
104 theorem reducedCompletionEquiv_isometry : Isometry (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ) :=
105   completionMap_isometry Γ
106 
107 @[simp] theorem reducedCompletionEquiv_coe (a : ReducedNormGroupAlgebra Γ) :
108     reducedCompletionEquiv Γ (a : ReducedCompletion Γ) =
109       ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.toReduced Γ a := completionMap_coe Γ a
110 
111 /-- The original algebra embeds in the abstract completion. -/
112 def groupAlgebraToCompletion : GroupAlgebra Γ →⋆ₐ[ℂ] ReducedCompletion Γ where
113   toAlgHom :=
114     { toRingHom := Completion.coeRingHom.comp
115         (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.ofGroupAlgebra Γ).toAlgEquiv.toAlgHom.toRingHom
116       commutes' _ := rfl }
117   map_star' a := (completion_star_coe Γ (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.ofGroupAlgebra Γ a)).symm
118 
119 @[simp] theorem norm_groupAlgebraToCompletion (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
120     ‖groupAlgebraToCompletion Γ a‖ = reducedNorm a :=
121   Completion.norm_coe (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.ofGroupAlgebra Γ a)
122 
123 @[simp] theorem reducedCompletionEquiv_extends (a : GroupAlgebra Γ) :
124     reducedCompletionEquiv Γ (groupAlgebraToCompletion Γ a) = integratedToReduced a :=
125   completionMap_coe Γ (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.ofGroupAlgebra Γ a)
126 
127 /-- Uniqueness already holds among all continuous functions with the prescribed restriction. -/
128 theorem reducedCompletionEquiv_unique (f : ReducedCompletion Γ → ReducedGroupCStar Γ)
129     (hf : Continuous f)
130     (h : ∀ a : GroupAlgebra Γ, f (groupAlgebraToCompletion Γ a) = integratedToReduced a) :
131     f = reducedCompletionEquiv Γ := by
132   apply Completion.ext hf (reducedCompletionEquiv_isometry Γ).continuous
133   intro a
134   obtain ⟨b, rfl⟩ := (ReducedNormGroupAlgebra.ofGroupAlgebra Γ).surjective a
135   exact (h b).trans (reducedCompletionEquiv_extends Γ b).symm
136 
137 /-- In particular the extension is the unique isometric star-algebra isomorphism. -/
138 theorem reducedCompletionEquiv_unique_isometric
139     (e : ReducedCompletion Γ ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] ReducedGroupCStar Γ) (he : Isometry e)
140     (h : ∀ a : GroupAlgebra Γ, e (groupAlgebraToCompletion Γ a) = integratedToReduced a) :
141     e = reducedCompletionEquiv Γ := by
142   apply DFunLike.coe_injective
143   exact reducedCompletionEquiv_unique Γ e he.continuous h
144 
145 end BC4lean

OperatorProjections.lean

源码 SHA-256：ebd907953cb61d6852bdec4eab229baa97b554af4c4010f998f3febb913ce0c3

  1 import Mathlib.Analysis.CStarAlgebra.Classes
  2 import Mathlib.Algebra.Star.StarProjection
  3 
  4 /-! # Projections and Murray–von Neumann equivalence
  5 
  6 Projection-level foundations for operator K₀. The two support equations for
  7 partial isometries are consequences of the C⋆-identity, not extra assumptions.
  8 -/
  9 
 10 noncomputable section
 11 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 12 
 13 variable {A B C : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B] [CStarAlgebra C]
 14 
 15 /-- A projection in a unital complex C⋆-algebra. -/
 16 abbrev Projection (A : Type*) [CStarAlgebra A] := {p : A // IsStarProjection p}
 17 
 18 namespace Projection
 19 
 20 @[simp] theorem star_coe (p : Projection A) : star (p : A) = p :=
 21   p.property.isSelfAdjoint.star_eq
 22 
 23 @[simp] theorem mul_self (p : Projection A) : (p : A) * p = p :=
 24   p.property.isIdempotentElem.eq
 25 
 26 def zero : Projection A := ⟨0, IsStarProjection.zero A⟩
 27 def one : Projection A := ⟨1, IsStarProjection.one A⟩
 28 def complement (p : Projection A) : Projection A := ⟨1 - p, p.property.one_sub⟩
 29 
 30 def map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : Projection A) : Projection B :=
 31   ⟨φ p, p.property.map φ⟩
 32 
 33 @[simp] theorem map_coe (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : Projection A) :
 34     (map φ p : B) = φ p := rfl
 35 
 36 @[simp] theorem map_id (p : Projection A) : map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) p = p := rfl
 37 
 38 @[simp] theorem map_comp (ψ : B →⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : Projection A) :
 39     map (ψ.comp φ) p = map ψ (map φ p) := rfl
 40 
 41 @[simp] theorem map_zero (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : map φ zero = zero :=
 42   Subtype.ext (_root_.map_zero φ)
 43 
 44 @[simp] theorem map_one (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : map φ one = one :=
 45   Subtype.ext (_root_.map_one φ)
 46 
 47 /-- The initial projection acts as the right support of a partial isometry. -/
 48 theorem right_support (p : Projection A) (v : A) (hv : star v * v = p) : v * p = v := by
 49   apply sub_eq_zero.mp
 50   apply (CStarRing.star_mul_self_eq_zero_iff (v * p - v)).mp
 51   have h₁ : star v * (v * p) = p := by rw [← mul_assoc, hv, mul_self]
 52   simp only [star_sub, star_mul, star_coe, sub_mul, mul_sub, mul_assoc, h₁, hv,
 53     mul_self, sub_self]
 54 
 55 /-- Equivalence implemented by the usual two partial-isometry equations. -/
 56 def Equivalent (p q : Projection A) : Prop :=
 57   ∃ v : A, star v * v = p ∧ v * star v = q
 58 
 59 theorem equivalent_refl (p : Projection A) : Equivalent p p :=
 60   ⟨p, by simp, by simp⟩
 61 
 62 theorem equivalent_symm {p q : Projection A} (h : Equivalent p q) : Equivalent q p := by
 63   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := h
 64   exact ⟨star v, by simpa using hv', by simpa using hv⟩
 65 
 66 theorem equivalent_trans {p q r : Projection A}
 67     (hpq : Equivalent p q) (hqr : Equivalent q r) : Equivalent p r := by
 68   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := hpq
 69   obtain ⟨w, hw, hw'⟩ := hqr
 70   have hvp := right_support p v hv
 71   have hqv : (q : A) * v = v := by rw [← hv', mul_assoc, hv, hvp]
 72   have hwq := right_support q w hw
 73   refine ⟨w * v, ?_, ?_⟩
 74   · calc
 75       star (w * v) * (w * v) = star v * (star w * w) * v := by simp only [star_mul, mul_assoc]
 76       _ = star v * q * v := by rw [hw]
 77       _ = p := by rw [mul_assoc, hqv, hv]
 78   · calc
 79       (w * v) * star (w * v) = w * (v * star v) * star w := by simp only [star_mul, mul_assoc]
 80       _ = w * q * star w := by rw [hv']
 81       _ = r := by rw [hwq, hw']
 82 
 83 /-- Equivalence classes at a fixed algebra, in particular a fixed matrix size. -/
 84 def equivalenceSetoid : Setoid (Projection A) where
 85   r := Equivalent
 86   iseqv := ⟨equivalent_refl, equivalent_symm, equivalent_trans⟩
 87 
 88 abbrev Classes (A : Type*) [CStarAlgebra A] := Quotient (equivalenceSetoid (A := A))
 89 
 90 theorem equivalent_map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) {p q : Projection A}
 91     (h : Equivalent p q) : Equivalent (map φ p) (map φ q) := by
 92   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := h
 93   refine ⟨φ v, ?_, ?_⟩
 94   · simpa only [map_mul, map_star, map_coe] using congrArg φ hv
 95   · simpa only [map_mul, map_star, map_coe] using congrArg φ hv'
 96 
 97 def mapClasses (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : Classes A → Classes B :=
 98   Quotient.map (map φ) (fun _ _ h => equivalent_map φ h)
 99 
100 @[simp] theorem mapClasses_mk (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : Projection A) :
101     mapClasses φ (Quotient.mk _ p) = Quotient.mk _ (map φ p) := rfl
102 
103 end Projection
104 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

MatrixStabilization.lean

源码 SHA-256：ed088f2a4c275e44cb052d1304c855e6e966b5ecdbad4881ddbdd38bfd4c59e1

  1 import BC4lean.OperatorProjections
  2 import Mathlib.Analysis.CStarAlgebra.CStarMatrix
  3 import Mathlib.Data.Matrix.Block
  4 import Mathlib.Algebra.Star.Unitary
  5 
  6 /-! # Matrix projections, unitaries, and stabilization
  7 
  8 We use Mathlib's C⋆-matrix norm, not the entrywise supremum norm. The order
  9 assumptions are the standard compatible C⋆-order required by that API.
 10 Block sums first use sum-indexed matrices, avoiding arbitrary index choices.
 11 -/
 12 
 13 noncomputable section
 14 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 15 
 16 open scoped Matrix
 17 
 18 variable {A : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A]
 19 variable {ι κ : Type*}
 20 
 21 /-- Block diagonal sum of C⋆-matrices. -/
 22 def matrixBlockSum (p : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (q : CStarMatrix κ κ A) :
 23     CStarMatrix (ι ⊕ κ) (ι ⊕ κ) A :=
 24   CStarMatrix.ofMatrix (Matrix.fromBlocks (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm p) 0 0
 25     (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm q))
 26 
 27 @[simp] theorem matrixBlockSum_star (p : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (q : CStarMatrix κ κ A) :
 28     star (matrixBlockSum p q) = matrixBlockSum (star p) (star q) := by
 29   change (Matrix.fromBlocks (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm p) (0 : Matrix ι κ A)
 30     (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm q))ᴴ = _
 31   simp only [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.conjTranspose_zero]
 32   rfl
 33 
 34 theorem matrixBlockSum_mul [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ] (p r : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (q s : CStarMatrix κ κ A) :
 35     matrixBlockSum p q * matrixBlockSum r s = matrixBlockSum (p * r) (q * s) := by
 36   change Matrix.fromBlocks (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm p) (0 : Matrix ι κ A)
 37     (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm q) *
 38     Matrix.fromBlocks (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm r) (0 : Matrix ι κ A)
 39     (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm s) =
 40     Matrix.fromBlocks (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm p * CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm r)
 41       (0 : Matrix ι κ A) (0 : Matrix κ ι A)
 42       (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm q * CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm s)
 43   simp only [Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply, Matrix.mul_zero, Matrix.zero_mul, add_zero, zero_add]
 44 
 45 @[simp] theorem matrixBlockSum_one [DecidableEq ι] [DecidableEq κ] :
 46     matrixBlockSum (1 : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (1 : CStarMatrix κ κ A) = 1 :=
 47   Matrix.fromBlocks_one
 48 
 49 @[simp] theorem matrixBlockSum_zero :
 50     matrixBlockSum (0 : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (0 : CStarMatrix κ κ A) = 0 :=
 51   Matrix.fromBlocks_zero
 52 
 53 variable [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 54 variable [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ] [DecidableEq ι] [DecidableEq κ]
 55 
 56 /-- A matrix projection with the C⋆-matrix norm. -/
 57 abbrev MatrixProjection (A : Type*) [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 58     (ι : Type*) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] := Projection (CStarMatrix ι ι A)
 59 
 60 def projectionBlockSum (p : MatrixProjection A ι) (q : MatrixProjection A κ) :
 61     MatrixProjection A (ι ⊕ κ) :=
 62   ⟨matrixBlockSum p q, ⟨by
 63     change matrixBlockSum p.val q.val * matrixBlockSum p.val q.val = matrixBlockSum p.val q.val
 64     rw [matrixBlockSum_mul]; simp, by
 65     change star (matrixBlockSum p.val q.val) = matrixBlockSum p.val q.val
 66     rw [matrixBlockSum_star]; simp⟩⟩
 67 
 68 @[simp] theorem projectionBlockSum_coe (p : MatrixProjection A ι) (q : MatrixProjection A κ) :
 69     (projectionBlockSum p q : CStarMatrix (ι ⊕ κ) (ι ⊕ κ) A) = matrixBlockSum p q := rfl
 70 
 71 /-- Projection stabilization adds a zero block. -/
 72 def stabilizeProjection (p : MatrixProjection A ι) : MatrixProjection A (ι ⊕ κ) :=
 73   projectionBlockSum p Projection.zero
 74 
 75 theorem equivalent_blockSum {p r : MatrixProjection A ι} {q s : MatrixProjection A κ}
 76     (h : Projection.Equivalent p r) (k : Projection.Equivalent q s) :
 77     Projection.Equivalent (projectionBlockSum p q) (projectionBlockSum r s) := by
 78   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := h
 79   obtain ⟨w, hw, hw'⟩ := k
 80   refine ⟨matrixBlockSum v w, ?_, ?_⟩
 81   · simp only [matrixBlockSum_star, matrixBlockSum_mul, hv, hw, projectionBlockSum_coe]
 82   · simp only [matrixBlockSum_star, matrixBlockSum_mul, hv', hw', projectionBlockSum_coe]
 83 
 84 theorem equivalent_stabilize {p q : MatrixProjection A ι} (h : Projection.Equivalent p q) :
 85     Projection.Equivalent (stabilizeProjection (κ := κ) p) (stabilizeProjection q) :=
 86   equivalent_blockSum h (Projection.equivalent_refl Projection.zero)
 87 
 88 /-- Block sum descends to Murray–von Neumann classes at the indicated matrix sizes. -/
 89 def blockSumClasses : Projection.Classes (CStarMatrix ι ι A) →
 90     Projection.Classes (CStarMatrix κ κ A) →
 91     Projection.Classes (CStarMatrix (ι ⊕ κ) (ι ⊕ κ) A) :=
 92   Quotient.map₂ projectionBlockSum (fun _ _ h _ _ k => equivalent_blockSum h k)
 93 
 94 /-- A matrix unitary with the operator-norm topology. -/
 95 abbrev MatrixUnitary (A : Type*) [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 96     (ι : Type*) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] := unitary (CStarMatrix ι ι A)
 97 
 98 def unitaryBlockSum (u : MatrixUnitary A ι) (v : MatrixUnitary A κ) :
 99     MatrixUnitary A (ι ⊕ κ) :=
100   ⟨matrixBlockSum u v, by
101     rw [Unitary.mem_iff]
102     constructor
103     · rw [matrixBlockSum_star, matrixBlockSum_mul,
104         Unitary.star_mul_self_of_mem u.property, Unitary.star_mul_self_of_mem v.property,
105         matrixBlockSum_one]
106     · rw [matrixBlockSum_star, matrixBlockSum_mul,
107         Unitary.mul_star_self_of_mem u.property, Unitary.mul_star_self_of_mem v.property,
108         matrixBlockSum_one]⟩
109 
110 @[simp] theorem unitaryBlockSum_coe (u : MatrixUnitary A ι) (v : MatrixUnitary A κ) :
111     (unitaryBlockSum u v : CStarMatrix (ι ⊕ κ) (ι ⊕ κ) A) = matrixBlockSum u v := rfl
112 
113 /-- Unitary stabilization adds an identity block, unlike projection stabilization. -/
114 def stabilizeUnitary (u : MatrixUnitary A ι) : MatrixUnitary A (ι ⊕ κ) :=
115   unitaryBlockSum u 1
116 
117 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

UnitaryHomotopy.lean

源码 SHA-256：bf4d4b1221bbfc6f46f53faf1e26975d55901455326576a207c7b5ee1ce7e23e

  1 import BC4lean.MatrixStabilization
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Star.Unitary
  3 import Mathlib.Topology.Connected.PathConnected
  4 
  5 /-! # Norm homotopy of matrix unitaries
  6 
  7 These are path components at a fixed matrix size, not yet the stable group K₁.
  8 The topology is the subspace topology of Mathlib's operator-norm C⋆-matrices.
  9 -/
 10 
 11 noncomputable section
 12 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 13 
 14 variable {A : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 15 variable {ι κ : Type*} [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ] [DecidableEq ι] [DecidableEq κ]
 16 
 17 /-- A norm-continuous path of matrix unitaries joins the two endpoints. -/
 18 def UnitaryHomotopic (u v : MatrixUnitary A ι) : Prop := Joined u v
 19 
 20 theorem unitaryHomotopic_refl (u : MatrixUnitary A ι) : UnitaryHomotopic u u :=
 21   Joined.refl u
 22 
 23 theorem unitaryHomotopic_symm {u v : MatrixUnitary A ι} (h : UnitaryHomotopic u v) :
 24     UnitaryHomotopic v u := Joined.symm h
 25 
 26 theorem unitaryHomotopic_trans {u v w : MatrixUnitary A ι}
 27     (h : UnitaryHomotopic u v) (k : UnitaryHomotopic v w) : UnitaryHomotopic u w :=
 28   Joined.trans h k
 29 
 30 theorem unitaryHomotopic_mul {u v w z : MatrixUnitary A ι}
 31     (h : UnitaryHomotopic u v) (k : UnitaryHomotopic w z) :
 32     UnitaryHomotopic (u * w) (v * z) := Joined.mul h k
 33 
 34 theorem unitaryHomotopic_inv {u v : MatrixUnitary A ι} (h : UnitaryHomotopic u v) :
 35     UnitaryHomotopic u⁻¹ v⁻¹ := Joined.inv h
 36 
 37 /-- Fixed-size norm-path components of the unitary group. -/
 38 abbrev UnitaryHomotopyClasses (A : Type*) [CStarAlgebra A]
 39     [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] (ι : Type*) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] :=
 40   Quotient (pathSetoid (MatrixUnitary A ι))
 41 
 42 omit [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ]
 43     [DecidableEq ι] [DecidableEq κ] in
 44 theorem continuous_matrixBlockSum :
 45     Continuous (fun x : CStarMatrix ι ι A × CStarMatrix κ κ A =>
 46       matrixBlockSum x.1 x.2) := by
 47   exact CStarMatrix.ofMatrixL.continuous.comp
 48     ((CStarMatrix.ofMatrixL.symm.continuous.comp continuous_fst).matrix_fromBlocks
 49       continuous_const continuous_const
 50       (CStarMatrix.ofMatrixL.symm.continuous.comp continuous_snd))
 51 
 52 theorem continuous_unitaryBlockSum :
 53     Continuous (fun x : MatrixUnitary A ι × MatrixUnitary A κ =>
 54       unitaryBlockSum x.1 x.2) := by
 55   apply Continuous.subtype_mk
 56   exact continuous_matrixBlockSum.comp
 57     ((continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_fst).prodMk
 58       (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_snd))
 59 
 60 theorem unitaryHomotopic_blockSum {u v : MatrixUnitary A ι} {w z : MatrixUnitary A κ}
 61     (h : UnitaryHomotopic u v) (k : UnitaryHomotopic w z) :
 62     UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryBlockSum u w) (unitaryBlockSum v z) := by
 63   exact (Joined.prod h k).map
 64     continuous_unitaryBlockSum
 65 
 66 theorem unitaryHomotopic_stabilize {u v : MatrixUnitary A ι}
 67     (h : UnitaryHomotopic u v) :
 68     UnitaryHomotopic (stabilizeUnitary (κ := κ) u) (stabilizeUnitary v) :=
 69   unitaryHomotopic_blockSum h (unitaryHomotopic_refl 1)
 70 
 71 /-- Stabilization is well-defined on norm-path components at each size. -/
 72 def stabilizeUnitaryClasses : UnitaryHomotopyClasses A ι →
 73     UnitaryHomotopyClasses A (ι ⊕ κ) :=
 74   Quotient.map stabilizeUnitary (fun _ _ h => unitaryHomotopic_stabilize h)
 75 
 76 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

RectangularEquivalence.lean

源码 SHA-256：64e81b38d480648be4ef60b8def7cb16a622f16290f763a4f91d959c4c1112dd

  1 import BC4lean.MatrixStabilization
  2 
  3 /-! # Murray–von Neumann equivalence across finite matrix sizes
  4 
  5 Rectangular witnesses compare projections without choosing an ordering of the
  6 finite index sets. Their support identities follow from the C⋆-identity.
  7 -/
  8 
  9 noncomputable section
 10 open scoped Matrix
 11 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 12 
 13 variable {A : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 14 variable {ι κ τ υ : Type*} [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ] [Fintype τ] [Fintype υ]
 15 variable [DecidableEq ι] [DecidableEq κ] [DecidableEq τ] [DecidableEq υ]
 16 
 17 abbrev projectionMatrix (p : MatrixProjection A ι) : Matrix ι ι A :=
 18   CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm p.val
 19 
 20 @[simp] theorem projectionMatrix_star (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 21     (projectionMatrix p)ᴴ = projectionMatrix p := p.property.isSelfAdjoint.star_eq
 22 
 23 @[simp] theorem projectionMatrix_mul (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 24     projectionMatrix p * projectionMatrix p = projectionMatrix p := p.property.isIdempotentElem.eq
 25 
 26 /-- Initial and final projections of a rectangular partial isometry. -/
 27 def RectEquivalent (p : MatrixProjection A ι) (q : MatrixProjection A κ) : Prop :=
 28   ∃ v : Matrix ι κ A, v * vᴴ = projectionMatrix p ∧ vᴴ * v = projectionMatrix q
 29 
 30 /-- The right support equation is derived, rather than included in the definition. -/
 31 theorem rectangular_right_support (q : MatrixProjection A κ) (v : Matrix ι κ A)
 32     (hv : vᴴ * v = projectionMatrix q) : v * projectionMatrix q = v := by
 33   let V : CStarMatrix (ι ⊕ κ) (ι ⊕ κ) A :=
 34     CStarMatrix.ofMatrix (Matrix.fromBlocks (0 : Matrix ι ι A) v 0 0)
 35   let P : MatrixProjection A (ι ⊕ κ) := projectionBlockSum Projection.zero q
 36   have hMat :
 37       (Matrix.fromBlocks (0 : Matrix ι ι A) v (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (0 : Matrix κ κ A))ᴴ *
 38         Matrix.fromBlocks (0 : Matrix ι ι A) v (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (0 : Matrix κ κ A) =
 39         Matrix.fromBlocks (0 : Matrix ι ι A) (0 : Matrix ι κ A)
 40           (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (projectionMatrix q) := by
 41     simp only [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.conjTranspose_zero,
 42       Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply, Matrix.zero_mul, Matrix.mul_zero, add_zero, hv]
 43   have hV : star V * V = P.val := hMat
 44   have h := Projection.right_support P V hV
 45   have hMat' :
 46       Matrix.fromBlocks (0 : Matrix ι ι A) v (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (0 : Matrix κ κ A) *
 47         Matrix.fromBlocks (0 : Matrix ι ι A) (0 : Matrix ι κ A)
 48           (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (projectionMatrix q) =
 49       Matrix.fromBlocks (0 : Matrix ι ι A) v (0 : Matrix κ ι A) (0 : Matrix κ κ A) := h
 50   simp only [Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply, Matrix.zero_mul, Matrix.mul_zero,
 51     zero_add, add_zero] at hMat'
 52   exact congrArg Matrix.toBlocks₁₂ hMat'
 53 
 54 theorem rectangular_left_support (p : MatrixProjection A ι) (v : Matrix ι κ A)
 55     (hv : v * vᴴ = projectionMatrix p) : projectionMatrix p * v = v := by
 56   have h := rectangular_right_support p vᴴ (by simpa using hv)
 57   simpa using congrArg Matrix.conjTranspose h
 58 
 59 theorem rectEquivalent_refl (p : MatrixProjection A ι) : RectEquivalent p p :=
 60   ⟨projectionMatrix p, by simp, by simp⟩
 61 
 62 theorem rectEquivalent_symm {p : MatrixProjection A ι} {q : MatrixProjection A κ}
 63     (h : RectEquivalent p q) : RectEquivalent q p := by
 64   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := h
 65   exact ⟨vᴴ, by simpa using hv', by simpa using hv⟩
 66 
 67 theorem rectEquivalent_trans {p : MatrixProjection A ι} {q : MatrixProjection A κ}
 68     {r : MatrixProjection A τ} (h : RectEquivalent p q) (k : RectEquivalent q r) :
 69     RectEquivalent p r := by
 70   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := h
 71   obtain ⟨w, hw, hw'⟩ := k
 72   refine ⟨v * w, ?_, ?_⟩
 73   · calc
 74       (v * w) * (v * w)ᴴ = (v * (w * wᴴ)) * vᴴ := by
 75         simp only [Matrix.conjTranspose_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc]
 76       _ = (v * projectionMatrix q) * vᴴ := by rw [hw]
 77       _ = projectionMatrix p := by rw [rectangular_right_support q v hv', hv]
 78   · calc
 79       (v * w)ᴴ * (v * w) = wᴴ * ((vᴴ * v) * w) := by
 80         simp only [Matrix.conjTranspose_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc]
 81       _ = wᴴ * (projectionMatrix q * w) := by rw [hv']
 82       _ = projectionMatrix r := by rw [rectangular_left_support q w hw, hw']
 83 
 84 /-- Rectangular equivalence agrees with the earlier relation at equal index types. -/
 85 theorem rectEquivalent_iff_equivalent (p q : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 86     RectEquivalent p q ↔ Projection.Equivalent p q := by
 87   constructor
 88   · rintro ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩
 89     refine ⟨CStarMatrix.ofMatrix vᴴ, ?_, ?_⟩
 90     · change vᴴᴴ * vᴴ = projectionMatrix p
 91       simpa only [Matrix.conjTranspose_conjTranspose] using hv
 92     · change vᴴ * vᴴᴴ = projectionMatrix q
 93       simpa only [Matrix.conjTranspose_conjTranspose] using hv' 
 94   · rintro ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩
 95     change (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm v)ᴴ * CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm v = projectionMatrix p at hv
 96     change CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm v * (CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm v)ᴴ = projectionMatrix q at hv'
 97     exact ⟨(CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm v)ᴴ, by simpa using hv, by simpa using hv'⟩
 98 
 99 theorem rectEquivalent_blockSum {p : MatrixProjection A ι} {q : MatrixProjection A κ}
100     {r : MatrixProjection A τ} {s : MatrixProjection A υ}
101     (h : RectEquivalent p q) (k : RectEquivalent r s) :
102     RectEquivalent (projectionBlockSum p r) (projectionBlockSum q s) := by
103   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := h
104   obtain ⟨w, hw, hw'⟩ := k
105   refine ⟨Matrix.fromBlocks v 0 0 w, ?_, ?_⟩
106   · change Matrix.fromBlocks v (0 : Matrix ι υ A) (0 : Matrix τ κ A) w *
107       (Matrix.fromBlocks v 0 0 w)ᴴ =
108       Matrix.fromBlocks (projectionMatrix p) 0 0 (projectionMatrix r)
109     simp only [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.conjTranspose_zero,
110       Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply, Matrix.zero_mul, Matrix.mul_zero, zero_add, add_zero, hv, hw]
111   · change (Matrix.fromBlocks v (0 : Matrix ι υ A) (0 : Matrix τ κ A) w)ᴴ *
112       Matrix.fromBlocks v 0 0 w =
113       Matrix.fromBlocks (projectionMatrix q) 0 0 (projectionMatrix s)
114     simp only [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.conjTranspose_zero,
115       Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply, Matrix.zero_mul, Matrix.mul_zero, zero_add, add_zero, hv', hw']
116 
117 /-- A change of finite matrix indices does not change the equivalence class. -/
118 theorem rectEquivalent_of_reindex (p : MatrixProjection A ι) (q : MatrixProjection A κ)
119     (e : κ ≃ ι) (hq : projectionMatrix q = (projectionMatrix p).submatrix e e) :
120     RectEquivalent p q := by
121   refine ⟨(projectionMatrix p).submatrix id e, ?_, ?_⟩
122   · simp only [Matrix.conjTranspose_submatrix, projectionMatrix_star,
123       Matrix.submatrix_mul_equiv, projectionMatrix_mul, Matrix.submatrix_id_id]
124   · rw [Matrix.conjTranspose_submatrix, projectionMatrix_star]
125     change (projectionMatrix p).submatrix e (Equiv.refl ι) *
126       (projectionMatrix p).submatrix (Equiv.refl ι) e = projectionMatrix q
127     rw [Matrix.submatrix_mul_equiv, projectionMatrix_mul, ← hq]
128 
129 theorem rectEquivalent_zero : RectEquivalent (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A ι)
130     (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A κ) := by
131   refine ⟨0, ?_, ?_⟩ <;> simp only [Matrix.conjTranspose_zero, Matrix.zero_mul] <;> rfl
132 
133 theorem rectEquivalent_block_comm (p : MatrixProjection A ι) (q : MatrixProjection A κ) :
134     RectEquivalent (projectionBlockSum p q) (projectionBlockSum q p) := by
135   apply rectEquivalent_of_reindex _ _ (Equiv.sumComm κ ι)
136   ext i j
137   cases i <;> cases j <;> rfl
138 
139 /-- Adding a zero block does not change the rectangular equivalence class. -/
140 theorem rectEquivalent_stabilize (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
141     RectEquivalent p (stabilizeProjection (κ := κ) p) := by
142   have h : RectEquivalent (projectionBlockSum p (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A κ))
143       (projectionBlockSum p (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A Empty)) :=
144     rectEquivalent_blockSum (rectEquivalent_refl p) rectEquivalent_zero
145   have k : RectEquivalent p (projectionBlockSum p (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A Empty)) := by
146     apply rectEquivalent_of_reindex _ _ (Equiv.sumEmpty ι Empty)
147     ext i j
148     rcases i with i | i
149     · rcases j with j | j
150       · rfl
151       · exact j.elim
152     · exact i.elim
153   exact rectEquivalent_trans k (rectEquivalent_symm h)
154 
155 /-- Rectangular witnesses are equivalent to ordinary equivalence in a common
156 matrix algebra after the two projections are placed in disjoint corners. -/
157 theorem rectEquivalent_iff_padded (p : MatrixProjection A ι) (q : MatrixProjection A κ) :
158     RectEquivalent p q ↔ Projection.Equivalent
159       (projectionBlockSum p (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A κ))
160       (projectionBlockSum (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A ι) q) := by
161   have hp := rectEquivalent_stabilize (κ := κ) p
162   have hq := rectEquivalent_trans (rectEquivalent_stabilize (κ := ι) q)
163     (rectEquivalent_block_comm q (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A ι))
164   rw [← rectEquivalent_iff_equivalent]
165   constructor
166   · intro h
167     exact rectEquivalent_trans (rectEquivalent_trans (rectEquivalent_symm hp) h) hq
168   · intro h
169     exact rectEquivalent_trans (rectEquivalent_trans hp h) (rectEquivalent_symm hq)
170 
171 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

StableProjectionMonoid.lean

源码 SHA-256：e61cd47688e2e352293eb0a933f93b06d5fdd59275359faf87e6858386e8c6a6

  1 import BC4lean.RectangularEquivalence
  2 
  3 /-! # The stable projection monoid
  4 
  5 Objects are projections indexed by arbitrary small finite types. Rectangular
  6 Murray–von Neumann witnesses make the quotient independent of the index type.
  7 Addition is actual matrix block sum, not a freely imposed relation.
  8 -/
  9 
 10 noncomputable section
 11 open scoped Matrix
 12 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 13 
 14 universe u
 15 variable {A : Type u} [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 16 
 17 /-- A finite matrix projection, with its finite index type recorded. -/
 18 structure FiniteProjection (A : Type u) [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] where
 19   Index : Type
 20   [indexFintype : Fintype Index]
 21   [indexDecidableEq : DecidableEq Index]
 22   projection : MatrixProjection A Index
 23 
 24 attribute [instance] FiniteProjection.indexFintype FiniteProjection.indexDecidableEq
 25 
 26 namespace FiniteProjection
 27 
 28 /-- Record the index type of a matrix projection. -/
 29 def ofProjection {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 30     FiniteProjection A where
 31   Index := ι
 32   projection := p
 33 
 34 /-- Every object can be represented using a standard finite ordinal. -/
 35 def toFin (p : FiniteProjection A) : FiniteProjection A where
 36   Index := Fin (Fintype.card p.Index)
 37   projection := Projection.map
 38     (CStarMatrix.reindexₐ ℂ A (Fintype.equivFin p.Index)).toStarAlgHom p.projection
 39 
 40 /-- Block sum of finite matrix projections. -/
 41 def blockSum (p q : FiniteProjection A) : FiniteProjection A where
 42   Index := p.Index ⊕ q.Index
 43   projection := projectionBlockSum p.projection q.projection
 44 
 45 /-- The empty matrix represents zero. -/
 46 def zero : FiniteProjection A where
 47   Index := Empty
 48   projection := Projection.zero
 49 
 50 /-- Equivalence compares different sizes using a rectangular partial isometry. -/
 51 def Equivalent (p q : FiniteProjection A) : Prop := RectEquivalent p.projection q.projection
 52 
 53 theorem equivalent_refl (p : FiniteProjection A) : Equivalent p p :=
 54   rectEquivalent_refl p.projection
 55 
 56 theorem equivalent_symm {p q : FiniteProjection A} (h : Equivalent p q) : Equivalent q p :=
 57   rectEquivalent_symm h
 58 
 59 theorem equivalent_trans {p q r : FiniteProjection A}
 60     (h : Equivalent p q) (k : Equivalent q r) : Equivalent p r := rectEquivalent_trans h k
 61 
 62 theorem equivalent_toFin (p : FiniteProjection A) : Equivalent p p.toFin := by
 63   apply rectEquivalent_of_reindex _ _ (Fintype.equivFin p.Index).symm
 64   rfl
 65 
 66 theorem equivalent_zero {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] :
 67     Equivalent (ofProjection (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A ι)) zero :=
 68   rectEquivalent_zero
 69 
 70 def setoid : Setoid (FiniteProjection A) where
 71   r := Equivalent
 72   iseqv := ⟨equivalent_refl, equivalent_symm, equivalent_trans⟩
 73 
 74 theorem blockSum_congr {p q r s : FiniteProjection A}
 75     (h : Equivalent p q) (k : Equivalent r s) : Equivalent (blockSum p r) (blockSum q s) :=
 76   rectEquivalent_blockSum h k
 77 
 78 theorem blockSum_comm (p q : FiniteProjection A) :
 79     Equivalent (blockSum p q) (blockSum q p) := by
 80   apply rectEquivalent_of_reindex _ _ (Equiv.sumComm q.Index p.Index)
 81   ext i j
 82   cases i <;> cases j <;> rfl
 83 
 84 theorem blockSum_assoc (p q r : FiniteProjection A) :
 85     Equivalent (blockSum (blockSum p q) r) (blockSum p (blockSum q r)) := by
 86   apply rectEquivalent_of_reindex _ _ (Equiv.sumAssoc p.Index q.Index r.Index).symm
 87   ext i j
 88   rcases i with i | (i | i) <;> rcases j with j | (j | j) <;> rfl
 89 
 90 theorem zero_blockSum (p : FiniteProjection A) : Equivalent (blockSum zero p) p := by
 91   apply equivalent_symm
 92   apply rectEquivalent_of_reindex _ _ (Equiv.emptySum Empty p.Index)
 93   ext i j
 94   rcases i with i | i
 95   · exact i.elim
 96   · rcases j with j | j
 97     · exact j.elim
 98     · rfl
 99 
100 theorem blockSum_zero (p : FiniteProjection A) : Equivalent (blockSum p zero) p :=
101   equivalent_trans (blockSum_comm p zero) (zero_blockSum p)
102 
103 end FiniteProjection
104 
105 /-- Stable Murray–von Neumann classes of finite matrix projections. -/
106 abbrev StableProjectionMonoid (A : Type u) [CStarAlgebra A]
107     [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] := Quotient (FiniteProjection.setoid (A := A))
108 
109 namespace StableProjectionMonoid
110 
111 /-- The class represented by a finite matrix projection. -/
112 def of (p : FiniteProjection A) : StableProjectionMonoid A := Quotient.mk _ p
113 
114 instance : Zero (StableProjectionMonoid A) := ⟨of FiniteProjection.zero⟩
115 
116 instance : Add (StableProjectionMonoid A) :=
117   ⟨Quotient.map₂ FiniteProjection.blockSum (fun _ _ h _ _ k =>
118     FiniteProjection.blockSum_congr h k)⟩
119 
120 @[simp] theorem of_blockSum (p q : FiniteProjection A) :
121     of (FiniteProjection.blockSum p q) = of p + of q := rfl
122 
123 theorem of_eq_of_equivalent {p q : FiniteProjection A} (h : FiniteProjection.Equivalent p q) :
124     of p = of q := Quotient.sound h
125 
126 theorem of_eq_iff (p q : FiniteProjection A) :
127     of p = of q ↔ FiniteProjection.Equivalent p q := Quotient.eq
128 
129 /-- Block sum gives an additive commutative monoid on the stable classes. -/
130 instance instAddCommMonoid : AddCommMonoid (StableProjectionMonoid A) where
131   add_assoc a b c := Quotient.inductionOn₃ a b c fun p q r =>
132     Quotient.sound (FiniteProjection.blockSum_assoc p q r)
133   zero_add a := Quotient.inductionOn a fun p => Quotient.sound (FiniteProjection.zero_blockSum p)
134   add_zero a := Quotient.inductionOn a fun p => Quotient.sound (FiniteProjection.blockSum_zero p)
135   add_comm a b := Quotient.inductionOn₂ a b fun p q =>
136     Quotient.sound (FiniteProjection.blockSum_comm p q)
137   nsmul := nsmulRec
138 
139 @[simp] theorem of_zeroProjection {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] :
140     of (FiniteProjection.ofProjection (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A ι)) = 0 :=
141   Quotient.sound FiniteProjection.equivalent_zero
142 
143 /-- The quotient really identifies zero-padding of a projection. -/
144 theorem of_stabilize {ι κ : Type} [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ]
145     [DecidableEq ι] [DecidableEq κ] (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
146     of (FiniteProjection.ofProjection (stabilizeProjection (κ := κ) p)) =
147       of (FiniteProjection.ofProjection p) :=
148   Quotient.sound (rectEquivalent_symm (rectEquivalent_stabilize p))
149 
150 /-- Allowing arbitrary small finite index types adds no extra classes. -/
151 theorem exists_finRepresentative (x : StableProjectionMonoid A) :
152     ∃ (n : ℕ) (p : MatrixProjection A (Fin n)), of (FiniteProjection.ofProjection p) = x := by
153   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
154   intro p
155   exact ⟨Fintype.card p.Index, p.toFin.projection,
156     Quotient.sound (FiniteProjection.equivalent_symm (FiniteProjection.equivalent_toFin p))⟩
157 
158 end StableProjectionMonoid
159 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

OperatorK0.lean

源码 SHA-256：bf4d9c7370788493ac0339b6bb884700db599ec86a87e5c5f147abe95e1492d6

  1 import BC4lean.StableProjectionMonoid
  2 import Mathlib.GroupTheory.MonoidLocalization.GrothendieckGroup
  3 
  4 /-! # Unital operator K₀ as a Grothendieck group
  5 
  6 The input is the verified stable projection monoid. No cancellation or
  7 injectivity of its canonical map to the group completion is assumed.
  8 -/
  9 
 10 noncomputable section
 11 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 12 
 13 universe u v
 14 variable {A : Type u} [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 15 
 16 /-- The unital operator K₀ group, obtained by group-completing V(A). -/
 17 abbrev K0 (A : Type u) [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] :=
 18   Algebra.GrothendieckAddGroup (StableProjectionMonoid A)
 19 
 20 /-- The canonical additive map from stable projection classes to K₀. -/
 21 def k0Class : StableProjectionMonoid A →+ K0 A := Algebra.GrothendieckAddGroup.of
 22 
 23 /-- The K₀ class of a finite matrix projection. -/
 24 def k0Projection (p : FiniteProjection A) : K0 A := k0Class (StableProjectionMonoid.of p)
 25 
 26 @[simp] theorem k0Projection_zero : k0Projection (FiniteProjection.zero : FiniteProjection A) = 0 :=
 27   map_zero k0Class
 28 
 29 @[simp] theorem k0Projection_blockSum (p q : FiniteProjection A) :
 30     k0Projection (FiniteProjection.blockSum p q) = k0Projection p + k0Projection q := by
 31   simp only [k0Projection, StableProjectionMonoid.of_blockSum, map_add]
 32 
 33 theorem k0Projection_eq_of_equivalent {p q : FiniteProjection A}
 34     (h : FiniteProjection.Equivalent p q) : k0Projection p = k0Projection q :=
 35   congrArg k0Class (StableProjectionMonoid.of_eq_of_equivalent h)
 36 
 37 /-- Every additive map from V(A) to an abelian group extends uniquely to K₀(A). -/
 38 def k0Universal {G : Type v} [AddCommGroup G] :
 39     (StableProjectionMonoid A →+ G) ≃ (K0 A →+ G) := Algebra.GrothendieckAddGroup.lift
 40 
 41 @[simp] theorem k0Universal_comp {G : Type v} [AddCommGroup G]
 42     (f : StableProjectionMonoid A →+ G) : (k0Universal f).comp k0Class = f :=
 43   k0Universal.left_inv f
 44 
 45 @[simp] theorem k0Universal_class {G : Type v} [AddCommGroup G]
 46     (f : StableProjectionMonoid A →+ G) (x : StableProjectionMonoid A) :
 47     k0Universal f (k0Class x) = f x := DFunLike.congr_fun (k0Universal_comp f) x
 48 
 49 theorem k0Universal_unique {G : Type v} [AddCommGroup G]
 50     (f : StableProjectionMonoid A →+ G) (g : K0 A →+ G)
 51     (h : g.comp k0Class = f) : k0Universal f = g := by
 52   apply k0Universal.symm.injective
 53   exact (k0Universal.left_inv f).trans h.symm
 54 
 55 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

  6 
  7 The reduced group C⋆-algebra carries its canonical spectral order. This makes
  8 its stable projection monoid and unital operator K₀ available for every group.

 13 namespace BC4lean

 16 variable (Γ : Type u) [Group Γ]

 27   exact inferInstanceAs (AddCommMonoid (OperatorKTheory.StableProjectionMonoid (ReducedGroupCStar Γ)))
 28

源码 SHA-256：8302a0991199155f7aa3e835bb5fcc264ba4ad9b845b96f83dd81941cfb37592

  1 import BC4lean.StableProjectionMonoid
  2 
  3 /-! # Entrywise maps of finite matrix projections
  4 
  5 A unital complex ⋆-homomorphism acts on rectangular witnesses entrywise.
  6 Consequently it preserves stable Murray–von Neumann equivalence and block sums.
  7 -/
  8 
  9 noncomputable section
 10 open scoped Matrix
 11 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 12 
 13 universe u v w
 14 variable {A : Type u} {B : Type v} {C : Type w}
 15 variable [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B] [CStarAlgebra C]
 16 variable {ι κ : Type*} [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ] [DecidableEq ι] [DecidableEq κ]
 17 
 18 /-- The entrywise map, bundled on square matrices with the C⋆-matrix norm. -/
 19 def matrixMap (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : CStarMatrix ι ι A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] CStarMatrix ι ι B :=
 20   CStarMatrix.ofMatrixStarAlgEquiv.toStarAlgHom.comp
 21     (({ φ.toAlgHom.mapMatrix with
 22         map_star' := fun _ => Matrix.conjTranspose_map φ (fun a => map_star φ a) } :
 23           Matrix ι ι A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] Matrix ι ι B).comp
 24       CStarMatrix.ofMatrixStarAlgEquiv.symm.toStarAlgHom)
 25 
 26 @[simp] theorem matrixMap_apply (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (M : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (i j : ι) :
 27     matrixMap φ M i j = φ (M i j) := rfl
 28 
 29 @[simp] theorem matrixMap_id :
 30     matrixMap (ι := ι) (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) = StarAlgHom.id ℂ (CStarMatrix ι ι A) := by
 31   ext M i j
 32   rfl
 33 
 34 @[simp] theorem matrixMap_comp (ψ : B →⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 35     matrixMap (ι := ι) (ψ.comp φ) = (matrixMap ψ).comp (matrixMap φ) := by
 36   ext M i j
 37   rfl
 38 
 39 variable [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 40 variable [PartialOrder B] [StarOrderedRing B]
 41 variable [PartialOrder C] [StarOrderedRing C]
 42 
 43 /-- Applying the entrywise homomorphism to a matrix projection. -/
 44 def matrixProjectionMap (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : MatrixProjection A ι) : MatrixProjection B ι :=
 45   Projection.map (matrixMap φ) p
 46 
 47 @[simp] theorem projectionMatrix_map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 48     projectionMatrix (matrixProjectionMap φ p) = (projectionMatrix p).map φ := rfl
 49 
 50 @[simp] theorem matrixProjectionMap_id (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 51     matrixProjectionMap (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) p = p := by
 52   apply Subtype.ext
 53   rfl
 54 
 55 @[simp] theorem matrixProjectionMap_comp (ψ : B →⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B)
 56     (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 57     matrixProjectionMap (ψ.comp φ) p = matrixProjectionMap ψ (matrixProjectionMap φ p) := rfl
 58 
 59 @[simp] theorem matrixProjectionMap_zero (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 60     matrixProjectionMap φ (Projection.zero : MatrixProjection A ι) = Projection.zero :=
 61   Projection.map_zero _
 62 
 63 @[simp] theorem matrixProjectionMap_blockSum (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B)
 64     (p : MatrixProjection A ι) (q : MatrixProjection A κ) :
 65     matrixProjectionMap φ (projectionBlockSum p q) =
 66       projectionBlockSum (matrixProjectionMap φ p) (matrixProjectionMap φ q) := by
 67   apply Subtype.ext
 68   ext i j
 69   cases i <;> cases j <;> simp [matrixProjectionMap, Projection.map,
 70     matrixMap_apply, projectionBlockSum, matrixBlockSum, Matrix.fromBlocks]
 71 
 72 /-- Map the actual rectangular witness; no injectivity of φ is required. -/
 73 theorem rectEquivalent_map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B)
 74     {p : MatrixProjection A ι} {q : MatrixProjection A κ} (h : RectEquivalent p q) :
 75     RectEquivalent (matrixProjectionMap φ p) (matrixProjectionMap φ q) := by
 76   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := h
 77   refine ⟨v.map φ, ?_, ?_⟩
 78   · simpa only [Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.conjTranspose_map φ (fun a => map_star φ a),
 79       projectionMatrix_map] using congrArg (fun M => M.map φ) hv
 80   · simpa only [Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.conjTranspose_map φ (fun a => map_star φ a),
 81       projectionMatrix_map] using congrArg (fun M => M.map φ) hv'

 84 
 85 /-- Entrywise transport keeps the finite index type. -/
 86 def map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : FiniteProjection A) : FiniteProjection B where
 87   Index := p.Index
 88   projection := matrixProjectionMap φ p.projection
 89 
 90 @[simp] theorem map_id (p : FiniteProjection A) : map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) p = p := by
 91   cases p

 94 @[simp] theorem map_comp (ψ : B →⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : FiniteProjection A) :
 95     map (ψ.comp φ) p = map ψ (map φ p) := rfl
 96 
 97 @[simp] theorem map_zero (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : map φ zero = zero := by
 98   exact congrArg (fun p : MatrixProjection B Empty => ofProjection p) (matrixProjectionMap_zero φ)
 99

102   exact congrArg (fun r : MatrixProjection B (p.Index ⊕ q.Index) => ofProjection r)
