OperatorK0Functoriality.lean

源码 SHA-256：c8a9e804b78fef151656b5af0b31794e6400684db49dd051775e2a018dad8e37

  1 import BC4lean.MatrixProjectionMap
  2 import BC4lean.OperatorK0
  3 
  4 /-! # Functoriality of unital operator K₀
  5 
  6 First descend entrywise maps to the stable projection monoid, then use the
  7 Grothendieck group universal property. No cancellation hypothesis is needed.
  8 -/
  9 
 10 noncomputable section
 11 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 12 
 13 universe u v w t
 14 variable {A : Type u} {B : Type v} {C : Type w}
 15 variable [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B] [CStarAlgebra C]
 16 variable [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 17 variable [PartialOrder B] [StarOrderedRing B]
 18 variable [PartialOrder C] [StarOrderedRing C]
 19 
 20 namespace StableProjectionMonoid
 21 
 22 /-- The additive map on stable projection classes induced by a unital ⋆-homomorphism. -/
 23 def map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : StableProjectionMonoid A →+ StableProjectionMonoid B where
 24   toFun := Quotient.map (FiniteProjection.map φ) (fun _ _ h => FiniteProjection.equivalent_map φ h)
 25   map_zero' := by
 26     change of (FiniteProjection.map φ FiniteProjection.zero) = of FiniteProjection.zero
 27     rw [FiniteProjection.map_zero]
 28   map_add' x y := by
 29     refine Quotient.inductionOn₂ x y ?_
 30     intro p q
 31     change of (FiniteProjection.map φ (FiniteProjection.blockSum p q)) =
 32       of (FiniteProjection.blockSum (FiniteProjection.map φ p) (FiniteProjection.map φ q))
 33     rw [FiniteProjection.map_blockSum]
 34 
 35 @[simp] theorem map_of (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : FiniteProjection A) :
 36     map φ (of p) = of (FiniteProjection.map φ p) := rfl
 37 
 38 @[simp] theorem map_id : map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) = AddMonoidHom.id (StableProjectionMonoid A) := by
 39   ext x
 40   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
 41   intro p
 42   change of (FiniteProjection.map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) p) = of p
 43   rw [FiniteProjection.map_id]
 44 
 45 @[simp] theorem map_comp (ψ : B →⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 46     map (ψ.comp φ) = (map ψ).comp (map φ) := by
 47   ext x
 48   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
 49   intro p
 50   rfl
 51 
 52 end StableProjectionMonoid
 53 
 54 /-- The homomorphism K₀(A) → K₀(B) induced by a unital complex ⋆-homomorphism. -/
 55 def k0Map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : K0 A →+ K0 B :=
 56   k0Universal (k0Class.comp (StableProjectionMonoid.map φ))
 57 
 58 @[simp] theorem k0Map_class (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (x : StableProjectionMonoid A) :
 59     k0Map φ (k0Class x) = k0Class (StableProjectionMonoid.map φ x) :=
 60   k0Universal_class _ x
 61 
 62 /-- Naturality of the canonical group-completion homomorphism. -/
 63 theorem k0Map_comp_class (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 64     (k0Map φ).comp k0Class = k0Class.comp (StableProjectionMonoid.map φ) :=
 65   k0Universal_comp _
 66 
 67 @[simp] theorem k0Map_projection (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : FiniteProjection A) :
 68     k0Map φ (k0Projection p) = k0Projection (FiniteProjection.map φ p) :=
 69   k0Map_class φ (StableProjectionMonoid.of p)
 70 
 71 /-- Homomorphisms out of K₀ are determined by their values on projection classes. -/
 72 theorem k0Hom_ext {G : Type t} [AddCommGroup G] {f g : K0 A →+ G}
 73     (h : ∀ p : FiniteProjection A, f (k0Projection p) = g (k0Projection p)) : f = g := by
 74   apply k0Universal.symm.injective
 75   change f.comp k0Class = g.comp k0Class
 76   ext x
 77   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
 78   intro p
 79   exact h p
 80 
 81 @[simp] theorem k0Map_id : k0Map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) = AddMonoidHom.id (K0 A) := by
 82   apply k0Hom_ext
 83   intro p
 84   simp only [k0Map_projection, FiniteProjection.map_id, AddMonoidHom.id_apply]
 85 
 86 @[simp] theorem k0Map_comp (ψ : B →⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 87     k0Map (ψ.comp φ) = (k0Map ψ).comp (k0Map φ) := by
 88   apply k0Hom_ext
 89   intro p
 90   simp only [k0Map_projection, FiniteProjection.map_comp, AddMonoidHom.comp_apply]
 91 
 92 /-- The induced map is the unique homomorphism with the entrywise projection-class formula. -/
 93 theorem k0Map_unique (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (f : K0 A →+ K0 B)
 94     (h : ∀ p : FiniteProjection A, f (k0Projection p) = k0Projection (FiniteProjection.map φ p)) :
 95     k0Map φ = f := by
 96   apply k0Hom_ext
 97   intro p
 98   exact (k0Map_projection φ p).trans (h p).symm
 99 
100 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

OperatorK0Equivalence.lean

源码 SHA-256：9e950900ab47ebfbfe4b9f715a551aade982f90c85b6e2d88b9ed5a56a9c0307

  1 import BC4lean.OperatorK0Functoriality
  2 
  3 /-! # Invariance of unital K₀ under star-algebra equivalence
  4 
  5 The forward map comes from the given equivalence and the inverse map from its
  6 inverse. Both inverse laws follow from functoriality. The compatible star orders
  7 remain the explicit implementation assumptions of the matrix-algebra API.
  8 -/
  9 
 10 noncomputable section
 11 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 12 
 13 universe u v w
 14 variable {A : Type u} {B : Type v} {C : Type w}
 15 variable [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B] [CStarAlgebra C]
 16 variable [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 17 variable [PartialOrder B] [StarOrderedRing B]
 18 variable [PartialOrder C] [StarOrderedRing C]
 19 
 20 namespace StableProjectionMonoid
 21 
 22 theorem map_leftInverse (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 23     Function.LeftInverse (map e.symm.toStarAlgHom) (map e.toStarAlgHom) := by
 24   intro x
 25   have h : e.symm.toStarAlgHom.comp e.toStarAlgHom = StarAlgHom.id ℂ A := by
 26     ext a
 27     exact e.symm_apply_apply a
 28   change ((map e.symm.toStarAlgHom).comp (map e.toStarAlgHom)) x = x
 29   rw [← map_comp, h, map_id]
 30   rfl
 31 
 32 theorem map_rightInverse (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 33     Function.RightInverse (map e.symm.toStarAlgHom) (map e.toStarAlgHom) := by
 34   intro x
 35   have h : e.toStarAlgHom.comp e.symm.toStarAlgHom = StarAlgHom.id ℂ B := by
 36     ext a
 37     exact e.apply_symm_apply a
 38   change ((map e.toStarAlgHom).comp (map e.symm.toStarAlgHom)) x = x
 39   rw [← map_comp, h, map_id]
 40   rfl
 41 
 42 /-- A unital star-algebra equivalence induces an additive equivalence of stable projection monoids. -/
 43 def mapEquiv (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : StableProjectionMonoid A ≃+ StableProjectionMonoid B where
 44   toFun := map e.toStarAlgHom
 45   invFun := map e.symm.toStarAlgHom
 46   left_inv := map_leftInverse e
 47   right_inv := map_rightInverse e
 48   map_add' := (map e.toStarAlgHom).map_add
 49 
 50 @[simp] theorem mapEquiv_toAddMonoidHom (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 51     (mapEquiv e).toAddMonoidHom = map e.toStarAlgHom := rfl
 52 
 53 @[simp] theorem mapEquiv_of (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : FiniteProjection A) :
 54     mapEquiv e (of p) = of (FiniteProjection.map e.toStarAlgHom p) := rfl
 55 
 56 @[simp] theorem mapEquiv_refl :
 57     mapEquiv (StarAlgEquiv.refl ℂ A) = AddEquiv.refl (StableProjectionMonoid A) := by
 58   ext x
 59   change map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) x = x
 60   rw [map_id]
 61   rfl
 62 
 63 @[simp] theorem mapEquiv_symm (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : mapEquiv e.symm = (mapEquiv e).symm := by
 64   ext x
 65   rfl
 66 
 67 @[simp] theorem mapEquiv_trans (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (f : B ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) :
 68     mapEquiv (e.trans f) = (mapEquiv e).trans (mapEquiv f) := by
 69   ext x
 70   exact DFunLike.congr_fun (map_comp f.toStarAlgHom e.toStarAlgHom) x
 71 
 72 end StableProjectionMonoid
 73 
 74 theorem k0Map_leftInverse (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 75     Function.LeftInverse (k0Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom) (k0Map e.toStarAlgHom) := by
 76   intro x
 77   have h : e.symm.toStarAlgHom.comp e.toStarAlgHom = StarAlgHom.id ℂ A := by
 78     ext a
 79     exact e.symm_apply_apply a
 80   change ((k0Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom).comp (k0Map e.toStarAlgHom)) x = x
 81   rw [← k0Map_comp, h, k0Map_id]
 82   rfl
 83 
 84 theorem k0Map_rightInverse (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 85     Function.RightInverse (k0Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom) (k0Map e.toStarAlgHom) := by
 86   intro x
 87   have h : e.toStarAlgHom.comp e.symm.toStarAlgHom = StarAlgHom.id ℂ B := by
 88     ext a
 89     exact e.apply_symm_apply a
 90   change ((k0Map e.toStarAlgHom).comp (k0Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom)) x = x
 91   rw [← k0Map_comp, h, k0Map_id]
 92   rfl
 93 
 94 /-- The canonical additive equivalence on unital K₀ induced by a star-algebra equivalence. -/
 95 def k0Equiv (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : K0 A ≃+ K0 B where
 96   toFun := k0Map e.toStarAlgHom
 97   invFun := k0Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom
 98   left_inv := k0Map_leftInverse e
 99   right_inv := k0Map_rightInverse e
100   map_add' := (k0Map e.toStarAlgHom).map_add
101 
102 @[simp] theorem k0Equiv_toAddMonoidHom (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
103     (k0Equiv e).toAddMonoidHom = k0Map e.toStarAlgHom := rfl
104 
105 @[simp] theorem k0Equiv_class (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (x : StableProjectionMonoid A) :
106     k0Equiv e (k0Class x) = k0Class (StableProjectionMonoid.mapEquiv e x) :=
107   k0Map_class e.toStarAlgHom x
108 
109 @[simp] theorem k0Equiv_projection (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (p : FiniteProjection A) :
110     k0Equiv e (k0Projection p) = k0Projection (FiniteProjection.map e.toStarAlgHom p) :=
111   k0Map_projection e.toStarAlgHom p
112 
113 @[simp] theorem k0Equiv_refl : k0Equiv (StarAlgEquiv.refl ℂ A) = AddEquiv.refl (K0 A) := by
114   ext x
115   change k0Map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) x = x
116   rw [k0Map_id]
117   rfl
118 
119 @[simp] theorem k0Equiv_symm (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : k0Equiv e.symm = (k0Equiv e).symm := by
120   ext x
121   rfl
122 
123 @[simp] theorem k0Equiv_trans (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (f : B ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) :
124     k0Equiv (e.trans f) = (k0Equiv e).trans (k0Equiv f) := by
125   ext x
126   exact DFunLike.congr_fun (k0Map_comp f.toStarAlgHom e.toStarAlgHom) x

ReducedCompletionK0.lean

源码 SHA-256：f46dd10925af007c8f7fa33ec2becbc25df9a0082c739a1de12cc630f4fcb21c

  1 import BC4lean.OperatorK0Equivalence
  2 import BC4lean.ReducedCompletion
  3 import BC4lean.ReducedOperatorK0
  4 
  5 /-! # Abstract and concrete models of the degree-zero analytic target
  6 
  7 The actual reduced-norm completion and the concrete reduced group C⋆-algebra
  8 are equipped with their canonical spectral orders. Their already verified
  9 star-algebra equivalence induces the comparison on projection classes and K₀.
 10 No countability assumption, computation of K₀, or assembly theorem is used.
 11 -/
 12 
 13 noncomputable section
 14 namespace BC4lean
 15 open OperatorKTheory
 16 
 17 universe u
 18 variable (Γ : Type u) [Group Γ]
 19 
 20 local instance completionSpectralOrder : PartialOrder (ReducedCompletion Γ) :=
 21   CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder (ReducedCompletion Γ)
 22 
 23 local instance completionSpectralOrderedRing : StarOrderedRing (ReducedCompletion Γ) :=
 24   CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing (ReducedCompletion Γ)
 25 
 26 local instance reducedSpectralOrder : PartialOrder (ReducedGroupCStar Γ) :=
 27   CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder (ReducedGroupCStar Γ)
 28 
 29 local instance reducedSpectralOrderedRing : StarOrderedRing (ReducedGroupCStar Γ) :=
 30   CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing (ReducedGroupCStar Γ)
 31 
 32 /-- Stable projection classes in the actual abstract reduced-norm completion. -/
 33 def ReducedCompletionProjectionMonoid : Type _ := StableProjectionMonoid (ReducedCompletion Γ)
 34 
 35 instance reducedCompletionProjectionAddCommMonoid :
 36     AddCommMonoid (ReducedCompletionProjectionMonoid Γ) :=
 37   inferInstanceAs (AddCommMonoid (StableProjectionMonoid (ReducedCompletion Γ)))
 38 
 39 /-- K₀ of the abstract reduced-norm completion, using its canonical spectral order. -/
 40 def ReducedCompletionK0 : Type _ := K0 (ReducedCompletion Γ)
 41 
 42 instance reducedCompletionK0AddCommGroup : AddCommGroup (ReducedCompletionK0 Γ) :=
 43   inferInstanceAs (AddCommGroup (K0 (ReducedCompletion Γ)))
 44 
 45 /-- The group-completion map for stable projection classes in the abstract model. -/
 46 def reducedCompletionK0Class : ReducedCompletionProjectionMonoid Γ →+ ReducedCompletionK0 Γ :=
 47   k0Class
 48 
 49 /-- Apply the canonical algebra comparison entrywise to a finite matrix projection. -/
 50 def reducedCompletionProjectionMap (p : FiniteProjection (ReducedCompletion Γ)) :
 51     FiniteProjection (ReducedGroupCStar Γ) :=
 52   FiniteProjection.map (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ).toStarAlgHom p
 53 
 54 @[simp] theorem reducedCompletionProjectionMap_entry (p : FiniteProjection (ReducedCompletion Γ))
 55     (i j : p.Index) :
 56     projectionMatrix (reducedCompletionProjectionMap Γ p).projection i j =
 57       reducedCompletionEquiv Γ (projectionMatrix p.projection i j) := rfl
 58 
 59 /-- Canonical comparison of the stable projection monoids of the two models. -/
 60 def reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv :
 61     ReducedCompletionProjectionMonoid Γ ≃+ ReducedProjectionMonoid Γ :=
 62   StableProjectionMonoid.mapEquiv (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ)
 63 
 64 @[simp] theorem reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv_of (p : FiniteProjection (ReducedCompletion Γ)) :
 65     reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv Γ (StableProjectionMonoid.of p) =
 66       StableProjectionMonoid.of (reducedCompletionProjectionMap Γ p) :=
 67   StableProjectionMonoid.mapEquiv_of (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ) p
 68 
 69 @[simp] theorem reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv_symm_of
 70     (p : FiniteProjection (ReducedGroupCStar Γ)) :
 71     (reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv Γ).symm (StableProjectionMonoid.of p) =
 72       StableProjectionMonoid.of
 73         (FiniteProjection.map (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ).symm.toStarAlgHom p) := rfl
 74 
 75 /-- The abstract completion gives canonically the same degree-zero analytic target. -/
 76 def reducedCompletionK0Equiv : ReducedCompletionK0 Γ ≃+ ReducedK0 Γ :=
 77   k0Equiv (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ)
 78 
 79 @[simp] theorem reducedCompletionK0Equiv_class (x : ReducedCompletionProjectionMonoid Γ) :
 80     reducedCompletionK0Equiv Γ (reducedCompletionK0Class Γ x) =
 81       reducedK0Class Γ (reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv Γ x) :=
 82   k0Equiv_class (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ) x
 83 
 84 @[simp] theorem reducedCompletionK0Equiv_symm_class (x : ReducedProjectionMonoid Γ) :
 85     (reducedCompletionK0Equiv Γ).symm (reducedK0Class Γ x) =
 86       reducedCompletionK0Class Γ ((reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv Γ).symm x) :=
 87   k0Equiv_class (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ).symm x
 88 
 89 /-- Naturality of group completion for the abstract/concrete comparison. -/
 90 theorem reducedCompletionK0Equiv_comp_class :
 91     (reducedCompletionK0Equiv Γ).toAddMonoidHom.comp (reducedCompletionK0Class Γ) =
 92       (reducedK0Class Γ).comp (reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv Γ).toAddMonoidHom := by
 93   ext x
 94   exact reducedCompletionK0Equiv_class Γ x
 95 
 96 @[simp] theorem reducedCompletionK0Equiv_projection (p : FiniteProjection (ReducedCompletion Γ)) :
 97     reducedCompletionK0Equiv Γ (reducedCompletionK0Class Γ (StableProjectionMonoid.of p)) =
 98       reducedK0Class Γ (StableProjectionMonoid.of (reducedCompletionProjectionMap Γ p)) :=
 99   k0Equiv_projection (reducedCompletionEquiv Γ) p
100 
101 /-- The comparison is uniquely determined by compatibility with projection classes. -/
102 theorem reducedCompletionK0Equiv_unique (f : ReducedCompletionK0 Γ →+ ReducedK0 Γ)
103     (h : f.comp (reducedCompletionK0Class Γ) =
104       (reducedK0Class Γ).comp (reducedCompletionProjectionEquiv Γ).toAddMonoidHom) :
105     (reducedCompletionK0Equiv Γ).toAddMonoidHom = f := by
106   apply k0Hom_ext
107   intro p
108   exact (reducedCompletionK0Equiv_class Γ (StableProjectionMonoid.of p)).trans
109     (DFunLike.congr_fun h (StableProjectionMonoid.of p)).symm

UnitaryRotation.lean

源码 SHA-256：997580bc744a09a0c35007ceb52f228aaefc3f5a884f3e7b564ccced1e999dc7

  1 import BC4lean.UnitaryHomotopy
  2 import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Trigonometric.Basic
  3 
  4 /-! # Block rotation and the inverse homotopy for matrix unitaries
  5 
  6 The explicit rotation path gives the inverse mechanism for stable K₁.
  7 The stable quotient and its group structure are separate constructions.
  8 -/
  9 
 10 noncomputable section
 11 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 12 open scoped Matrix
 13 
 14 variable {A : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 15 variable {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι]
 16 
 17 /-- The scalar block rotation through angle `t`, with identity blocks indexed by `ι`. -/
 18 def rotationMatrix (t : ℝ) : CStarMatrix (ι ⊕ ι) (ι ⊕ ι) A :=
 19   CStarMatrix.ofMatrix (Matrix.fromBlocks
 20     (Real.cos t • (1 : Matrix ι ι A)) (-Real.sin t • (1 : Matrix ι ι A))
 21     (Real.sin t • (1 : Matrix ι ι A)) (Real.cos t • (1 : Matrix ι ι A)))
 22 
 23 omit [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] in
 24 theorem rotationMatrix_mem_unitary (t : ℝ) :
 25     rotationMatrix (A := A) (ι := ι) t ∈ unitary (CStarMatrix (ι ⊕ ι) (ι ⊕ ι) A) := by
 26   have h : Real.cos t * Real.cos t + Real.sin t * Real.sin t = 1 := by
 27     nlinarith [Real.sin_sq_add_cos_sq t]
 28   have h' : Real.sin t * Real.sin t + Real.cos t * Real.cos t = 1 := by
 29     simpa only [add_comm] using h
 30   rw [Unitary.mem_iff]
 31   let M : Matrix (ι ⊕ ι) (ι ⊕ ι) A := Matrix.fromBlocks
 32     (Real.cos t • (1 : Matrix ι ι A)) (-Real.sin t • (1 : Matrix ι ι A))
 33     (Real.sin t • (1 : Matrix ι ι A)) (Real.cos t • (1 : Matrix ι ι A))
 34   change (Mᴴ * M = 1) ∧ (M * Mᴴ = 1)
 35   dsimp [M]
 36   simp only [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.conjTranspose_smul,
 37     star_trivial, Matrix.conjTranspose_one, Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply,
 38     Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.one_mul, smul_smul]
 39   constructor <;>
 40     simp [mul_neg, neg_smul, ← add_smul, h, h', mul_comm, Matrix.fromBlocks_one]
 41 
 42 /-- Scalar rotations, bundled as matrix unitaries. -/
 43 def rotationUnitary (t : ℝ) : MatrixUnitary A (ι ⊕ ι) :=
 44   ⟨rotationMatrix t, rotationMatrix_mem_unitary t⟩
 45 
 46 theorem continuous_rotationUnitary :
 47     Continuous (rotationUnitary (A := A) (ι := ι)) := by
 48   apply Continuous.subtype_mk
 49   exact CStarMatrix.ofMatrixL.continuous.comp
 50     ((Real.continuous_cos.smul continuous_const).matrix_fromBlocks
 51       (Real.continuous_sin.neg.smul continuous_const)
 52       (Real.continuous_sin.smul continuous_const)
 53       (Real.continuous_cos.smul continuous_const))
 54 
 55 @[simp] theorem rotationUnitary_zero : rotationUnitary (A := A) (ι := ι) 0 = 1 := by
 56   apply Subtype.ext
 57   change rotationMatrix 0 = 1
 58   simp only [rotationMatrix, Real.cos_zero, Real.sin_zero, neg_zero,
 59     one_smul, zero_smul, Matrix.fromBlocks_one]
 60   rfl
 61 
 62 @[simp] theorem unitaryBlockSum_one :
 63     unitaryBlockSum (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι) (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι) = 1 := by
 64   apply Subtype.ext
 65   exact matrixBlockSum_one
 66 
 67 theorem unitaryBlockSum_mul (u v w z : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
 68     unitaryBlockSum u v * unitaryBlockSum w z = unitaryBlockSum (u * w) (v * z) := by
 69   apply Subtype.ext
 70   change matrixBlockSum (u : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (v : CStarMatrix ι ι A) *
 71     matrixBlockSum (w : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (z : CStarMatrix ι ι A) =
 72     matrixBlockSum ((u : CStarMatrix ι ι A) * (w : CStarMatrix ι ι A))
 73       ((v : CStarMatrix ι ι A) * (z : CStarMatrix ι ι A))
 74   exact matrixBlockSum_mul (u : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (w : CStarMatrix ι ι A)
 75     (v : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (z : CStarMatrix ι ι A)
 76 
 77 /-- A quarter-turn exchanges diagonal blocks by conjugation. -/
 78 theorem rotationUnitary_conjugate (u v : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
 79     rotationUnitary (Real.pi / 2) * unitaryBlockSum u v *
 80       (rotationUnitary (Real.pi / 2))⁻¹ = unitaryBlockSum v u := by
 81   apply Subtype.ext
 82   let U : Matrix ι ι A := CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm u
 83   let V : Matrix ι ι A := CStarMatrix.ofMatrix.symm v
 84   let R : Matrix (ι ⊕ ι) (ι ⊕ ι) A := Matrix.fromBlocks
 85     (Real.cos (Real.pi / 2) • (1 : Matrix ι ι A))
 86     (-Real.sin (Real.pi / 2) • (1 : Matrix ι ι A))
 87     (Real.sin (Real.pi / 2) • (1 : Matrix ι ι A))
 88     (Real.cos (Real.pi / 2) • (1 : Matrix ι ι A))
 89   change R * Matrix.fromBlocks U 0 0 V * Rᴴ = Matrix.fromBlocks V 0 0 U
 90   dsimp [R]
 91   simp [Real.cos_pi_div_two, Real.sin_pi_div_two,
 92     Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]
 93 
 94 /-- Rotate the second factor from the lower block into the upper block. -/
 95 def unitaryBlockProductPath (u v : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
 96     Path (unitaryBlockSum u v) (unitaryBlockSum (u * v) 1) where
 97   toFun t := unitaryBlockSum u 1 * rotationUnitary ((t : ℝ) * (Real.pi / 2)) *
 98     unitaryBlockSum 1 v * (rotationUnitary ((t : ℝ) * (Real.pi / 2)))⁻¹
 99   continuous_toFun := by
100     have h : Continuous (fun t : unitInterval =>
101         rotationUnitary (A := A) (ι := ι) ((t : ℝ) * (Real.pi / 2))) :=
102       continuous_rotationUnitary.comp
103       (continuous_subtype_val.mul_const (Real.pi / 2))
104     exact ((continuous_const.mul h).mul continuous_const).mul h.inv
105   source' := by
106     simp [unitaryBlockSum_mul]
107   target' := by
108     simp only [Set.Icc.coe_one, one_mul]
109     rw [mul_assoc (unitaryBlockSum u 1), mul_assoc (unitaryBlockSum u 1),
110       rotationUnitary_conjugate]
111     simp only [unitaryBlockSum_mul, one_mul]
112 
113 /-- After doubling the matrix size, block sum agrees with multiplication up to norm homotopy. -/
114 theorem unitaryHomotopic_blockSum_mul (u v : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
115     UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryBlockSum u v) (unitaryBlockSum (u * v) 1) :=
116   ⟨unitaryBlockProductPath u v⟩
117 
118 /-- The block sum of a unitary and its adjoint has an explicit path to the identity. -/
119 def unitaryInversePath (u : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
120     Path (unitaryBlockSum u (star u)) 1 := by
121   simpa using unitaryBlockProductPath u (star u)
122 
123 theorem unitaryHomotopic_blockSum_star (u : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
124     UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryBlockSum u (star u)) 1 :=
125   ⟨unitaryInversePath u⟩
126 
127 theorem unitaryHomotopic_star_blockSum (u : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
128     UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryBlockSum (star u) u) 1 := by
129   simpa using unitaryHomotopic_blockSum_star (star u)
130 
131 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

StableUnitary.lean

源码 SHA-256：9e3fbfdfcf96bcc88a2345931487a93224ccd8636020ecd7f12c590ed279fe17

  1 import BC4lean.UnitaryRotation
  2 import BC4lean.MatrixProjectionMap
  3 import Mathlib.Logic.Relation
  4 
  5 /-! # Stable norm-homotopy of finite matrix unitaries
  6 
  7 The generators are actual norm homotopies (after a change of finite coordinates)
  8 and adjoining identity blocks. No additive or inverse relations are imposed.
  9 -/
 10 noncomputable section
 11 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 12 universe u
 13 variable {A : Type u} [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
 14 variable {ι κ ρ σ : Type} [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ] [Fintype ρ] [Fintype σ]
 15 variable [DecidableEq ι] [DecidableEq κ] [DecidableEq ρ] [DecidableEq σ]
 16 
 17 def unitaryReindex (e : ι ≃ κ) (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) : MatrixUnitary A κ :=
 18   ⟨CStarMatrix.reindexₐ ℂ A e a, Unitary.map_mem (CStarMatrix.reindexₐ ℂ A e) a.property⟩
 19 
 20 @[simp] theorem unitaryReindex_apply (e : ι ≃ κ) (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) (i j : κ) :
 21     (unitaryReindex e a : CStarMatrix κ κ A) i j = (a : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (e.symm i) (e.symm j) := rfl
 22 
 23 @[simp] theorem unitaryReindex_refl (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
 24     unitaryReindex (Equiv.refl ι) a = a := by apply Subtype.ext; rfl
 25 
 26 @[simp] theorem unitaryReindex_star (e : ι ≃ κ) (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
 27     unitaryReindex e (star a) = star (unitaryReindex e a) := by
 28   apply Subtype.ext
 29   exact map_star (CStarMatrix.reindexₐ ℂ A e) (a : CStarMatrix ι ι A)
 30 
 31 theorem unitaryReindex_blockSum (e : ι ≃ κ) (f : ρ ≃ σ)
 32     (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) (b : MatrixUnitary A ρ) :
 33     unitaryReindex (e.sumCongr f) (unitaryBlockSum a b) =
 34       unitaryBlockSum (unitaryReindex e a) (unitaryReindex f b) := by
 35   apply Subtype.ext
 36   ext i j
 37   cases i <;> cases j <;> rfl
 38 
 39 @[simp] theorem unitaryBlockSum_star (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) (b : MatrixUnitary A κ) :
 40     star (unitaryBlockSum a b) = unitaryBlockSum (star a) (star b) := by
 41   apply Subtype.ext
 42   exact matrixBlockSum_star (a : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (b : CStarMatrix κ κ A)
 43 
 44 structure FiniteUnitary (A : Type u) [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] where
 45   Index : Type
 46   [indexFintype : Fintype Index]
 47   [indexDecidableEq : DecidableEq Index]
 48   val : MatrixUnitary A Index
 49 attribute [instance] FiniteUnitary.indexFintype FiniteUnitary.indexDecidableEq
 50 
 51 namespace FiniteUnitary
 52 
 53 abbrev ofUnitary (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) : FiniteUnitary A := ⟨ι, a⟩
 54 abbrev identity (ι : Type) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] : FiniteUnitary A := ofUnitary (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι)
 55 abbrev zero : FiniteUnitary A := identity Empty
 56 
 57 abbrev blockSum (a b : FiniteUnitary A) : FiniteUnitary A :=
 58   ofUnitary (unitaryBlockSum a.val b.val)
 59 
 60 abbrev adjoint (a : FiniteUnitary A) : FiniteUnitary A := ofUnitary (star a.val)
 61 
 62 /-- The geometric moves defining the stable quotient. -/
 63 inductive Move : FiniteUnitary A → FiniteUnitary A → Prop
 64   | homotopy (a b : FiniteUnitary A) (e : a.Index ≃ b.Index)
 65       (h : UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryReindex e a.val) b.val) : Move a b
 66   | stabilize (a : FiniteUnitary A) (κ : Type) [Fintype κ] [DecidableEq κ] :
 67       Move a (blockSum a (identity κ))
 68 
 69 /-- A finite chain of coordinate changes, norm homotopies, and identity stabilizations. -/
 70 def Equivalent : FiniteUnitary A → FiniteUnitary A → Prop := Relation.EqvGen Move
 71 
 72 theorem equivalent_refl (a : FiniteUnitary A) : Equivalent a a := .refl _
 73 theorem equivalent_symm {a b : FiniteUnitary A} (h : Equivalent a b) : Equivalent b a := .symm _ _ h
 74 theorem equivalent_trans {a b c : FiniteUnitary A} (h : Equivalent a b) (k : Equivalent b c) :
 75     Equivalent a c := .trans _ _ _ h k
 76 
 77 theorem equivalent_reindex (a b : FiniteUnitary A) (e : a.Index ≃ b.Index)
 78     (h : unitaryReindex e a.val = b.val) : Equivalent a b :=
 79   .rel _ _ (.homotopy a b e (h ▸ unitaryHomotopic_refl _))
 80 
 81 theorem equivalent_homotopy {a b : MatrixUnitary A ι} (h : UnitaryHomotopic a b) :
 82     Equivalent (ofUnitary a) (ofUnitary b) :=
 83   .rel _ _ (.homotopy _ _ (Equiv.refl ι) (by
 84     change UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryReindex (Equiv.refl ι) a) b
 85     simpa only [unitaryReindex_refl] using h))
 86 
 87 theorem equivalent_stabilize (a : FiniteUnitary A) (κ : Type) [Fintype κ] [DecidableEq κ] :
 88     Equivalent a (blockSum a (identity κ)) := .rel _ _ (.stabilize a κ)
 89 
 90 theorem blockSum_comm (a b : FiniteUnitary A) : Equivalent (blockSum a b) (blockSum b a) := by
 91   apply equivalent_reindex _ _ (Equiv.sumComm a.Index b.Index)
 92   apply Subtype.ext
 93   ext i j
 94   cases i <;> cases j <;> rfl
 95 
 96 theorem blockSum_assoc (a b c : FiniteUnitary A) :
 97     Equivalent (blockSum (blockSum a b) c) (blockSum a (blockSum b c)) := by
 98   apply equivalent_reindex _ _ (Equiv.sumAssoc a.Index b.Index c.Index)
 99   apply Subtype.ext
100   ext i j
101   rcases i with i | (i | i) <;> rcases j with j | (j | j) <;> rfl
102 
103 theorem zero_blockSum (a : FiniteUnitary A) : Equivalent (blockSum zero a) a := by
104   apply equivalent_reindex _ _ (Equiv.emptySum Empty a.Index)
105   apply Subtype.ext
106   ext i j
107   rfl
108 
109 theorem blockSum_zero (a : FiniteUnitary A) : Equivalent (blockSum a zero) a :=
110   equivalent_trans (blockSum_comm a zero) (zero_blockSum a)
111 
112 /-- Compatibility with a block context is derived from the geometric moves. -/
113 theorem blockSum_right {a b : FiniteUnitary A} (h : Equivalent a b) (c : FiniteUnitary A) :
114     Equivalent (blockSum a c) (blockSum b c) := by
115   induction h with
116   | refl a => exact equivalent_refl _
117   | symm a b h ih => exact equivalent_symm ih
118   | trans a b d h k ih ik => exact equivalent_trans ih ik
119   | rel a b h =>
120     cases h with
121     | homotopy b e h =>
122       apply Relation.EqvGen.rel
123       apply Move.homotopy _ _ (e.sumCongr (Equiv.refl c.Index))
124       simpa only [blockSum, ofUnitary, unitaryReindex_blockSum, unitaryReindex_refl] using
125         unitaryHomotopic_blockSum h (unitaryHomotopic_refl c.val)
126     | stabilize κ =>
127       -- Move the new identity block past the fixed context by finite reindexing.
128       apply equivalent_trans (equivalent_stabilize (blockSum a c) κ)
129       apply equivalent_reindex _ _
130         (((Equiv.sumAssoc a.Index c.Index κ).trans
131           ((Equiv.refl a.Index).sumCongr (Equiv.sumComm c.Index κ))).trans
132           (Equiv.sumAssoc a.Index κ c.Index).symm)
133       apply Subtype.ext
134       ext i j
135       rcases i with (i | i) | i <;> rcases j with (j | j) | j <;> rfl
136 
137 theorem blockSum_congr {a b c d : FiniteUnitary A}
138     (h : Equivalent a b) (k : Equivalent c d) : Equivalent (blockSum a c) (blockSum b d) :=
139   equivalent_trans (blockSum_right h c)
140     (equivalent_trans (blockSum_comm b c)
141       (equivalent_trans (blockSum_right k b) (blockSum_comm d b)))
142 
143 theorem adjoint_congr {a b : FiniteUnitary A} (h : Equivalent a b) :
144     Equivalent (adjoint a) (adjoint b) := by
145   induction h with
146   | refl a => exact equivalent_refl _
147   | symm a b h ih => exact equivalent_symm ih
148   | trans a b c h k ih ik => exact equivalent_trans ih ik
149   | rel a b h =>
150     cases h with
151     | homotopy b e h =>
152       apply Relation.EqvGen.rel
153       apply Move.homotopy (adjoint a) (adjoint b) e
154       change UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryReindex e (star a.val)) (star b.val)
155       rw [unitaryReindex_star]
156       exact unitaryHomotopic_inv h
157     | stabilize κ =>
158       have eq : adjoint (blockSum a (identity κ)) = blockSum (adjoint a) (identity κ) := by
159         change ofUnitary (star (unitaryBlockSum a.val 1)) = ofUnitary (unitaryBlockSum (star a.val) 1)
160         rw [unitaryBlockSum_star, star_one]
161       rw [eq]
162       exact equivalent_stabilize (adjoint a) κ
163 
164 theorem equivalent_identity (ι : Type) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] :
165     Equivalent (identity (A := A) ι) zero :=
166   equivalent_symm (equivalent_trans (equivalent_stabilize zero ι) (zero_blockSum (identity ι)))
167 
168 theorem adjoint_blockSum (a : FiniteUnitary A) : Equivalent (blockSum (adjoint a) a) zero :=
169   equivalent_trans (equivalent_homotopy (unitaryHomotopic_star_blockSum a.val))
170     (equivalent_identity (a.Index ⊕ a.Index))
171 
172 def setoid : Setoid (FiniteUnitary A) := Relation.EqvGen.setoid Move
173 
174 end FiniteUnitary
175 
176 /-- The stable unitary norm-homotopy quotient, with arbitrary finite coordinates. -/
177 abbrev K1 (A : Type u) [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] :=
178   Quotient (FiniteUnitary.setoid (A := A))
179 
180 namespace K1
181 
182 def of (a : FiniteUnitary A) : K1 A := Quotient.mk _ a
183 instance : Zero (K1 A) := ⟨of FiniteUnitary.zero⟩
184 instance : Add (K1 A) := ⟨Quotient.map₂ FiniteUnitary.blockSum
185   (fun _ _ h _ _ k => FiniteUnitary.blockSum_congr h k)⟩
186 instance : Neg (K1 A) := ⟨Quotient.map FiniteUnitary.adjoint (fun _ _ h => FiniteUnitary.adjoint_congr h)⟩
187 
188 @[simp] theorem of_blockSum (a b : FiniteUnitary A) : of (FiniteUnitary.blockSum a b) = of a + of b := rfl
189 @[simp] theorem of_adjoint (a : FiniteUnitary A) : of (FiniteUnitary.adjoint a) = -of a := rfl
190 
191 theorem of_eq_iff (a b : FiniteUnitary A) : of a = of b ↔ FiniteUnitary.Equivalent a b := Quotient.eq
192 
193 instance instAddCommGroup : AddCommGroup (K1 A) where
194   add_assoc a b c := Quotient.inductionOn₃ a b c fun a b c => Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.blockSum_assoc a b c)
195   zero_add a := Quotient.inductionOn a fun a => Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.zero_blockSum a)
196   add_zero a := Quotient.inductionOn a fun a => Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.blockSum_zero a)
197   add_comm a b := Quotient.inductionOn₂ a b fun a b => Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.blockSum_comm a b)
198   neg_add_cancel a := Quotient.inductionOn a fun a => Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.adjoint_blockSum a)
199   nsmul := nsmulRec
200   zsmul := zsmulRec
201 
202 @[simp] theorem of_identity (ι : Type) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] :
203     of (FiniteUnitary.identity (A := A) ι) = 0 := Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.equivalent_identity ι)
204 
205 theorem of_homotopic {a b : MatrixUnitary A ι} (h : UnitaryHomotopic a b) :
206     of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary a) = of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary b) :=
207   Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.equivalent_homotopy h)
208 
209 theorem of_stabilize (a : FiniteUnitary A) (κ : Type) [Fintype κ] [DecidableEq κ] :
210     of (FiniteUnitary.blockSum a (FiniteUnitary.identity κ)) = of a :=
211   Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.equivalent_symm (FiniteUnitary.equivalent_stabilize a κ))
212 
213 end K1
214 /-- Every stable unitary class has an ordinary finite-matrix representative. -/
215 theorem K1.exists_finRepresentative (x : K1 A) :
216     ∃ (n : ℕ) (a : MatrixUnitary A (Fin n)), K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary a) = x := by
217   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
218   intro a
219   let e := Fintype.equivFin a.Index
220   refine ⟨Fintype.card a.Index, unitaryReindex e a.val, ?_⟩
221   exact Quotient.sound (FiniteUnitary.equivalent_symm
222     (FiniteUnitary.equivalent_reindex a (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (unitaryReindex e a.val)) e rfl))
223 
224 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

 15 def matrixUnitaryMap (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) : MatrixUnitary B ι :=
 16   ⟨matrixMap φ a, Unitary.map_mem (matrixMap φ) a.property⟩
 17 
 18 @[simp] theorem matrixUnitaryMap_apply (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) (i j : ι) :
 19     (matrixUnitaryMap φ a : CStarMatrix ι ι B) i j = φ ((a : CStarMatrix ι ι A) i j) := rfl
 20 
 21 theorem continuous_matrixUnitaryMap (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 22     Continuous (matrixUnitaryMap (ι := ι) φ) :=
 23   ((map_continuous (matrixMap φ)).comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
 24 
 25 @[simp] theorem matrixUnitaryMap_one (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 26     matrixUnitaryMap φ (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι) = 1 := by
 27   apply Subtype.ext
 28   exact map_one (matrixMap φ)
 29 
 30 @[simp] theorem matrixUnitaryMap_blockSum (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B)
 31     (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) (b : MatrixUnitary A κ) :
 32     matrixUnitaryMap φ (unitaryBlockSum a b) =
 33       unitaryBlockSum (matrixUnitaryMap φ a) (matrixUnitaryMap φ b) := by
 34   apply Subtype.ext

 38 @[simp] theorem matrixUnitaryMap_reindex (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (e : ι ≃ κ) (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
 39     matrixUnitaryMap φ (unitaryReindex e a) = unitaryReindex e (matrixUnitaryMap φ a) := by
 40   apply Subtype.ext

 44     (h : UnitaryHomotopic a b) : UnitaryHomotopic (matrixUnitaryMap φ a) (matrixUnitaryMap φ b) :=
 45   h.map (continuous_matrixUnitaryMap φ)

 47 namespace FiniteUnitary
 48 
 49 abbrev map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (a : FiniteUnitary A) : FiniteUnitary B :=
 50   ofUnitary (matrixUnitaryMap φ a.val)
 51 
 52 @[simp] theorem map_identity (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (ι : Type) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] :
 53     map φ (identity ι) = identity ι := by
 54   change ofUnitary (matrixUnitaryMap φ 1) = ofUnitary 1
 55   rw [matrixUnitaryMap_one]
 56 
 57 @[simp] theorem map_blockSum (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (a b : FiniteUnitary A) :
 58     map φ (blockSum a b) = blockSum (map φ a) (map φ b) := by
 59   change ofUnitary (matrixUnitaryMap φ (unitaryBlockSum a.val b.val)) = _
 60   rw [matrixUnitaryMap_blockSum]
 61 
 62 theorem equivalent_map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) {a b : FiniteUnitary A} (h : Equivalent a b) :
 63     Equivalent (map φ a) (map φ b) := by
 64   induction h with
 65   | refl a => exact equivalent_refl _
 66   | symm a b h ih => exact equivalent_symm ih
 67   | trans a b c h k ih ik => exact equivalent_trans ih ik
 68   | rel a b h =>
 69     cases h with
 70     | homotopy b e h =>
 71       apply Relation.EqvGen.rel
 72       apply Move.homotopy (map φ a) (map φ b) e
 73       simpa only [map, ofUnitary, matrixUnitaryMap_reindex] using unitaryHomotopic_map φ h
 74     | stabilize κ =>
 75       rw [map_blockSum, map_identity]
 76       exact equivalent_stabilize _ κ
 77 
 78 @[simp] theorem map_id (a : FiniteUnitary A) : map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) a = a := by
 79   cases a
 80   rfl
 81 
 82 @[simp] theorem map_comp (ψ : B →⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (a : FiniteUnitary A) :
 83     map (ψ.comp φ) a = map ψ (map φ a) := rfl
 84 end FiniteUnitary

 86 def k1Map (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : K1 A →+ K1 B where
 87   toFun := Quotient.map (FiniteUnitary.map φ) (fun _ _ h => FiniteUnitary.equivalent_map φ h)
 88   map_zero' := by
 89     change K1.of (FiniteUnitary.map φ (FiniteUnitary.identity Empty)) = _
 90     rw [FiniteUnitary.map_identity]
 91     rfl
 92   map_add' a b := by
 93     refine Quotient.inductionOn₂ a b ?_
 94     intro a b
 95     change K1.of (FiniteUnitary.map φ (FiniteUnitary.blockSum a b)) = _
 96     rw [FiniteUnitary.map_blockSum]
 97     rfl
 98 
 99 @[simp] theorem k1Map_of (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) (a : FiniteUnitary A) :
100     k1Map φ (K1.of a) = K1.of (FiniteUnitary.map φ a) := rfl
101 
102 @[simp] theorem k1Map_id : k1Map (StarAlgHom.id ℂ A) = AddMonoidHom.id (K1 A) := by
103   ext x
104   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
105   intro a
106   exact congrArg K1.of (FiniteUnitary.map_id a)
107 
108 @[simp] theorem k1Map_comp (ψ : B →⋆ₐ[ℂ] C) (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
109     k1Map (ψ.comp φ) = (k1Map ψ).comp (k1Map φ) := by
110   ext x
111   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
112   intro a
113   rfl

  7 /-! # Operator K-groups through forced unitization
  8 
  9 For a possibly nonunital complex C*-algebra, take the kernels of the scalar
 10 quotient maps in degrees zero and one. All matrix orders are spectral orders.
 11 -/
 12 noncomputable section
 13 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 14 universe u v w
 15 
 16 local instance operatorSpectralOrder (B : Type*) [CStarAlgebra B] : PartialOrder B :=
 17   CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder B
 18 local instance operatorSpectralOrderedRing (B : Type*) [CStarAlgebra B] : StarOrderedRing B :=
 19   CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing B
 20 
 21 variable (A : Type u) [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra A]
 22 
 23 /-- The scalar quotient of the forced unitization. -/
 24 def scalarQuotient : Unitization ℂ A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] ℂ :=
 25   { Unitization.fstHom ℂ A with map_star' := Unitization.fst_star }
 26 
 27 /-- The scalar inclusion is a unital section. -/
 28 def scalarSection : ℂ →⋆ₐ[ℂ] Unitization ℂ A := StarAlgHom.ofId ℂ _
 29 
 30 @[simp] theorem scalarQuotient_section :
 31     (scalarQuotient A).comp (scalarSection A) = StarAlgHom.id ℂ ℂ := by
 32   ext z
 33   rfl
 34 
 35 /-- Degree-zero operator K-theory for a possibly nonunital algebra. -/
 36 def OperatorK0 : Type _ := (k0Map (scalarQuotient A)).ker
 37 
 38 /-- Degree-one operator K-theory for a possibly nonunital algebra. -/
 39 def OperatorK1 : Type _ := (k1Map (scalarQuotient A)).ker
 40 
 41 instance operatorK0AddCommGroup : AddCommGroup (OperatorK0 A) :=
 42   inferInstanceAs (AddCommGroup (k0Map (scalarQuotient A)).ker)
 43 instance operatorK1AddCommGroup : AddCommGroup (OperatorK1 A) :=
 44   inferInstanceAs (AddCommGroup (k1Map (scalarQuotient A)).ker)
 45 
 46 /-- Inclusion of the degree-zero kernel into the K-group of the unitization. -/
 47 def operatorK0Inclusion : OperatorK0 A →+ K0 (Unitization ℂ A) :=
 48   (k0Map (scalarQuotient A)).ker.subtype
 49 
 50 /-- Inclusion of the degree-one kernel into the K-group of the unitization. -/
 51 def operatorK1Inclusion : OperatorK1 A →+ K1 (Unitization ℂ A) :=
 52   (k1Map (scalarQuotient A)).ker.subtype
 53 
 54 theorem operatorK0Inclusion_injective : Function.Injective (operatorK0Inclusion A) := Subtype.val_injective
 55 theorem operatorK1Inclusion_injective : Function.Injective (operatorK1Inclusion A) := Subtype.val_injective
 56 
 57 theorem operatorK0_scalar_zero (x : OperatorK0 A) : k0Map (scalarQuotient A) (operatorK0Inclusion A x) = 0 := x.property
 58 theorem operatorK1_scalar_zero (x : OperatorK1 A) : k1Map (scalarQuotient A) (operatorK1Inclusion A x) = 0 := x.property
 59 
 60 variable {A} {B : Type v} {C : Type w} [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra B] [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra C]
 61 
 62 theorem scalarQuotient_natural (φ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) :
 63     (scalarQuotient B).comp (Unitization.starMap φ) = scalarQuotient A := by
 64   ext x
 65   change (Unitization.starMap φ (x : Unitization ℂ A)).fst = 0
 66   simp
 67 
 68 def operatorK0Map (φ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) : OperatorK0 A →+ OperatorK0 B where
 69   toFun x := ⟨k0Map (Unitization.starMap φ) x.val, by
 70     change k0Map (scalarQuotient B) (k0Map (Unitization.starMap φ) x.val) = 0
 71     rw [← AddMonoidHom.comp_apply, ← k0Map_comp, scalarQuotient_natural]
 72     exact x.property⟩
 73   map_zero' := Subtype.ext (map_zero _)
 74   map_add' x y := Subtype.ext (map_add _ x.val y.val)
 75 
 76 def operatorK1Map (φ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) : OperatorK1 A →+ OperatorK1 B where
 77   toFun x := ⟨k1Map (Unitization.starMap φ) x.val, by
 78     change k1Map (scalarQuotient B) (k1Map (Unitization.starMap φ) x.val) = 0
 79     rw [← AddMonoidHom.comp_apply, ← k1Map_comp, scalarQuotient_natural]
 80     exact x.property⟩
 81   map_zero' := Subtype.ext (map_zero _)
 82   map_add' x y := Subtype.ext (map_add _ x.val y.val)

 84 @[simp] theorem operatorK0Map_id : operatorK0Map (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.id ℂ A) = AddMonoidHom.id (OperatorK0 A) := by
 85   ext x

UnitizationSplitting.lean

  1 import Mathlib.Analysis.CStarAlgebra.Unitization
  2 import Mathlib.Algebra.Star.StarAlgHom

  4 /-! # The forced unitization of an already unital algebra -/
  5 noncomputable section
  6 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
  7 variable (A : Type*) [CStarAlgebra A]
  8 
  9 /-- Evaluation of the new scalar unit using the original unit. -/
 10 def unitizationEval : Unitization ℂ A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] A :=
 11   Unitization.starLift (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.id ℂ A)
 12 
 13 @[simp] theorem unitizationEval_apply (x : Unitization ℂ A) :
 14     unitizationEval A x = algebraMap ℂ A x.fst + x.snd := rfl
 15 
 16 /-- The scalar coordinate and original-unit evaluation split the forced unitization. -/
 17 def unitizationProductHom : Unitization ℂ A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] ℂ × A :=
 18   ({ Unitization.fstHom ℂ A with map_star' := Unitization.fst_star } :
 19     Unitization ℂ A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] ℂ).prod (unitizationEval A)
 20 
 21 /-- The splitting is an actual star-algebra isomorphism, with no K-theory premise. -/
 22 def unitizationProductEquiv : Unitization ℂ A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] ℂ × A :=
 23   StarAlgEquiv.ofBijective (unitizationProductHom A) (by
 24     constructor
 25     · intro x y h
 26       have hfst := congrArg (fun z : ℂ × A => z.1) h
 27       change x.fst = y.fst at hfst
 28       have hsnd := congrArg (fun z : ℂ × A => z.2) h
 29       change algebraMap ℂ A x.fst + x.snd = algebraMap ℂ A y.fst + y.snd at hsnd
 30       apply Unitization.ext hfst
 31       rw [hfst] at hsnd
 32       exact add_left_cancel hsnd
 33     · intro x
 34       refine ⟨⟨x.1, x.2 - algebraMap ℂ A x.1⟩, ?_⟩
 35       apply Prod.ext
 36       · rfl
 37       · change algebraMap ℂ A x.1 + (x.2 - algebraMap ℂ A x.1) = x.2
 38         abel)
 39 
 40 @[simp] theorem unitizationProductEquiv_fst (x : Unitization ℂ A) :
 41     (unitizationProductEquiv A x).1 = x.fst := rfl
 42 
 43 @[simp] theorem unitizationProductEquiv_snd (x : Unitization ℂ A) :
 44     (unitizationProductEquiv A x).2 = algebraMap ℂ A x.fst + x.snd := rfl
 45 
 46 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

 14 def projectionMapNU (φ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) (p : MatrixProjection A ι) : MatrixProjection B ι :=
 15   ⟨CStarMatrix.ofMatrix ((projectionMatrix p).map φ), ⟨by
 16     change (projectionMatrix p).map φ * (projectionMatrix p).map φ = (projectionMatrix p).map φ
 17     rw [← Matrix.map_mul, projectionMatrix_mul], by
 18     change ((projectionMatrix p).map φ)ᴴ = (projectionMatrix p).map φ
 19     rw [← Matrix.conjTranspose_map φ (fun x => map_star φ x), projectionMatrix_star]⟩⟩
 20 
 21 @[simp] theorem projectionMatrix_mapNU (φ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 22     projectionMatrix (projectionMapNU φ p) = (projectionMatrix p).map φ := rfl
 23 
 24 theorem rectEquivalent_mapNU (φ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B)
 25     {p : MatrixProjection A ι} {q : MatrixProjection A κ} (h : RectEquivalent p q) :
 26     RectEquivalent (projectionMapNU φ p) (projectionMapNU φ q) := by
 27   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := h
 28   refine ⟨v.map φ, ?_, ?_⟩
 29   · simpa only [Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.conjTranspose_map φ (fun a => map_star φ a),
 30       projectionMatrix_mapNU] using congrArg (fun M => M.map φ) hv
 31   · simpa only [Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.conjTranspose_map φ (fun a => map_star φ a),
 32       projectionMatrix_mapNU] using congrArg (fun M => M.map φ) hv'

 39 @[simp] theorem mapNU_blockSum (φ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) (p q : FiniteProjection A) :
 40     mapNU φ (blockSum p q) = blockSum (mapNU φ p) (mapNU φ q) := by
 41   apply congrArg (fun p : MatrixProjection B (p.Index ⊕ q.Index) => ofProjection p)
 42   apply Subtype.ext
 43   change (Matrix.fromBlocks (projectionMatrix p.projection) (0 : Matrix p.Index q.Index A)
 44     (0 : Matrix q.Index p.Index A) (projectionMatrix q.projection)).map φ =
 45     Matrix.fromBlocks ((projectionMatrix p.projection).map φ) 0 0 ((projectionMatrix q.projection).map φ)
 46   ext i j
 47   cases i <;> cases j <;> simp [Matrix.fromBlocks]

 50   apply congrArg (fun p : MatrixProjection B Empty => ofProjection p)
 51   apply Subtype.ext
 52   ext i
 53   exact i.elim

OperatorK0Product.lean

源码 SHA-256：ee890a855e1f628ce2f800f5d8498a696ebd8d9e1b73bed497776c1afe6c86ff

  1 import BC4lean.NonUnitalK0Map
  2 import Mathlib.Data.Matrix.ColumnRowPartitioned
  3 
  4 /-! # Product decomposition for projection K₀ -/
  5 noncomputable section
  6 open scoped Matrix
  7 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
  8 universe u v
  9 variable {A : Type u} {B : Type v} [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B]
 10 variable [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] [PartialOrder B] [StarOrderedRing B]
 11 variable [PartialOrder (A × B)] [StarOrderedRing (A × B)]
 12 
 13 /-- A projection over a product is the orthogonal sum of its two components. -/
 14 theorem productProjection_split (p : FiniteProjection (A × B)) :
 15     FiniteProjection.Equivalent p
 16       (FiniteProjection.blockSum
 17         (FiniteProjection.mapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B)
 18           (FiniteProjection.map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B) p))
 19         (FiniteProjection.mapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B)
 20           (FiniteProjection.map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) p))) := by
 21   let P := projectionMatrix p.projection
 22   let l : A × B →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] A × B :=
 23     (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B).comp (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B)
 24   let r : A × B →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] A × B :=
 25     (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B).comp (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B)
 26   let L := P.map l
 27   let R := P.map r
 28   have hL : Lᴴ = L := by
 29     dsimp [L]
 30     rw [← Matrix.conjTranspose_map l (fun x => map_star l x)]
 31     exact congrArg (fun M => M.map l) (projectionMatrix_star p.projection)
 32   have hR : Rᴴ = R := by
 33     dsimp [R]
 34     rw [← Matrix.conjTranspose_map r (fun x => map_star r x)]
 35     exact congrArg (fun M => M.map r) (projectionMatrix_star p.projection)
 36   have hLL : L * L = L := by
 37     dsimp [L]
 38     rw [← Matrix.map_mul]
 39     exact congrArg (fun M => M.map l) (projectionMatrix_mul p.projection)
 40   have hRR : R * R = R := by
 41     dsimp [R]
 42     rw [← Matrix.map_mul]
 43     exact congrArg (fun M => M.map r) (projectionMatrix_mul p.projection)
 44   have hLR : L * R = 0 := by
 45     funext i j
 46     apply Prod.ext <;> simp [L, R, l, r, Matrix.mul_apply]
 47   have hRL : R * L = 0 := by
 48     funext i j
 49     apply Prod.ext <;> simp [L, R, l, r, Matrix.mul_apply]
 50   have hsum : L + R = P := by
 51     funext i j
 52     apply Prod.ext <;> simp [L, R, l, r]
 53   change ∃ v : Matrix p.Index (p.Index ⊕ p.Index) (A × B),
 54     v * vᴴ = P ∧ vᴴ * v = Matrix.fromBlocks L 0 0 R
 55   refine ⟨Matrix.fromCols L R, ?_, ?_⟩
 56   · rw [Matrix.conjTranspose_fromCols_eq_fromRows_conjTranspose,
 57       Matrix.fromCols_mul_fromRows, hL, hR, hLL, hRR]
 58     exact hsum
 59   · rw [Matrix.conjTranspose_fromCols_eq_fromRows_conjTranspose,
 60       Matrix.fromRows_mul_fromCols, hL, hR, hLL, hRR, hLR, hRL]
 61 
 62 variable (A B)
 63 
 64 def k0ProductForward : K0 (A × B) →+ K0 A × K0 B :=
 65   (k0Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B)).prod (k0Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B))
 66 
 67 def k0ProductInverse : K0 A × K0 B →+ K0 (A × B) :=
 68   (k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B)).coprod (k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B))
 69 
 70 /-- The component maps reconstruct every K₀ class. -/
 71 theorem k0Product_leftInverse : Function.LeftInverse (k0ProductInverse A B) (k0ProductForward A B) := by
 72   have h : (k0ProductInverse A B).comp (k0ProductForward A B) = AddMonoidHom.id _ := by
 73     apply k0Hom_ext
 74     intro p
 75     change k0MapNU _ (k0Map _ (k0Projection p)) + k0MapNU _ (k0Map _ (k0Projection p)) = k0Projection p
 76     simp only [k0Map_projection, k0MapNU_projection]
 77     rw [← k0Projection_blockSum]
 78     exact (k0Projection_eq_of_equivalent (productProjection_split p)).symm
 79   intro x
 80   exact DFunLike.congr_fun h x
 81 
 82 variable {A B}
 83 
 84 omit [PartialOrder (A × B)] [StarOrderedRing (A × B)] in
 85 @[simp] theorem k0MapNU_zero : k0MapNU (0 : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) = 0 := by
 86   apply k0Hom_ext
 87   intro p
 88   rw [k0MapNU_projection]
 89   change k0Projection (FiniteProjection.ofProjection (projectionMapNU 0 p.projection)) = 0
 90   have h : projectionMapNU (0 : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) p.projection = Projection.zero := by
 91     apply Subtype.ext
 92     rfl
 93   rw [h]
 94   exact (congrArg k0Class (StableProjectionMonoid.of_zeroProjection (A := B) (ι := p.Index))).trans
 95     (map_zero k0Class)
 96 
 97 variable (A B)
 98 
 99 theorem k0Product_rightInverse : Function.RightInverse (k0ProductInverse A B) (k0ProductForward A B) := by
100   have fl : (k0Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B)).comp (k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B)) =
101       AddMonoidHom.id (K0 A) := by
102     rw [← k0MapNU_unital, ← k0MapNU_comp]
103     have h : ((StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B).toNonUnitalStarAlgHom).comp
104         (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B) = NonUnitalStarAlgHom.id ℂ A := by ext; rfl
105     rw [h, k0MapNU_id]
106   have fr : (k0Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B)).comp (k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B)) = 0 := by
107     rw [← k0MapNU_unital, ← k0MapNU_comp]
108     have h : ((StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B).toNonUnitalStarAlgHom).comp
109         (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B) = 0 := by ext; rfl
110     rw [h, k0MapNU_zero]
111   have sl : (k0Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B)).comp (k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B)) = 0 := by
112     rw [← k0MapNU_unital, ← k0MapNU_comp]
113     have h : ((StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B).toNonUnitalStarAlgHom).comp
114         (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B) = 0 := by ext; rfl
115     rw [h, k0MapNU_zero]
116   have sr : (k0Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B)).comp (k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B)) =
117       AddMonoidHom.id (K0 B) := by
118     rw [← k0MapNU_unital, ← k0MapNU_comp]
119     have h : ((StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B).toNonUnitalStarAlgHom).comp
120         (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B) = NonUnitalStarAlgHom.id ℂ B := by ext; rfl
121     rw [h, k0MapNU_id]
122   intro x
123   apply Prod.ext
124   · change k0Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B)
125       (k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B) x.1 +
126         k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B) x.2) = x.1
127     rw [map_add]
128     exact (congrArg₂ (· + ·) (DFunLike.congr_fun fl x.1) (DFunLike.congr_fun fr x.2)).trans (add_zero _)
129   · change k0Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B)
130       (k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inl ℂ A B) x.1 +
131         k0MapNU (NonUnitalStarAlgHom.inr ℂ A B) x.2) = x.2
132     rw [map_add]
133     exact (congrArg₂ (· + ·) (DFunLike.congr_fun sl x.1) (DFunLike.congr_fun sr x.2)).trans (zero_add _)
134 
135 /-- K₀ carries finite products of unital C*-algebras to products of abelian groups. -/
136 def k0ProductEquiv : K0 (A × B) ≃+ K0 A × K0 B where
137   toFun := k0ProductForward A B
138   invFun := k0ProductInverse A B
139   left_inv := k0Product_leftInverse A B
140   right_inv := k0Product_rightInverse A B
141   map_add' := map_add (k0ProductForward A B)
142 
143 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

 11 def matrixPair (x : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (y : CStarMatrix ι ι B) : CStarMatrix ι ι (A × B) :=

 14 @[simp] theorem matrixMap_fst_pair (x : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (y : CStarMatrix ι ι B) :

 16 @[simp] theorem matrixMap_snd_pair (x : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (y : CStarMatrix ι ι B) :

 19 theorem matrix_prod_ext {x y : CStarMatrix ι ι (A × B)}
 20     (h : matrixMap (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B) x = matrixMap (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B) y)
 21     (k : matrixMap (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) x = matrixMap (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) y) : x = y := by
 22   funext i j

 29 /-- Pair finite matrix unitaries of the same size, entry by entry. -/

 34       simp only [map_mul, map_star, map_one, matrixMap_fst_pair, matrixMap_snd_pair,
 35         Unitary.star_mul_self_of_mem a.property, Unitary.mul_star_self_of_mem a.property,
 36         Unitary.star_mul_self_of_mem b.property, Unitary.mul_star_self_of_mem b.property]⟩

 38 @[simp] theorem unitaryPair_fst (a : MatrixUnitary A ι) (b : MatrixUnitary B ι) :

 41     matrixUnitaryMap (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) (unitaryPair a b) = b := by apply Subtype.ext; rfl

 43 @[simp] theorem unitaryPair_one : unitaryPair (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι) (1 : MatrixUnitary B ι) = 1 := by
 44   apply Subtype.ext
 45   change matrixPair (1 : CStarMatrix ι ι A) (1 : CStarMatrix ι ι B) = 1
 46   apply matrix_prod_ext <;> simp only [map_one, matrixMap_fst_pair, matrixMap_snd_pair]

 48 theorem continuous_unitaryPair : Continuous (fun x : MatrixUnitary A ι × MatrixUnitary B ι => unitaryPair x.1 x.2) := by
 49   apply Continuous.subtype_mk
 50   apply CStarMatrix.ofMatrixL.continuous.comp
 51   apply continuous_pi
 52   intro i
 53   apply continuous_pi
 54   intro j
 55   have ha := CStarMatrix.ofMatrixL.symm.continuous.comp
 56     (continuous_subtype_val.comp (continuous_fst : Continuous (fun x : MatrixUnitary A ι × MatrixUnitary B ι => x.1)))
 57   have hb := CStarMatrix.ofMatrixL.symm.continuous.comp
 58     (continuous_subtype_val.comp (continuous_snd : Continuous (fun x : MatrixUnitary A ι × MatrixUnitary B ι => x.2)))
 59   exact ((continuous_apply j).comp ((continuous_apply i).comp ha)).prodMk
 60     ((continuous_apply j).comp ((continuous_apply i).comp hb))

 79 namespace FiniteUnitary
 80 abbrev prodInl (a : FiniteUnitary A) : FiniteUnitary (A × B) := ofUnitary (unitaryPair a.val 1)

 82 theorem prodInl_blockSum (a b : FiniteUnitary A) :
 83     prodInl (B := B) (blockSum a b) = blockSum (prodInl a) (prodInl b) := by
 84   have h : unitaryBlockSum (1 : MatrixUnitary B a.Index) (1 : MatrixUnitary B b.Index) = 1 :=
 85     Subtype.ext matrixBlockSum_one
 86   change ofUnitary (unitaryPair (unitaryBlockSum a.val b.val) 1) = _
 87   rw [← h, unitaryPair_blockSum]

 89 @[simp] theorem prodInl_identity (ι : Type) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] :
 90     prodInl (B := B) (identity (A := A) ι) = identity ι := by
 91   change ofUnitary (unitaryPair (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι) (1 : MatrixUnitary B ι)) = ofUnitary 1
 92   rw [unitaryPair_one]
 93 
 94 theorem prodInl_congr {a b : FiniteUnitary A} (h : Equivalent a b) :
 95     Equivalent (prodInl (B := B) a) (prodInl b) := by
 96   induction h with
 97   | refl a => exact equivalent_refl _
 98   | symm a b h ih => exact equivalent_symm ih
 99   | trans a b c h k ih ik => exact equivalent_trans ih ik
100   | rel a b h =>
101     cases h with
102     | homotopy b e h =>
103       apply Relation.EqvGen.rel
104       apply Move.homotopy (prodInl a) (prodInl b) e
105       change UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryReindex e (unitaryPair a.val 1)) (unitaryPair b.val 1)
106       rw [unitaryPair_reindex, unitaryReindex_one]
107       exact (Joined.prod h (unitaryHomotopic_refl (1 : MatrixUnitary B b.Index))).map continuous_unitaryPair
108     | stabilize κ =>
109       rw [prodInl_blockSum, prodInl_identity]
110       exact equivalent_stabilize _ κ
111 end FiniteUnitary
112 
113 variable (A B)
114 def k1ProductInl : K1 A →+ K1 (A × B) where
115   toFun := Quotient.map FiniteUnitary.prodInl (fun _ _ h => FiniteUnitary.prodInl_congr h)
116   map_zero' := congrArg K1.of (FiniteUnitary.prodInl_identity (A := A) (B := B) Empty)
117   map_add' a b := by
118     refine Quotient.inductionOn₂ a b ?_
119     intro a b
120     exact congrArg K1.of (FiniteUnitary.prodInl_blockSum a b)
121 
122 /-- Swap the factors using the unital coordinate homomorphisms. -/
123 def productSwap : B × A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] A × B := (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ B A).prod (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ B A)
124 
125 def k1ProductInr : K1 B →+ K1 (A × B) := (k1Map (productSwap A B)).comp (k1ProductInl B A)
126 
127 @[simp] theorem k1ProductInl_of (a : FiniteUnitary A) :
128     k1ProductInl A B (K1.of a) = K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (unitaryPair a.val (1 : MatrixUnitary B a.Index))) := rfl
129 
130 @[simp] theorem k1ProductInr_of (b : FiniteUnitary B) :
131     k1ProductInr A B (K1.of b) = K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (unitaryPair (1 : MatrixUnitary A b.Index) b.val)) := by
132   apply congrArg K1.of
133   apply congrArg FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary
134   apply Subtype.ext
135   rfl
136 
137 variable {A B}
138 /-- Multiplication and block sum give the same stable K₁ class. -/
139 theorem k1_of_mul (a b : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
140     K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (a * b)) =
141       K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary a) + K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary b) := by
142   calc
143     _ = K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (unitaryBlockSum (a * b) 1)) :=
144       (K1.of_stabilize (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (a * b)) ι).symm
145     _ = K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (unitaryBlockSum a b)) :=
146       (K1.of_homotopic (unitaryHomotopic_blockSum_mul a b)).symm
147     _ = _ := rfl
148 
149 variable (A B)
150 
151 def k1ProductForward : K1 (A × B) →+ K1 A × K1 B :=
152   (k1Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B)).prod (k1Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B))
153 
154 def k1ProductInverse : K1 A × K1 B →+ K1 (A × B) :=
155   (k1ProductInl A B).coprod (k1ProductInr A B)
156 
157 theorem k1Product_leftInverse : Function.LeftInverse (k1ProductInverse A B) (k1ProductForward A B) := by
158   intro x
159   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
160   intro p
161   change k1ProductInl A B (k1Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B) (K1.of p)) +
162     k1ProductInr A B (k1Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) (K1.of p)) = K1.of p
163   rw [k1Map_of, k1Map_of, k1ProductInl_of, k1ProductInr_of, ← k1_of_mul]
164   apply congrArg K1.of
165   apply congrArg FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary
166   apply Subtype.ext
167   change matrixPair (matrixMap (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B) (p.val : CStarMatrix p.Index p.Index (A × B)))
168       (1 : CStarMatrix p.Index p.Index B) *
169     matrixPair (1 : CStarMatrix p.Index p.Index A)
170       (matrixMap (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) (p.val : CStarMatrix p.Index p.Index (A × B))) =
171     (p.val : CStarMatrix p.Index p.Index (A × B))
172   apply matrix_prod_ext <;>
173     simp only [map_mul, matrixMap_fst_pair, matrixMap_snd_pair, one_mul, mul_one]
174 
175 theorem k1Product_rightInverse : Function.RightInverse (k1ProductInverse A B) (k1ProductForward A B) := by
176   rintro ⟨x,y⟩
177   refine Quotient.inductionOn₂ x y ?_
178   intro p q
179   apply Prod.ext
180   · change k1Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B) (k1ProductInl A B (K1.of p) + k1ProductInr A B (K1.of q)) = K1.of p
181     rw [map_add, k1ProductInl_of, k1ProductInr_of, k1Map_of, k1Map_of]
182     change K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (matrixUnitaryMap (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B) (unitaryPair p.val 1))) +
183       K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (matrixUnitaryMap (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ A B) (unitaryPair 1 q.val))) = _
184     rw [unitaryPair_fst, unitaryPair_fst]
185     change K1.of p + K1.of (FiniteUnitary.identity q.Index) = K1.of p
186     rw [K1.of_identity, add_zero]
187   · change k1Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) (k1ProductInl A B (K1.of p) + k1ProductInr A B (K1.of q)) = K1.of q
188     rw [map_add, k1ProductInl_of, k1ProductInr_of, k1Map_of, k1Map_of]
189     change K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (matrixUnitaryMap (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) (unitaryPair p.val 1))) +
190       K1.of (FiniteUnitary.ofUnitary (matrixUnitaryMap (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ A B) (unitaryPair 1 q.val))) = _
191     rw [unitaryPair_snd, unitaryPair_snd]
192     change K1.of (FiniteUnitary.identity p.Index) + K1.of q = K1.of q
193     rw [K1.of_identity, zero_add]
194 
195 /-- K₁ carries finite products to products of abelian groups. -/
196 def k1ProductEquiv : K1 (A × B) ≃+ K1 A × K1 B where
197   toFun := k1ProductForward A B
198   invFun := k1ProductInverse A B
199   left_inv := k1Product_leftInverse A B
200   right_inv := k1Product_rightInverse A B
201   map_add' := map_add (k1ProductForward A B)
202 
203 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

KernelProductEquiv.lean

源码 SHA-256：7fb17db3a3e1dceabd714e9f0fda1fcf944509133ce8b13ac83606be2a168033

  1 import Mathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Ker
  2 
  3 /-! # Identifying a kernel from an additive product decomposition -/
  4 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
  5 variable {G H J : Type*} [AddCommGroup G] [AddCommGroup H] [AddCommGroup J]
  6 
  7 /-- If the first coordinate of an additive product decomposition is `f`, its kernel
  8 is canonically the second factor. -/
  9 def kernelProductEquiv (e : G ≃+ H × J) (f : G →+ H) (h : ∀ x, (e x).1 = f x) :
 10     f.ker ≃+ J where
 11   toFun x := (e x.val).2
 12   invFun y := ⟨e.symm (0, y), by
 13     change f (e.symm (0, y)) = 0
 14     rw [← h, e.apply_symm_apply]⟩
 15   left_inv x := by
 16     apply Subtype.ext
 17     apply e.injective
 18     rw [e.apply_symm_apply]
 19     apply Prod.ext
 20     · exact (h x.val |>.trans x.property).symm
 21     · rfl
 22   right_inv y := by simp
 23   map_add' x y := by
 24     change (e (x.val + y.val)).2 = (e x.val).2 + (e y.val).2
 25     rw [map_add]
 26     rfl
 27 
 28 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

 13 theorem k1Map_leftInverse (e : A ≃⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 14     Function.LeftInverse (k1Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom) (k1Map e.toStarAlgHom) := by
 15   intro x

 17     ext a
 18     exact e.symm_apply_apply a
 19   change ((k1Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom).comp (k1Map e.toStarAlgHom)) x = x

 24     Function.RightInverse (k1Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom) (k1Map e.toStarAlgHom) := by

 30   rw [← k1Map_comp, h, k1Map_id]

 36   invFun := k1Map e.symm.toStarAlgHom

 38   right_inv := k1Map_rightInverse e

 47   rw [k1Map_id]
 48   rfl

 51   ext x

 55     k1Equiv (e.trans f) = (k1Equiv e).trans (k1Equiv f) := by

  9 /-! # Agreement of the unitization-kernel and unital definitions
 10 
 11 Split the forced unitization as ℂ × A, split the K-groups of that product,
 12 and identify the scalar kernel with the second factor. The construction uses
 13 canonical spectral orders throughout.
 14 -/
 15 noncomputable section
 16 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 17 
 18 local instance comparisonSpectralOrder (B : Type*) [CStarAlgebra B] : PartialOrder B :=
 19   CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder B
 20 local instance comparisonSpectralOrderedRing (B : Type*) [CStarAlgebra B] : StarOrderedRing B :=
 21   CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing B

 24 
 25 theorem unitizationProduct_fst :
 26     (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ ℂ A).comp (unitizationProductEquiv A).toStarAlgHom = scalarQuotient A := by
 27   apply DFunLike.ext
 28   intro x
 29   rfl
 30 
 31 theorem unitizationProduct_snd :
 32     (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ ℂ A).comp (unitizationProductEquiv A).toStarAlgHom = unitizationEval A := by
 33   apply DFunLike.ext
 34   intro x
 35   rfl

 37 /-- The degree-zero kernel definition agrees canonically with unital projection K₀. -/
 38 def operatorK0UnitalEquiv : OperatorK0 A ≃+ K0 A :=
 39   kernelProductEquiv
 40     ((k0Equiv (unitizationProductEquiv A)).trans (k0ProductEquiv ℂ A))
 41     (k0Map (scalarQuotient A)) (by
 42       intro x
 43       change k0Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ ℂ A) (k0Map (unitizationProductEquiv A).toStarAlgHom x) = _
 44       rw [← AddMonoidHom.comp_apply, ← k0Map_comp, unitizationProduct_fst])
 45

 47 def operatorK1UnitalEquiv : OperatorK1 A ≃+ K1 A :=
 48   kernelProductEquiv
 49     ((k1Equiv (unitizationProductEquiv A)).trans (k1ProductEquiv ℂ A))
 50     (k1Map (scalarQuotient A)) (by
 51       intro x
 52       change k1Map (StarAlgHom.fst ℂ ℂ A) (k1Map (unitizationProductEquiv A).toStarAlgHom x) = _
 53       rw [← AddMonoidHom.comp_apply, ← k1Map_comp, unitizationProduct_fst])
 54 
 55 /-- The comparison sends a kernel class by evaluation using the original unit. -/
 56 theorem operatorK0UnitalEquiv_apply (x : OperatorK0 A) :
 57     operatorK0UnitalEquiv A x = k0Map (unitizationEval A) (operatorK0Inclusion A x) := by
 58   change k0Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ ℂ A) (k0Map (unitizationProductEquiv A).toStarAlgHom x.val) = _
 59   rw [← AddMonoidHom.comp_apply, ← k0Map_comp, unitizationProduct_snd]
 60   rfl
 61 
 62 theorem operatorK1UnitalEquiv_apply (x : OperatorK1 A) :
 63     operatorK1UnitalEquiv A x = k1Map (unitizationEval A) (operatorK1Inclusion A x) := by
 64   change k1Map (StarAlgHom.snd ℂ ℂ A) (k1Map (unitizationProductEquiv A).toStarAlgHom x.val) = _
 65   rw [← AddMonoidHom.comp_apply, ← k1Map_comp, unitizationProduct_snd]
 66   rfl

 70 theorem unitizationEval_natural (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) :
 71     (unitizationEval B).comp (Unitization.starMap φ.toNonUnitalStarAlgHom) =
 72       φ.comp (unitizationEval A) := by
 73   ext x

 84     k0Map φ (k0Map (unitizationEval A) x.val)
