UnitaryStableConjugation.lean

源码 SHA-256：8db6d5e677d31606b748f997feffc45c4c26cee79bbed4f3b865e106dc03c33c

  1 import BC4lean.StableUnitary
  2 
  3 /-! # A geometric justification for stable changes of coordinates -/
  4 noncomputable section
  5 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
  6 variable {A : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
  7 variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι]
  8 
  9 /-- Conjugation by any matrix unitary is norm homotopic to the identity action
 10 on a unitary after adjoining an identity block. -/
 11 theorem unitaryHomotopic_stable_conjugate (w a : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
 12     UnitaryHomotopic (unitaryBlockSum (w * a * star w) (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι)) (unitaryBlockSum a (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι)) := by
 13   have h := unitaryHomotopic_blockSum_star w
 14   have hi := unitaryHomotopic_inv h
 15   have hstar : (unitaryBlockSum w (star w))⁻¹ = unitaryBlockSum (star w) w := by
 16     change star (unitaryBlockSum w (star w)) = _
 17     rw [unitaryBlockSum_star, star_star]
 18   rw [hstar, inv_one] at hi
 19   have hh := unitaryHomotopic_mul (unitaryHomotopic_mul h
 20     (unitaryHomotopic_refl (unitaryBlockSum a (1 : MatrixUnitary A ι)))) hi
 21   simpa only [unitaryBlockSum_mul, one_mul, mul_one, Unitary.star_mul_self] using hh
 22 
 23 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

ProjectionHomotopy.lean

源码 SHA-256：9ede6e13834de074b9c88a4af30ab4e414bf555843c982b99cc87b23571decc1

  1 import BC4lean.OperatorProjections
  2 import Mathlib.Analysis.CStarAlgebra.ContinuousFunctionalCalculus.Basic
  3 import Mathlib.Analysis.CStarAlgebra.ContinuousFunctionalCalculus.Commute
  4 import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.ContinuousFunctionalCalculus.Rpow.Basic
  5 import Mathlib.Analysis.Normed.Ring.Units
  6 import Mathlib.Topology.LocallyConstant.Basic
  7 import Mathlib.Topology.Connected.PathConnected
  8 import Mathlib.Tactic.NoncommRing
  9 
 10 /-! # Norm homotopies of projections preserve Murray–von Neumann classes -/
 11 noncomputable section
 12 open Filter Topology
 13 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 14 namespace Projection
 15 variable {A : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A]
 16 
 17 /-- An invertible intertwiner between projections can be normalized to a partial isometry. -/
 18 theorem equivalent_of_invertible_intertwiner (p q : Projection A) (s : A)
 19     (hs : IsUnit s) (hsp : s * p = q * s) : Equivalent p q := by
 20   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder A
 21   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing A
 22   let t := star s * s
 23   have ht : IsUnit t := hs.star.mul hs
 24   have htpos : 0 ≤ t := star_mul_self_nonneg s
 25   let z : A := CFC.rpow t (- (1 / 2 : ℝ))
 26   have hzstar : star z = z := (CFC.rpow_nonneg (a := t) (y := -(1 / 2 : ℝ))).isSelfAdjoint.star_eq
 27   have hzunit : IsUnit z := ht.cfcRpow _ htpos
 28   have hstar : (p : A) * star s = star s * q := by
 29     simpa only [star_mul, star_coe] using congrArg star hsp
 30   have htp : Commute t (p : A) := by
 31     show (star s * s) * p = p * (star s * s)
 32     calc
 33       (star s * s) * p = star s * (q * s) := by rw [mul_assoc, hsp]
 34       _ = (p * star s) * s := by rw [hstar, mul_assoc]
 35       _ = p * (star s * s) := mul_assoc _ _ _
 36   have hzp : z * p = p * z := (htp.cfc_nnreal (fun x => x ^ (-(1 / 2 : ℝ)))).eq
 37   have hztz : z * t * z = 1 := by
 38     simp only [z, CFC.rpow_eq_pow]
 39     calc
 40       t ^ (-(1 / 2 : ℝ)) * t * t ^ (-(1 / 2 : ℝ)) = (t ^ (-(1 / 2 : ℝ)) * t ^ (1 : ℝ)) *
 41           t ^ (-(1 / 2 : ℝ)) := by rw [CFC.rpow_one t htpos]
 42       _ = t ^ ((-(1 / 2 : ℝ) + 1) + -(1 / 2 : ℝ)) := by
 43         rw [CFC.rpow_add ht, CFC.rpow_add ht]
 44       _ = 1 := by norm_num; exact CFC.rpow_zero t htpos
 45   let u := s * z
 46   have hu : star u * u = 1 := by
 47     calc
 48       star u * u = z * t * z := by simp only [u, t, star_mul, hzstar, mul_assoc]
 49       _ = 1 := hztz
 50   have huu : u * star u = 1 := by
 51     apply (hs.mul hzunit).mul_right_cancel
 52     change (u * star u) * u = 1 * u
 53     rw [mul_assoc, hu, mul_one, one_mul]
 54   have hup : u * p = q * u := by
 55     change (s * z) * p = q * (s * z)
 56     rw [mul_assoc, hzp, ← mul_assoc, hsp, mul_assoc]
 57   refine ⟨u * p, ?_, ?_⟩
 58   · calc
 59       star (u * p) * (u * p) = p * (star u * u) * p := by simp only [star_mul, star_coe, mul_assoc]
 60       _ = p := by rw [hu, mul_one, mul_self]
 61   · calc
 62       (u * p) * star (u * p) = u * ((p * p) * star u) := by simp only [star_mul, star_coe, mul_assoc]
 63       _ = (u * p) * star u := by rw [mul_self]; exact (mul_assoc u (p : A) (star u)).symm
 64       _ = q * (u * star u) := by rw [hup, mul_assoc]
 65       _ = q := by rw [huu, mul_one]
 66 
 67 /-- The standard intertwiner is invertible on a neighborhood of a projection. -/
 68 theorem eventually_equivalent (p : Projection A) : ∀ᶠ q in nhds p, Equivalent p q := by
 69   let s : Projection A → A := fun q => (q : A) * p + (1 - q) * (1 - p)
 70   have hc : Continuous s :=
 71     (continuous_subtype_val.mul continuous_const).add
 72       ((continuous_const.sub continuous_subtype_val).mul continuous_const)
 73   have hsp : s p = 1 := by
 74     dsimp [s]
 75     noncomm_ring [mul_self p]
 76   have he : ∀ᶠ q in nhds p, IsUnit (s q) :=
 77     hc.continuousAt (Units.isOpen.mem_nhds (hsp ▸ isUnit_one))
 78   filter_upwards [he] with q hq
 79   apply equivalent_of_invertible_intertwiner p q (s q) hq
 80   dsimp [s]
 81   simp only [add_mul, mul_add, sub_mul, mul_sub, one_mul, mul_one, mul_assoc,
 82     ← mul_assoc (q : A) (q : A) (p : A), mul_self]
 83   noncomm_ring
 84 
 85 /-- The Murray–von Neumann class of a projection is locally constant in norm. -/
 86 theorem isLocallyConstant_class :
 87     IsLocallyConstant (fun p : Projection A => (Quotient.mk _ p : Classes A)) := by
 88   rw [IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq]
 89   intro p
 90   filter_upwards [eventually_equivalent p] with q hq
 91   exact Quotient.sound (equivalent_symm hq)
 92 
 93 /-- Endpoints of a norm-continuous projection path are Murray–von Neumann equivalent. -/
 94 theorem equivalent_of_path {p q : Projection A} (h : Path p q) : Equivalent p q := by
 95   have hc := isLocallyConstant_class.comp_continuous h.continuous
 96   have he := hc.apply_eq_of_preconnectedSpace (0 : unitInterval) 1
 97   change (Quotient.mk _ (h 0) : Classes A) = Quotient.mk _ (h 1) at he
 98   rw [h.source, h.target] at he
 99   exact Quotient.exact he
100 
101 end Projection
102 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

StarHomotopy.lean

源码 SHA-256：5de668399d50bc41ef29e3ad531f47e942c089f519e267cf5e731b8969fb820f

  1 import BC4lean.OperatorK1Functoriality
  2 import Mathlib.Analysis.CStarAlgebra.Unitization
  3 
  4 /-! # Point-norm homotopies of complex star homomorphisms
  5 
  6 Continuity is required separately at every fixed algebra element; no continuity
  7 in the operator norm on the space of homomorphisms is assumed.
  8 -/
  9 noncomputable section
 10 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
 11 
 12 /-- A point-norm homotopy through unital complex star homomorphisms. -/
 13 structure UnitalStarHomotopy {A B : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B]
 14     (φ ψ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) where
 15   toFun : unitInterval → A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B
 16   continuous_apply : ∀ a : A, Continuous (fun t => toFun t a)
 17   source : toFun 0 = φ
 18   target : toFun 1 = ψ
 19 
 20 /-- A point-norm homotopy through possibly nonunital complex star homomorphisms. -/
 21 structure StarHomotopy {A B : Type*} [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra A] [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra B]
 22     (φ ψ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) where
 23   toFun : unitInterval → A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B
 24   continuous_apply : ∀ a : A, Continuous (fun t => toFun t a)
 25   source : toFun 0 = φ
 26   target : toFun 1 = ψ
 27 
 28 namespace UnitalStarHomotopy
 29 variable {A B : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B]
 30 variable {φ ψ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B}
 31 
 32 /-- A constant family is a point-norm homotopy. -/
 33 def refl (φ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B) : UnitalStarHomotopy φ φ where
 34   toFun _ := φ
 35   continuous_apply _ := continuous_const
 36   source := rfl
 37   target := rfl
 38 
 39 /-- Forget preservation of the unit without changing the point-norm continuity. -/
 40 def toStarHomotopy (h : UnitalStarHomotopy φ ψ) :
 41     StarHomotopy φ.toNonUnitalStarAlgHom ψ.toNonUnitalStarAlgHom where
 42   toFun t := (h.toFun t).toNonUnitalStarAlgHom
 43   continuous_apply := h.continuous_apply
 44   source := congrArg StarAlgHom.toNonUnitalStarAlgHom h.source
 45   target := congrArg StarAlgHom.toNonUnitalStarAlgHom h.target
 46 
 47 variable {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι]
 48 
 49 /-- Point-norm continuity gives norm continuity on each fixed finite matrix. -/
 50 theorem continuous_matrixMap (h : UnitalStarHomotopy φ ψ) (M : CStarMatrix ι ι A) :
 51     Continuous (fun t => matrixMap (h.toFun t) M) := by
 52   apply CStarMatrix.ofMatrixL.continuous.comp
 53   exact continuous_pi (fun i => continuous_pi (fun j => h.continuous_apply (M i j)))
 54 
 55 variable [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] [PartialOrder B] [StarOrderedRing B]
 56 
 57 /-- Evaluate a point-norm homotopy on a matrix projection. -/
 58 def projectionPath (h : UnitalStarHomotopy φ ψ) (p : MatrixProjection A ι) :
 59     Path (matrixProjectionMap φ p) (matrixProjectionMap ψ p) where
 60   toFun t := matrixProjectionMap (h.toFun t) p
 61   continuous_toFun := (h.continuous_matrixMap p.val).subtype_mk _
 62   source' := by rw [h.source]
 63   target' := by rw [h.target]
 64 
 65 /-- Evaluate a point-norm homotopy on a matrix unitary. -/
 66 def unitaryPath (h : UnitalStarHomotopy φ ψ) (u : MatrixUnitary A ι) :
 67     Path (⟨matrixMap φ u, Unitary.map_mem (matrixMap φ) u.property⟩ : MatrixUnitary B ι)
 68       ⟨matrixMap ψ u, Unitary.map_mem (matrixMap ψ) u.property⟩ where
 69   toFun t := ⟨matrixMap (h.toFun t) u, Unitary.map_mem (matrixMap (h.toFun t)) u.property⟩
 70   continuous_toFun := (h.continuous_matrixMap u.val).subtype_mk _
 71   source' := by rw [h.source]
 72   target' := by rw [h.target]
 73 end UnitalStarHomotopy
 74 
 75 namespace StarHomotopy
 76 variable {A B : Type*} [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra A] [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra B]
 77 variable {φ ψ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B}
 78 
 79 def refl (φ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B) : StarHomotopy φ φ where
 80   toFun _ := φ
 81   continuous_apply _ := continuous_const
 82   source := rfl
 83   target := rfl
 84 
 85 /-- Forced unitization preserves point-norm homotopies. -/
 86 def unitization (h : StarHomotopy φ ψ) :
 87     UnitalStarHomotopy (Unitization.starMap φ) (Unitization.starMap ψ) where
 88   toFun t := Unitization.starMap (h.toFun t)
 89   continuous_apply x := by
 90     have hx : (fun t => Unitization.starMap (h.toFun t) x) =
 91         fun t => algebraMap ℂ (Unitization ℂ B) x.fst + (h.toFun t x.snd : Unitization ℂ B) := by
 92       funext t
 93       conv_lhs => rw [← Unitization.inl_fst_add_inr_snd_eq x]
 94       rw [map_add, Unitization.starMap_inl, Unitization.starMap_inr]
 95     rw [hx]
 96     exact continuous_const.add (Unitization.continuous_inr.comp (h.continuous_apply x.snd))
 97   source := congrArg Unitization.starMap h.source
 98   target := congrArg Unitization.starMap h.target
 99 end StarHomotopy
100 end BC4lean.OperatorKTheory

OperatorKHomotopy.lean

源码 SHA-256：e0de7464914e757552fbd6966586697dccecb552450d8f33eee790673b0e08af

  1 import BC4lean.ProjectionHomotopy
  2 import BC4lean.StarHomotopy
  3 import BC4lean.NonunitalOperatorK
  4 
  5 /-! # Homotopy invariance of maps on operator K-theory -/
  6 noncomputable section
  7 namespace BC4lean.OperatorKTheory
  8 section Unital
  9 variable {A B : Type*} [CStarAlgebra A] [CStarAlgebra B]
 10 variable [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] [PartialOrder B] [StarOrderedRing B]
 11 variable {φ ψ : A →⋆ₐ[ℂ] B}
 12 
 13 /-- Point-norm homotopic unital homomorphisms have equivalent images of projections. -/
 14 theorem finiteProjection_equivalent_of_homotopy (h : UnitalStarHomotopy φ ψ)
 15     (p : FiniteProjection A) : FiniteProjection.Equivalent (FiniteProjection.map φ p)
 16       (FiniteProjection.map ψ p) := by
 17   obtain ⟨v, hv, hv'⟩ := Projection.equivalent_of_path (h.projectionPath p.projection)
 18   change ∃ w : CStarMatrix p.Index p.Index B,
 19     w * star w = (matrixProjectionMap φ p.projection).val ∧
 20     star w * w = (matrixProjectionMap ψ p.projection).val
 21   exact ⟨star v, by simpa using hv, by simpa using hv'⟩
 22 
 23 /-- Point-norm homotopic unital homomorphisms induce the same map on V(A). -/
 24 theorem stableProjectionMap_homotopy (h : UnitalStarHomotopy φ ψ) :
 25     StableProjectionMonoid.map φ = StableProjectionMonoid.map ψ := by
 26   ext x
 27   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
 28   intro p
 29   exact Quotient.sound (finiteProjection_equivalent_of_homotopy h p)
 30 
 31 /-- Point-norm homotopic unital homomorphisms induce the same K₀ map. -/
 32 theorem k0Map_homotopy (h : UnitalStarHomotopy φ ψ) : k0Map φ = k0Map ψ := by
 33   apply k0Hom_ext
 34   intro p
 35   rw [k0Map_projection, k0Map_projection]
 36   exact k0Projection_eq_of_equivalent (finiteProjection_equivalent_of_homotopy h p)
 37 
 38 /-- Point-norm homotopic unital homomorphisms induce the same K₁ map. -/
 39 theorem k1Map_homotopy (h : UnitalStarHomotopy φ ψ) : k1Map φ = k1Map ψ := by
 40   ext x
 41   refine Quotient.inductionOn x ?_
 42   intro u
 43   change k1Map φ (K1.of u) = k1Map ψ (K1.of u)
 44   rw [k1Map_of, k1Map_of]
 45   exact K1.of_homotopic ⟨h.unitaryPath u.val⟩
 46 end Unital
 47 
 48 section Nonunital
 49 variable {A B : Type*} [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra A] [NonUnitalCStarAlgebra B]
 50 variable {φ ψ : A →⋆ₙₐ[ℂ] B}
 51 
 52 /-- Point-norm homotopic possibly nonunital homomorphisms induce the same operator K₀ map. -/
 53 theorem operatorK0Map_homotopy (h : StarHomotopy φ ψ) : operatorK0Map φ = operatorK0Map ψ := by
 54   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder (Unitization ℂ A)
 55   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing (Unitization ℂ A)
 56   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder (Unitization ℂ B)
 57   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing (Unitization ℂ B)
 58   ext x
 59   apply Subtype.ext
 60   exact DFunLike.congr_fun (k0Map_homotopy h.unitization) x.val
 61 
 62 /-- Point-norm homotopic possibly nonunital homomorphisms induce the same operator K₁ map. -/
 63 theorem operatorK1Map_homotopy (h : StarHomotopy φ ψ) : operatorK1Map φ = operatorK1Map ψ := by
 64   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder (Unitization ℂ A)
 65   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing (Unitization ℂ A)
 66   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder (Unitization ℂ B)
 67   let _ := CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing (Unitization ℂ B)
 68   ext x
 69   apply Subtype.ext
 70   exact DFunLike.congr_fun (k1Map_homotopy h.unitization) x.val
