ProperActions.lean

源码 SHA-256：b0eebca03ad3cab0dda38aad1dafc667edfb67196ea5d8c3042bb5b0bad4a9eb

  1 import Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.ProperAction.CompactlyGenerated
  2 
  3 /-! # Proper actions of discrete groups
  4 
  5 We use Mathlib's `ProperSMul`, defined by properness of the action-pair map.
  6 The compact-transporter criterion requires a locally compact Hausdorff space;
  7 finite stabilizers are proved as a consequence, not used as a definition.
  8 -/
  9 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 10 open scoped Pointwise
 11 
 12 variable {Γ X : Type*} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
 13 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X]
 14 
 15 /-- The map whose properness defines a proper action. -/
 16 def actionMap : Γ × X → X × X := fun p => (p.1 • p.2, p.2)
 17 
 18 /-- The project uses precisely Mathlib's proper-action predicate. -/
 19 theorem proper_iff_actionMap : ProperSMul Γ X ↔ IsProperMap (actionMap (Γ := Γ) (X := X)) :=
 20   properSMul_iff Γ X
 21 
 22 /-- Group elements moving one set to meet another. -/
 23 def transporter (K L : Set X) : Set Γ := {g | (g • K ∩ L).Nonempty}
 24 
 25 /-- A proper discrete-group action has finite compact transporters. -/
 26 theorem finite_transporter [DiscreteTopology Γ] [ProperSMul Γ X]
 27     {K L : Set X} (hK : IsCompact K) (hL : IsCompact L) :
 28     (transporter (Γ := Γ) K L).Finite :=
 29   isCompact_iff_finite.mp (ProperSMul.isCompact_setOfPred_inter_nonempty hK hL)
 30 
 31 /-- Properness implies finite stabilizers without a local compactness hypothesis. -/
 32 theorem finite_stabilizer [DiscreteTopology Γ] [ProperSMul Γ X] (x : X) :
 33     (MulAction.stabilizer Γ x : Set Γ).Finite := by
 34   apply (finite_transporter (Γ := Γ) (K := {x}) (L := {x})
 35     isCompact_singleton isCompact_singleton).subset
 36   intro g hg
 37   refine ⟨g • x, Set.smul_mem_smul_set (Set.mem_singleton x), ?_⟩
 38   exact Set.mem_singleton_iff.mpr (MulAction.mem_stabilizer_iff.mp hg)
 39 
 40 /-- A proper action has a Hausdorff orbit space. -/
 41 theorem orbitSpace_t2 [ProperSMul Γ X] : T2Space (Quotient (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X)) :=
 42   inferInstance
 43 
 44 omit [TopologicalSpace Γ] in
 45 /-- An orbit space of a locally compact space is locally compact for a continuous action. -/
 46 theorem orbitSpace_locallyCompact [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] [LocallyCompactSpace X] :
 47     LocallyCompactSpace (Quotient (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X)) :=
 48   MulAction.isOpenQuotientMap_quotientMk.locallyCompactSpace
 49 
 50 /-- Restricting a proper discrete-group action to any subgroup preserves properness. -/
 51 theorem proper_subgroup [DiscreteTopology Γ] [ProperSMul Γ X] (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 52     ProperSMul H X :=
 53   properSMul_of_isClosedEmbedding H.subtype
 54     (isClosed_discrete (H : Set Γ)).isClosedEmbedding_subtypeVal (fun _ _ => rfl)
 55 
 56 /-- A proper action on a nonempty compact space forces a discrete group to be finite. -/
 57 theorem finite_group_of_compact [DiscreteTopology Γ] [ProperSMul Γ X]
 58     [CompactSpace X] [Nonempty X] : Finite Γ := by
 59   have h : (Set.univ : Set Γ).Finite := by
 60     simpa [transporter] using
 61       (finite_transporter (Γ := Γ) (X := X) isCompact_univ isCompact_univ)
 62   exact Set.finite_univ_iff.mp h
 63 
 64 section LocallyCompact
 65 variable [LocallyCompactSpace X] [T2Space X] [ContinuousSMul Γ X]
 66 
 67 /-- On a locally compact Hausdorff space, properness is compactness of compact preimages. -/
 68 theorem proper_iff_compact_preimages : ProperSMul Γ X ↔
 69     ∀ {K : Set (X × X)}, IsCompact K →
 70       IsCompact ((actionMap (Γ := Γ) (X := X)) ⁻¹' K) := by
 71   rw [proper_iff_actionMap, isProperMap_iff_isCompact_preimage]
 72   exact and_iff_right (continuous_smul.prodMk continuous_snd)
 73 
 74 /-- The compact-transporter characterization of proper discrete-group actions. -/
 75 theorem proper_iff_finite_transporters [DiscreteTopology Γ] : ProperSMul Γ X ↔
 76     ∀ {K L : Set X}, IsCompact K → IsCompact L → (transporter (Γ := Γ) K L).Finite := by
 77   rw [MulAction.properSMul_iff_isCompact_setOfPred_inter_nonempty]
 78   simp only [transporter, isCompact_iff_finite]
 79 
 80 /-- Every continuous action of a finite discrete group on an LCH space is proper. -/
 81 theorem proper_of_finite [DiscreteTopology Γ] [Finite Γ] : ProperSMul Γ X :=
 82   proper_iff_finite_transporters.mpr (fun _ _ => Set.toFinite _)
 83 end LocallyCompact
 84 
 85 /-- Left translation is proper for every topological group, in particular a discrete one. -/
 86 theorem proper_leftTranslation [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] : ProperSMul Γ Γ :=
 87   inferInstance
 88 
 89 end BC4lean.ProperActions

ProperFixedPoints.lean

源码 SHA-256：c81d7199622a28f862a8973980c1438010386ff359ca0ef7e1fc461b59329c90

  1 import BC4lean.ProperActions
  2 
  3 /-! # Subgroup fixed points of proper discrete-group actions
  4 
  5 The empty-fixed-point condition for infinite subgroups follows from properness.
  6 Contractibility of fixed points for finite subgroups is additional data, not a
  7 consequence of properness.
  8 -/
  9 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 10 
 11 variable {Γ X : Type*} [Group Γ] [MulAction Γ X]
 12 
 13 /-- The fixed-point space carries the subspace topology when `X` is topological. -/
 14 abbrev FixedPointSpace (H : Subgroup Γ) (X : Type*) [MulAction Γ X] :=
 15   ↥(MulAction.fixedPoints H X)
 16 
 17 /-- A point is fixed by a subgroup exactly when that subgroup lies in its stabilizer. -/
 18 theorem mem_fixedPoints_iff_le_stabilizer (H : Subgroup Γ) (x : X) :
 19     x ∈ MulAction.fixedPoints H X ↔ H ≤ MulAction.stabilizer Γ x := by
 20   constructor
 21   · intro hx g hg
 22     exact hx ⟨g, hg⟩
 23   · intro hx g
 24     exact hx g.property
 25 
 26 /-- Passing to a smaller subgroup enlarges the fixed-point set. -/
 27 theorem fixedPoints_antitone {H K : Subgroup Γ} (hHK : H ≤ K) :
 28     MulAction.fixedPoints K X ⊆ MulAction.fixedPoints H X := by
 29   intro x hx
 30   exact (mem_fixedPoints_iff_le_stabilizer H x).mpr
 31     (hHK.trans ((mem_fixedPoints_iff_le_stabilizer K x).mp hx))
 32 
 33 /-- Every point is fixed by the trivial subgroup. -/
 34 theorem fixedPoints_bot : MulAction.fixedPoints (⊥ : Subgroup Γ) X = Set.univ := by
 35   ext x
 36   simp
 37 
 38 section Topology
 39 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] [T2Space X]
 40 
 41 /-- Fixed-point sets of subgroups are closed in a Hausdorff space. -/
 42 theorem isClosed_fixedPoints (H : Subgroup Γ) : IsClosed (MulAction.fixedPoints H X) := by
 43   rw [MulAction.fixed_eq_iInter_fixedBy]
 44   apply isClosed_iInter
 45   intro h
 46   exact isClosed_eq (continuous_const_smul (h : Γ)) continuous_id
 47 
 48 /-- Subgroup fixed-point spaces of LCH spaces are locally compact. -/
 49 theorem fixedPointSpace_locallyCompact [LocallyCompactSpace X] (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 50     LocallyCompactSpace (FixedPointSpace H X) :=
 51   (isClosed_fixedPoints (X := X) H).locallyCompactSpace
 52 end Topology
 53 
 54 section Proper
 55 variable [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ] [TopologicalSpace X] [ProperSMul Γ X]
 56 
 57 /-- A subgroup fixing a point in a proper discrete-group action is finite. -/
 58 theorem finite_subgroup_of_fixedPoint {H : Subgroup Γ} {x : X}
 59     (hx : x ∈ MulAction.fixedPoints H X) : (H : Set Γ).Finite :=
 60   (finite_stabilizer (Γ := Γ) x).subset
 61     ((mem_fixedPoints_iff_le_stabilizer H x).mp hx)
 62 
 63 /-- Infinite subgroups have empty fixed-point sets in proper discrete-group actions. -/
 64 theorem fixedPoints_eq_empty_of_infinite (H : Subgroup Γ) [Infinite H] :
 65     MulAction.fixedPoints H X = ∅ := by
 66   apply Set.eq_empty_iff_forall_notMem.mpr
 67   intro x hx
 68   have : Finite H := (finite_subgroup_of_fixedPoint hx).to_subtype
 69   exact not_finite H
 70 
 71 /-- The topological fixed-point space is empty for an infinite subgroup. -/
 72 theorem fixedPointSpace_isEmpty (H : Subgroup Γ) [Infinite H] :
 73     IsEmpty (FixedPointSpace H X) :=
 74   Set.isEmpty_coe_sort.mpr (fixedPoints_eq_empty_of_infinite H)
 75 end Proper
 76 
 77 end BC4lean.ProperActions

EquivariantMaps.lean

源码 SHA-256：454abee2c148ae393bbf0ef5f8f10bf3364a68646718c2b005227eff4b091989

  1 import BC4lean.ProperFixedPoints
  2 import Mathlib.GroupTheory.GroupAction.Hom
  3 
  4 /-! # Continuous equivariant maps and their restrictions to fixed points -/
  5 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
  6 
  7 /-- A continuous map commuting with the action of the same group on source and target. -/
  8 structure EquivariantMap (Γ X Y : Type*) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace X]
  9     [TopologicalSpace Y] [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y] extends C(X, Y) where
 10   map_smul' : ∀ (g : Γ) (x : X), toFun (g • x) = g • toFun x
 11 
 12 namespace EquivariantMap
 13 variable {Γ X Y Z W : Type*} [Group Γ]
 14 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] [TopologicalSpace Z] [TopologicalSpace W]
 15 variable [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y] [MulAction Γ Z] [MulAction Γ W]
 16 
 17 instance instFunLike : FunLike (EquivariantMap Γ X Y) X Y where
 18   coe f := f.toContinuousMap
 19   coe_injective f g h := by
 20     cases f with
 21     | mk f hf =>
 22       cases g with
 23       | mk g hg =>
 24         have hfg : f = g := ContinuousMap.ext (congrFun h)
 25         cases hfg
 26         rfl
 27 
 28 @[ext] theorem ext {f g : EquivariantMap Γ X Y} (h : ∀ x, f x = g x) : f = g :=
 29   DFunLike.ext _ _ h
 30 
 31 @[simp] theorem map_smul (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) (g : Γ) (x : X) :
 32     f (g • x) = g • f x := f.map_smul' g x
 33 
 34 theorem continuous (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : Continuous f := f.toContinuousMap.continuous
 35 
 36 /-- Forget topology, retaining Mathlib's existing equivariant-map structure. -/
 37 def toMulActionHom (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : X →[Γ] Y where
 38   toFun := f
 39   map_smul' := f.map_smul
 40 
 41 /-- The equivariant identity map. -/
 42 def id : EquivariantMap Γ X X where
 43   toContinuousMap := ContinuousMap.id X
 44   map_smul' _ _ := rfl
 45 
 46 /-- Composition of continuous equivariant maps. -/
 47 def comp (g : EquivariantMap Γ Y Z) (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : EquivariantMap Γ X Z where
 48   toContinuousMap := g.toContinuousMap.comp f.toContinuousMap
 49   map_smul' a x := by
 50     change g (f (a • x)) = a • g (f x)
 51     rw [f.map_smul, g.map_smul]
 52 
 53 @[simp] theorem id_apply (x : X) : id (Γ := Γ) x = x := rfl
 54 @[simp] theorem comp_apply (g : EquivariantMap Γ Y Z) (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) (x : X) :
 55     g.comp f x = g (f x) := rfl
 56 @[simp] theorem id_comp (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : id.comp f = f := by ext; rfl
 57 @[simp] theorem comp_id (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : f.comp id = f := by ext; rfl
 58 theorem comp_assoc (h : EquivariantMap Γ Z W) (g : EquivariantMap Γ Y Z)
 59     (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : (h.comp g).comp f = h.comp (g.comp f) := by ext; rfl
 60 
 61 /-- An equivariant map sends subgroup fixed points to subgroup fixed points. -/
 62 theorem map_fixedPoint (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) (H : Subgroup Γ) {x : X}
 63     (hx : x ∈ MulAction.fixedPoints H X) : f x ∈ MulAction.fixedPoints H Y := by
 64   intro h
 65   change (h : Γ) • f x = f x
 66   rw [← f.map_smul]
 67   exact congrArg f (hx h)
 68 
 69 /-- Restrict an equivariant map to the H-fixed-point subspaces. -/
 70 def fixedPointsMap (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 71     C(FixedPointSpace H X, FixedPointSpace H Y) where
 72   toFun x := ⟨f x, f.map_fixedPoint H x.property⟩
 73   continuous_toFun := (f.continuous.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
 74 
 75 @[simp] theorem fixedPointsMap_apply (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) (H : Subgroup Γ)
 76     (x : FixedPointSpace H X) : (f.fixedPointsMap H x : Y) = f x := rfl
 77 
 78 @[simp] theorem fixedPointsMap_id (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 79     (id (Γ := Γ) (X := X)).fixedPointsMap H = ContinuousMap.id (FixedPointSpace H X) := by
 80   ext; rfl
 81 
 82 @[simp] theorem fixedPointsMap_comp (g : EquivariantMap Γ Y Z) (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y)
 83     (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 84     (g.comp f).fixedPointsMap H = (g.fixedPointsMap H).comp (f.fixedPointsMap H) := by
 85   ext; rfl
 86 
 87 end EquivariantMap
 88 end BC4lean.ProperActions

EquivariantHomotopy.lean

源码 SHA-256：41fbf68c613d5372a733d5d9520bb8c925df4356c5403bc79e20259e446c7a79

  1 import BC4lean.EquivariantMaps
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.Homotopy.Basic
  3 
  4 /-! # Jointly continuous equivariant homotopies and fixed-point restriction -/
  5 noncomputable section
  6 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
  7 variable {Γ X Y Z : Type*} [Group Γ]
  8 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] [TopologicalSpace Z]
  9 variable [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y] [MulAction Γ Z]
 10 
 11 /-- A jointly continuous homotopy all of whose time slices are equivariant. -/
 12 abbrev EquivariantHomotopy (f g : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) :=
 13   ContinuousMap.HomotopyWith f.toContinuousMap g.toContinuousMap
 14     (fun k => ∀ (a : Γ) (x : X), k (a • x) = a • k x)
 15 
 16 namespace EquivariantHomotopy
 17 variable {f₀ f₁ f₂ : EquivariantMap Γ X Y}
 18 
 19 /-- The constant equivariant homotopy. -/
 20 def refl (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : EquivariantHomotopy f f :=
 21   ContinuousMap.HomotopyWith.refl f.toContinuousMap f.map_smul
 22 
 23 /-- Reverse an equivariant homotopy. -/
 24 def symm (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) : EquivariantHomotopy f₁ f₀ :=
 25   ContinuousMap.HomotopyWith.symm F
 26 
 27 /-- Concatenate equivariant homotopies using the usual half-interval parametrization. -/
 28 def trans (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) (G : EquivariantHomotopy f₁ f₂) :
 29     EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₂ := ContinuousMap.HomotopyWith.trans F G
 30 
 31 /-- Compose two equivariant homotopies at the same time parameter. -/
 32 def comp {g₀ g₁ : EquivariantMap Γ Y Z} (G : EquivariantHomotopy g₀ g₁)
 33     (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) : EquivariantHomotopy (g₀.comp f₀) (g₁.comp f₁) where
 34   toHomotopy := G.toHomotopy.comp F.toHomotopy
 35   prop' t a x := by
 36     change G (t, F (t, a • x)) = a • G (t, F (t, x))
 37     have hF : F (t, a • x) = a • F (t, x) := F.prop t a x
 38     rw [hF]
 39     exact G.prop t a (F (t, x))
 40 
 41 /-- Each time slice is itself a continuous equivariant map. -/
 42 def atTime (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) (t : unitInterval) : EquivariantMap Γ X Y where
 43   toContinuousMap := F.toHomotopy.curry t
 44   map_smul' := F.prop t
 45 
 46 @[simp] theorem at_apply (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) (t : unitInterval) (x : X) :
 47     F.atTime t x = F (t, x) := rfl
 48 @[simp] theorem at_zero (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) : F.atTime 0 = f₀ := by
 49   ext x
 50   exact F.apply_zero x
 51 @[simp] theorem at_one (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) : F.atTime 1 = f₁ := by
 52   ext x
 53   exact F.apply_one x
 54 
 55 /-- Restrict the whole jointly continuous homotopy to subgroup fixed-point spaces. -/
 56 def onFixedPoints (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 57     ContinuousMap.Homotopy (f₀.fixedPointsMap H) (f₁.fixedPointsMap H) where
 58   toFun p := (F.atTime p.1).fixedPointsMap H p.2
 59   continuous_toFun := (F.continuous.comp
 60     (continuous_fst.prodMk (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_snd))).subtype_mk _
 61   map_zero_left x := Subtype.ext (F.apply_zero x.val)
 62   map_one_left x := Subtype.ext (F.apply_one x.val)
 63 
 64 @[simp] theorem onFixedPoints_apply (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) (H : Subgroup Γ)
 65     (t : unitInterval) (x : FixedPointSpace H X) :
 66     (F.onFixedPoints H (t, x) : Y) = F (t, x.val) := rfl
 67 
 68 @[simp] theorem onFixedPoints_refl (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 69     (refl f).onFixedPoints H = ContinuousMap.Homotopy.refl (f.fixedPointsMap H) := by
 70   ext p; rfl
 71 
 72 @[simp] theorem onFixedPoints_symm (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 73     (symm F).onFixedPoints H = (F.onFixedPoints H).symm := by
 74   ext p; rfl
 75 
 76 theorem onFixedPoints_trans (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) (G : EquivariantHomotopy f₁ f₂)
 77     (H : Subgroup Γ) : (trans F G).onFixedPoints H =
 78       (F.onFixedPoints H).trans (G.onFixedPoints H) := by
 79   ext ⟨t, x⟩
 80   simp only [onFixedPoints_apply, trans, ContinuousMap.HomotopyWith.trans_apply,
 81     ContinuousMap.Homotopy.trans_apply]
 82   split_ifs <;> rfl
 83 
 84 theorem onFixedPoints_comp {g₀ g₁ : EquivariantMap Γ Y Z}
 85     (G : EquivariantHomotopy g₀ g₁) (F : EquivariantHomotopy f₀ f₁) (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 86     (comp G F).onFixedPoints H = (G.onFixedPoints H).comp (F.onFixedPoints H) := by
 87   ext p; rfl
 88 end EquivariantHomotopy
 89 
 90 /-- Existence of a jointly continuous equivariant homotopy. -/
 91 def EquivariantlyHomotopic (f g : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : Prop :=
 92   Nonempty (EquivariantHomotopy f g)
 93 
 94 namespace EquivariantlyHomotopic
 95 variable {f₀ f₁ f₂ : EquivariantMap Γ X Y}
 96 
 97 theorem refl (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : EquivariantlyHomotopic f f :=
 98   ⟨EquivariantHomotopy.refl f⟩
 99 theorem symm (h : EquivariantlyHomotopic f₀ f₁) : EquivariantlyHomotopic f₁ f₀ :=
100   h.elim (fun F => ⟨EquivariantHomotopy.symm F⟩)
101 theorem trans (h : EquivariantlyHomotopic f₀ f₁) (k : EquivariantlyHomotopic f₁ f₂) :
102     EquivariantlyHomotopic f₀ f₂ :=
103   h.elim (fun F => k.elim (fun G => ⟨EquivariantHomotopy.trans F G⟩))
104 theorem comp {g₀ g₁ : EquivariantMap Γ Y Z} (h : EquivariantlyHomotopic g₀ g₁)
105     (k : EquivariantlyHomotopic f₀ f₁) : EquivariantlyHomotopic (g₀.comp f₀) (g₁.comp f₁) :=
106   h.elim (fun G => k.elim (fun F => ⟨EquivariantHomotopy.comp G F⟩))
107 
108 /-- Fixed-point restriction sends equivariant homotopy to ordinary homotopy. -/
109 theorem onFixedPoints (h : EquivariantlyHomotopic f₀ f₁) (H : Subgroup Γ) :
110     ContinuousMap.Homotopic (f₀.fixedPointsMap H) (f₁.fixedPointsMap H) :=
111   h.elim (fun F => ⟨F.onFixedPoints H⟩)
112 end EquivariantlyHomotopic
113 end BC4lean.ProperActions

EquivariantHomotopyEquiv.lean

源码 SHA-256：7994a431988bbe1f489665e11f76bac2d711bc726de2bc079e102cb13a567a96

  1 import BC4lean.EquivariantHomotopy
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.Homotopy.Equiv
  3 
  4 /-! # Equivariant homotopy equivalences induce equivalences on fixed-point spaces -/
  5 noncomputable section
  6 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
  7 
  8 /-- Equivariant maps inverse up to jointly continuous equivariant homotopy. -/
  9 structure EquivariantHomotopyEquiv (Γ X Y : Type*) [Group Γ]
 10     [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y] where
 11   toMap : EquivariantMap Γ X Y
 12   invMap : EquivariantMap Γ Y X
 13   left_inv : EquivariantlyHomotopic (invMap.comp toMap) EquivariantMap.id
 14   right_inv : EquivariantlyHomotopic (toMap.comp invMap) EquivariantMap.id
 15 
 16 namespace EquivariantHomotopyEquiv
 17 variable {Γ X Y Z : Type*} [Group Γ]
 18 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] [TopologicalSpace Z]
 19 variable [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y] [MulAction Γ Z]
 20 
 21 /-- The identity equivariant homotopy equivalence. -/
 22 def refl : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X X where
 23   toMap := EquivariantMap.id
 24   invMap := EquivariantMap.id
 25   left_inv := EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl _
 26   right_inv := EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl _
 27 
 28 /-- Reverse an equivariant homotopy equivalence. -/
 29 def symm (e : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X Y) : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ Y X where
 30   toMap := e.invMap
 31   invMap := e.toMap
 32   left_inv := e.right_inv
 33   right_inv := e.left_inv
 34 
 35 /-- Compose equivariant homotopy equivalences. -/
 36 def trans (e : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X Y) (d : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ Y Z) :
 37     EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X Z where
 38   toMap := d.toMap.comp e.toMap
 39   invMap := e.invMap.comp d.invMap
 40   left_inv := by
 41     have h := (EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl e.invMap).comp
 42       (d.left_inv.comp (EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl e.toMap))
 43     simp only [EquivariantMap.comp_assoc, EquivariantMap.id_comp] at h
 44     exact h.trans e.left_inv
 45   right_inv := by
 46     have h := (EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl d.toMap).comp
 47       (e.right_inv.comp (EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl d.invMap))
 48     simp only [EquivariantMap.comp_assoc, EquivariantMap.id_comp] at h
 49     exact h.trans d.right_inv
 50 
 51 /-- Taking H-fixed points preserves equivariant homotopy equivalences. -/
 52 def fixedPoints (e : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X Y) (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 53     ContinuousMap.HomotopyEquiv (FixedPointSpace H X) (FixedPointSpace H Y) where
 54   toFun := e.toMap.fixedPointsMap H
 55   invFun := e.invMap.fixedPointsMap H
 56   left_inv := by
 57     simpa only [EquivariantMap.fixedPointsMap_comp, EquivariantMap.fixedPointsMap_id]
 58       using e.left_inv.onFixedPoints H
 59   right_inv := by
 60     simpa only [EquivariantMap.fixedPointsMap_comp, EquivariantMap.fixedPointsMap_id]
 61       using e.right_inv.onFixedPoints H
 62 
 63 @[simp] theorem fixedPoints_toFun_apply (e : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X Y) (H : Subgroup Γ)
 64     (x : FixedPointSpace H X) : ((e.fixedPoints H).toFun x : Y) = e.toMap x := rfl
 65 
 66 @[simp] theorem fixedPoints_invFun_apply (e : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X Y) (H : Subgroup Γ)
 67     (y : FixedPointSpace H Y) : ((e.fixedPoints H).invFun y : X) = e.invMap y := rfl
 68 
 69 end EquivariantHomotopyEquiv
 70 end BC4lean.ProperActions

OrbitFixedPoints.lean

源码 SHA-256：89b1af04865bdc9ef351dba80008ef0c7eb4109eddab8e4e44bbb1ed886ed905

  1 import BC4lean.EquivariantMaps
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.Quotient
  3 
  4 /-! # Maps from homogeneous orbits
  5 
  6 Evaluation at the identity coset identifies continuous equivariant maps out of
  7 `Γ ⧸ H` with `H`-fixed points. No normality or finiteness hypothesis on `H` is used.
  8 -/
  9 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 10 
 11 variable {Γ X Y : Type*} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ]
 12 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X]
 13 variable [TopologicalSpace Y] [MulAction Γ Y]
 14 
 15 omit [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ] in
 16 /-- The identity coset is fixed by the subgroup defining the orbit. -/
 17 theorem subgroup_fixes_identity_coset (H : Subgroup Γ) (h : H) :
 18     (h : Γ) • (QuotientGroup.mk 1 : Γ ⧸ H) = QuotientGroup.mk 1 := by
 19   change QuotientGroup.mk ((h : Γ) * 1) = (QuotientGroup.mk 1 : Γ ⧸ H)
 20   rw [mul_one, QuotientGroup.eq]
 21   simpa only [mul_one] using H.inv_mem h.property
 22 
 23 /-- Descend `g ↦ g • x` to the orbit when `x` is `H`-fixed. -/
 24 def orbitMap (H : Subgroup Γ) (x : FixedPointSpace H X) : EquivariantMap Γ (Γ ⧸ H) X where
 25   toFun := Quotient.lift (fun g : Γ => g • (x : X)) (by
 26     intro a b hab
 27     have hab' : a⁻¹ * b ∈ H := QuotientGroup.leftRel_apply.mp hab
 28     have hx := x.property ⟨a⁻¹ * b, hab'⟩
 29     change (a⁻¹ * b) • (x : X) = x at hx
 30     have := congrArg (fun z : X => a • z) hx
 31     simpa [mul_smul] using this.symm)
 32   continuous_toFun := by
 33     let : DiscreteTopology (Γ ⧸ H) := QuotientGroup.discreteTopology (isOpen_discrete _)
 34     exact continuous_of_discreteTopology
 35   map_smul' g q := by
 36     induction q using QuotientGroup.induction_on with
 37     | H a => exact mul_smul g a (x : X)
 38 
 39 @[simp] theorem orbitMap_mk (H : Subgroup Γ) (x : FixedPointSpace H X) (g : Γ) :
 40     orbitMap H x (QuotientGroup.mk g) = g • (x : X) := rfl
 41 
 42 /-- Evaluation at the identity coset has values in the fixed-point subspace. -/
 43 def orbitEvaluation (H : Subgroup Γ) (f : EquivariantMap Γ (Γ ⧸ H) X) :
 44     FixedPointSpace H X :=
 45   ⟨f (QuotientGroup.mk 1), fun h => by
 46     change (h : Γ) • f (QuotientGroup.mk 1) = f (QuotientGroup.mk 1)
 47     rw [← f.map_smul, subgroup_fixes_identity_coset]⟩
 48 
 49 omit [DiscreteTopology Γ] in
 50 @[simp] theorem orbitEvaluation_val (H : Subgroup Γ) (f : EquivariantMap Γ (Γ ⧸ H) X) :
 51     (orbitEvaluation H f : X) = f (QuotientGroup.mk 1) := rfl
 52 
 53 /-- The orbit/fixed-point correspondence for continuous equivariant maps. -/
 54 def orbitFixedPointEquiv (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 55     EquivariantMap Γ (Γ ⧸ H) X ≃ FixedPointSpace H X where
 56   toFun := orbitEvaluation H
 57   invFun := orbitMap H
 58   left_inv f := by
 59     ext q
 60     induction q using QuotientGroup.induction_on with
 61     | H g =>
 62       change g • f (QuotientGroup.mk 1) = f (QuotientGroup.mk g)
 63       rw [← f.map_smul]
 64       congr 1
 65       change QuotientGroup.mk (g * 1) = (QuotientGroup.mk g : Γ ⧸ H)
 66       rw [mul_one]
 67   right_inv x := by
 68     apply Subtype.ext
 69     exact one_smul Γ (x : X)
 70 
 71 /-- The correspondence commutes with postcomposition. -/
 72 theorem orbitFixedPointEquiv_natural (H : Subgroup Γ)
 73     (f : EquivariantMap Γ (Γ ⧸ H) X) (g : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) :
 74     orbitFixedPointEquiv H (g.comp f) = g.fixedPointsMap H (orbitFixedPointEquiv H f) := rfl
 75 
 76 end BC4lean.ProperActions

ProperOrbitCells.lean

源码 SHA-256：829ce4e3bd4da88a2f66af0f06a97a46f7fce18601157fd56a03a146b41407b1

  1 import BC4lean.OrbitFixedPoints
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.AlexandrovDiscrete
  3 
  4 /-! # Proper homogeneous orbits and orbit cells -/
  5 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
  6 open scoped Pointwise
  7 
  8 variable {Γ X Y : Type*} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ]
  9 
 10 /-- For a finite subgroup of a discrete group, the coset projection is proper. -/
 11 theorem proper_cosetProjection (H : Subgroup Γ) [Finite H] :
 12     IsProperMap (QuotientGroup.mk : Γ → Γ ⧸ H) := by
 13   let : DiscreteTopology (Γ ⧸ H) := QuotientGroup.discreteTopology (isOpen_discrete _)
 14   apply isProperMap_iff_isClosedMap_and_compact_fibers.mpr
 15   refine ⟨continuous_of_discreteTopology, fun _ _ => isClosed_discrete _, ?_⟩
 16   intro q
 17   induction q using QuotientGroup.induction_on with | H g =>
 18     apply Set.Finite.isCompact
 19     apply (Set.finite_range (fun h : H => g * (h : Γ))).subset
 20     intro a ha
 21     have hga : g⁻¹ * a ∈ H := QuotientGroup.eq.mp (Set.mem_singleton_iff.mp ha).symm
 22     exact ⟨⟨g⁻¹ * a, hga⟩, mul_inv_cancel_left g a⟩
 23 
 24 /-- A homogeneous orbit with finite stabilizer is a proper discrete-group space. -/
 25 theorem proper_orbit (H : Subgroup Γ) [Finite H] : ProperSMul Γ (Γ ⧸ H) := by
 26   apply MulAction.properSMul_of_proper_orbitMap (x := (QuotientGroup.mk 1 : Γ ⧸ H))
 27   simpa only [MulAction.Quotient.smul_mk, smul_eq_mul, mul_one] using proper_cosetProjection H
 28 
 29 /-- Properness of the homogeneous orbit is equivalent to finiteness of its stabilizer. -/
 30 theorem proper_orbit_iff (H : Subgroup Γ) : ProperSMul Γ (Γ ⧸ H) ↔ Finite H := by
 31   constructor
 32   · intro h
 33     let := h
 34     exact (finite_subgroup_of_fixedPoint (H := H)
 35       (x := (QuotientGroup.mk 1 : Γ ⧸ H)) (subgroup_fixes_identity_coset H)).to_subtype
 36   · intro h
 37     let := h
 38     exact proper_orbit H
 39 
 40 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X] [ContinuousSMul Γ X]
 41 variable [LocallyCompactSpace X] [T2Space X]
 42 variable [TopologicalSpace Y] [MulAction Γ Y] [ProperSMul Γ Y]
 43 
 44 /-- An equivariant map to a proper space forces properness of an LCH source. -/
 45 theorem proper_of_equivariantMap (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) : ProperSMul Γ X := by
 46   apply proper_iff_finite_transporters.mpr
 47   intro K L hK hL
 48   apply (finite_transporter (Γ := Γ) (hK.image f.continuous) (hL.image f.continuous)).subset
 49   intro g hg
 50   obtain ⟨y, hy, hyL⟩ := hg
 51   obtain ⟨x, hx, hxy⟩ := hy
 52   refine ⟨f y, ?_, ⟨y, hyL, rfl⟩⟩
 53   refine ⟨f x, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩, ?_⟩
 54   change g • f x = f y
 55   exact (f.map_smul g x).symm.trans (congrArg f hxy)
 56 
 57 /-- An orbit cell, with the group acting only on its homogeneous-orbit factor.
 58 Taking `D` to be a disk gives the cells used in equivariant CW constructions. -/
 59 def OrbitCell (H : Subgroup Γ) (D : Type*) := (Γ ⧸ H) × D
 60 
 61 namespace OrbitCell
 62 variable (H : Subgroup Γ) (D : Type*) [TopologicalSpace D]
 63 
 64 instance instTopologicalSpace : TopologicalSpace (OrbitCell H D) :=
 65   inferInstanceAs (TopologicalSpace ((Γ ⧸ H) × D))
 66 
 67 instance instMulAction : MulAction Γ (OrbitCell H D) where
 68   smul g p := (g • p.1, p.2)
 69   one_smul p := Prod.ext (one_smul Γ p.1) rfl
 70   mul_smul g h p := Prod.ext (mul_smul g h p.1) rfl
 71 
 72 instance instContinuousSMul : ContinuousSMul Γ (OrbitCell H D) where
 73   continuous_smul :=
 74     (continuous_fst.smul continuous_snd.fst).prodMk continuous_snd.snd
 75 
 76 instance instLocallyCompactSpace [LocallyCompactSpace D] :
 77     LocallyCompactSpace (OrbitCell H D) := by
 78   let : DiscreteTopology (Γ ⧸ H) := QuotientGroup.discreteTopology (isOpen_discrete _)
 79   exact inferInstanceAs (LocallyCompactSpace ((Γ ⧸ H) × D))
 80 
 81 instance instT2Space [T2Space D] : T2Space (OrbitCell H D) := by
 82   let : DiscreteTopology (Γ ⧸ H) := QuotientGroup.discreteTopology (isOpen_discrete _)
 83   exact inferInstanceAs (T2Space ((Γ ⧸ H) × D))
 84 
 85 /-- Projection onto the homogeneous-orbit factor. -/
 86 def projection : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H D) (Γ ⧸ H) where
 87   toFun p := p.1
 88   continuous_toFun := continuous_fst
 89   map_smul' _ _ := rfl
 90 
 91 /-- Orbit cells with finite stabilizer and LCH parameter are proper. -/
 92 theorem proper [Finite H] [LocallyCompactSpace D] [T2Space D] :
 93     ProperSMul Γ (OrbitCell H D) := by
 94   let := proper_orbit H
 95   exact proper_of_equivariantMap (projection H D)
 96 
 97 end OrbitCell
 98 end BC4lean.ProperActions

HomotopyTerminal.lean

源码 SHA-256：051f80906db2d1159c31d709be2ca798bd3f15d88a3336e46216a46743f3386b

  1 import BC4lean.EquivariantHomotopyEquiv
  2 
  3 /-! # Uniqueness from a homotopy-universal mapping property
  4 
  5 These lemmas isolate the uniqueness argument. They do not assert that a space
  6 with this mapping property exists, or derive it from fixed-point contractibility.
  7 -/
  8 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
  9 
 10 variable {Γ X Y Z : Type*} [Group Γ]
 11 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] [TopologicalSpace Z]
 12 variable [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y] [MulAction Γ Z]
 13 
 14 /-- Maps from a specified source exist and are unique up to equivariant homotopy. -/
 15 def HomotopyTerminalFor (Γ X Y : Type*) [Group Γ]
 16     [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y] : Prop :=
 17   Nonempty (EquivariantMap Γ X Y) ∧
 18     ∀ f g : EquivariantMap Γ X Y, EquivariantlyHomotopic f g
 19 
 20 /-- The universal-property uniqueness argument, with its four premises explicit. -/
 21 theorem homotopyTerminal_unique
 22     (hXY : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ X Y) (hYX : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ Y X)
 23     (hXX : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ X X) (hYY : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ Y Y) :
 24     Nonempty (EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X Y) := by
 25   obtain ⟨f⟩ := hXY.1
 26   obtain ⟨g⟩ := hYX.1
 27   exact ⟨⟨f, g, hXX.2 _ _, hYY.2 _ _⟩⟩
 28 
 29 /-- Homotopy-terminal targets can be replaced by equivariantly homotopy-equivalent targets. -/
 30 theorem HomotopyTerminalFor.of_equiv (h : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ X Y)
 31     (e : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ Y Z) : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ X Z := by
 32   constructor
 33   · obtain ⟨f⟩ := h.1
 34     exact ⟨e.toMap.comp f⟩
 35   · intro f g
 36     have hf : EquivariantlyHomotopic (e.toMap.comp (e.invMap.comp f)) f := by
 37       simpa only [← EquivariantMap.comp_assoc, EquivariantMap.id_comp]
 38         using e.right_inv.comp (EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl f)
 39     have hg : EquivariantlyHomotopic (e.toMap.comp (e.invMap.comp g)) g := by
 40       simpa only [← EquivariantMap.comp_assoc, EquivariantMap.id_comp]
 41         using e.right_inv.comp (EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl g)
 42     exact hf.symm.trans (((EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl e.toMap).comp
 43       (h.2 (e.invMap.comp f) (e.invMap.comp g))).trans hg)
 44 
 45 /-- Uniqueness also identifies all subgroup fixed-point homotopy types. -/
 46 theorem homotopyTerminal_fixedPoints_unique
 47     (hXY : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ X Y) (hYX : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ Y X)
 48     (hXX : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ X X) (hYY : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ Y Y)
 49     (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 50     Nonempty (ContinuousMap.HomotopyEquiv (FixedPointSpace H X) (FixedPointSpace H Y)) := by
 51   obtain ⟨e⟩ := homotopyTerminal_unique hXY hYX hXX hYY
 52   exact ⟨e.fixedPoints H⟩
 53 
 54 end BC4lean.ProperActions

FixedPointCriterion.lean

源码 SHA-256：42ed97a4a4e236029d4ae40ab5b9d74ddb5cd35e6d7000971085773701577843

  1 import BC4lean.HomotopyTerminal
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.Homotopy.Contractible
  3 
  4 /-! # The fixed-point criterion and the finite-group point model
  5 
  6 This predicate records the fixed-point condition on a prospective model. It is
  7 not a definition of an equivariant CW complex, nor a proof of the general model
  8 existence or of the cellular universal mapping theorem.
  9 -/
 10 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 11 
 12 /-- The fixed-point condition expected of a universal proper model. -/
 13 def FixedPointCriterion (Γ X : Type*) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X] : Prop :=
 14   (∀ H : Subgroup Γ, Finite H → ContractibleSpace (FixedPointSpace H X)) ∧
 15   (∀ H : Subgroup Γ, Infinite H → IsEmpty (FixedPointSpace H X))
 16 
 17 variable {Γ X Y : Type*} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y]
 18 variable [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y]
 19 
 20 /-- The fixed-point criterion is invariant under equivariant homotopy equivalence. -/
 21 theorem FixedPointCriterion.of_equiv (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X)
 22     (e : EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ X Y) : FixedPointCriterion Γ Y := by
 23   constructor
 24   · intro H hH
 25     exact (e.fixedPoints H).contractibleSpace_iff.mp (h.1 H hH)
 26   · intro H hH
 27     let := h.2 H hH
 28     exact ⟨fun y => isEmptyElim (e.invMap.fixedPointsMap H y)⟩
 29 
 30 /-- A one-point space carrying the trivial action. -/
 31 def Point := Unit
 32 
 33 namespace Point
 34 instance instUnique : Unique Point := inferInstanceAs (Unique Unit)
 35 instance instTopologicalSpace : TopologicalSpace Point := ⊥
 36 instance instDiscreteTopology : DiscreteTopology Point := ⟨rfl⟩
 37 instance instMulAction : MulAction Γ Point where
 38   smul _ x := x
 39   one_smul _ := rfl
 40   mul_smul _ _ _ := rfl
 41 
 42 /-- Every fixed-point space of the trivial one-point action is contractible. -/
 43 theorem fixedPoints_contractible (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 44     ContractibleSpace (FixedPointSpace H Point) := by
 45   let : Nonempty (FixedPointSpace H Point) := ⟨⟨default, fun _ => rfl⟩⟩
 46   infer_instance
 47 
 48 /-- For finite groups the point satisfies the full fixed-point criterion. -/
 49 theorem fixedPointCriterion [Finite Γ] : FixedPointCriterion Γ Point := by
 50   refine ⟨fun H _ => fixedPoints_contractible H, ?_⟩
 51   intro H hH
 52   let := hH
 53   exact False.elim (not_finite H)
 54 
 55 /-- The point has the required mapping property for every source. -/
 56 theorem homotopyTerminal (Γ X : Type*) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X] :
 57     HomotopyTerminalFor Γ X Point := by
 58   constructor
 59   · exact ⟨{ toFun := fun _ => default, continuous_toFun := continuous_const,
 60              map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }⟩
 61   · intro f g
 62     have : f = g := EquivariantMap.ext (fun _ => Subsingleton.elim _ _)
 63     subst g
 64     exact EquivariantlyHomotopic.refl f
 65 
 66 /-- The one-point action of a finite discrete group is proper. -/
 67 theorem proper [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ] [Finite Γ] :
 68     ProperSMul Γ Point := by
 69   let : ContinuousSMul Γ Point := ⟨continuous_of_discreteTopology⟩
 70   exact proper_of_finite
 71 
 72 end Point
 73 end BC4lean.ProperActions

CocompactPieces.lean

源码 SHA-256：abd7fa7802d96e2e4dcdfd85cb4ed2d2d4ccd0d0ba26058b403173277aed32c9

  1 import BC4lean.EquivariantMaps
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.Maps.OpenQuotient
  3 
  4 /-! # Closed invariant cocompact pieces of a given proper space
  5 
  6 The indexing construction below is conditional on the ambient space. It does
  7 not construct a universal proper space or select a locally compact model.
  8 -/
  9 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 10 open Topology
 11 
 12 variable {Γ X : Type*} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X]
 13 
 14 /-- An invariant subset with the induced topology and action. -/
 15 structure InvariantSubset (Γ X : Type*) [Group Γ] [MulAction Γ X] where
 16   carrier : Set X
 17   smul_mem : ∀ (g : Γ) {x : X}, x ∈ carrier → g • x ∈ carrier
 18 
 19 namespace InvariantSubset
 20 instance instSetLike : SetLike (InvariantSubset Γ X) X where
 21   coe := carrier
 22   coe_injective := by rintro ⟨s, hs⟩ ⟨t, ht⟩ rfl; rfl
 23 
 24 instance instPartialOrder : PartialOrder (InvariantSubset Γ X) := .ofSetLike _ _
 25 
 26 instance instMulAction (s : InvariantSubset Γ X) : MulAction Γ s where
 27   smul g x := ⟨g • x.val, s.smul_mem g x.property⟩
 28   one_smul x := Subtype.ext (one_smul Γ x.val)
 29   mul_smul g h x := Subtype.ext (mul_smul g h x.val)
 30 
 31 omit [TopologicalSpace X] in
 32 @[simp] theorem coe_smul (s : InvariantSubset Γ X) (g : Γ) (x : s) :
 33     ((g • x : s) : X) = g • (x : X) := rfl
 34 
 35 instance instContinuousConstSMul (s : InvariantSubset Γ X) [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] :
 36     ContinuousConstSMul Γ s where
 37   continuous_const_smul g := (continuous_const_smul g).subtype_map (fun _ hx => s.smul_mem g hx)
 38 
 39 /-- Inclusion of an invariant subset is equivariant. -/
 40 def inclusion (s : InvariantSubset Γ X) : EquivariantMap Γ s X where
 41   toFun := Subtype.val
 42   continuous_toFun := continuous_subtype_val
 43   map_smul' _ _ := rfl
 44 
 45 /-- The map from the intrinsic orbit space to the ambient orbit space. -/
 46 def orbitInclusion (s : InvariantSubset Γ X) :
 47     Quotient (MulAction.orbitRel Γ s) → Quotient (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) :=
 48   Quotient.map Subtype.val (by
 49     intro a b h
 50     obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := h
 51     exact ⟨g, congrArg Subtype.val hg⟩)
 52 
 53 omit [TopologicalSpace X] in
 54 theorem orbitInclusion_injective (s : InvariantSubset Γ X) :
 55     Function.Injective s.orbitInclusion := by
 56   intro a b
 57   induction a using Quotient.inductionOn with | _ a =>
 58     induction b using Quotient.inductionOn with | _ b =>
 59       intro h
 60       apply Quotient.sound
 61       obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := Quotient.exact h
 62       exact ⟨g, Subtype.ext hg⟩
 63 
 64 /-- An invariant subspace's intrinsic orbit topology is its ambient subspace topology. -/
 65 theorem orbitInclusion_isEmbedding (s : InvariantSubset Γ X)
 66     [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] : IsEmbedding s.orbitInclusion := by
 67   apply isEmbedding_of_isOpenQuotientMap_of_isInducing
 68     (Subtype.val : s → X) s.orbitInclusion
 69     (Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ s)) (Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X))
 70     rfl IsInducing.subtypeVal isQuotientMap_quot_mk
 71     MulAction.isOpenQuotientMap_quotientMk s.orbitInclusion_injective
 72   rintro x ⟨_, ⟨y, rfl⟩, h⟩
 73   obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := Quotient.exact h.symm
 74   exact ⟨⟨x, hg ▸ s.smul_mem g y.property⟩, rfl⟩
 75 
 76 omit [TopologicalSpace X] in
 77 theorem range_orbitInclusion (s : InvariantSubset Γ X) :
 78     Set.range s.orbitInclusion = Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) '' (s : Set X) := by
 79   ext q
 80   constructor
 81   · rintro ⟨a, rfl⟩
 82     induction a using Quotient.inductionOn with | _ a => exact ⟨a, a.property, rfl⟩
 83   · rintro ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
 84     exact ⟨Quotient.mk _ ⟨x, hx⟩, rfl⟩
 85 
 86 /-- Compactness in the ambient quotient is equivalent to compactness of the intrinsic quotient. -/
 87 theorem compactSpace_orbit_iff (s : InvariantSubset Γ X) [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] :
 88     CompactSpace (Quotient (MulAction.orbitRel Γ s)) ↔
 89       IsCompact (Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) '' (s : Set X)) := by
 90   rw [← s.range_orbitInclusion, ← Set.image_univ,
 91     ← s.orbitInclusion_isEmbedding.isCompact_iff, isCompact_univ_iff]
 92 
 93 end InvariantSubset
 94 
 95 /-- Closed invariant subsets with compact quotient, encoded through the ambient orbit space. -/
 96 structure CocompactPiece (Γ X : Type*) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X]
 97     extends InvariantSubset Γ X where
 98   isClosed_carrier : IsClosed carrier
 99   isCompact_image : IsCompact (Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) '' carrier)
100 
101 namespace CocompactPiece
102 instance instSetLike : SetLike (CocompactPiece Γ X) X where
103   coe s := s.carrier
104   coe_injective := by rintro ⟨⟨s, hs⟩, hc, hk⟩ ⟨⟨t, ht⟩, hd, hl⟩ rfl; rfl
105 instance instPartialOrder : PartialOrder (CocompactPiece Γ X) := .ofSetLike _ _
106 
107 /-- The empty piece ensures that the indexing type is nonempty. -/
108 def empty : CocompactPiece Γ X where
109   carrier := ∅
110   smul_mem _ _ hx := False.elim hx
111   isClosed_carrier := isClosed_empty
112   isCompact_image := by simp
113 
114 /-- Finite unions provide upper bounds in the cocompact indexing system. -/
115 def union (s t : CocompactPiece Γ X) : CocompactPiece Γ X where
116   carrier := s.carrier ∪ t.carrier
117   smul_mem g _ hx := hx.elim (fun h => Or.inl (s.smul_mem g h))
118     (fun h => Or.inr (t.smul_mem g h))
119   isClosed_carrier := s.isClosed_carrier.union t.isClosed_carrier
120   isCompact_image := by
121     rw [Set.image_union]
122     exact s.isCompact_image.union t.isCompact_image
123 
124 theorem le_union_left (s t : CocompactPiece Γ X) : s ≤ s.union t := Set.subset_union_left
125 theorem le_union_right (s t : CocompactPiece Γ X) : t ≤ s.union t := Set.subset_union_right
126 
127 theorem directed : Directed (· ≤ · : CocompactPiece Γ X → CocompactPiece Γ X → Prop)
128     (fun s => s) := fun s t => ⟨s.union t, le_union_left s t, le_union_right s t⟩
129 
130 /-- Every piece has compact intrinsic orbit space. -/
131 theorem compactSpace_orbit (s : CocompactPiece Γ X) [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] :
132     CompactSpace (Quotient (MulAction.orbitRel Γ s.toInvariantSubset)) :=
133   s.toInvariantSubset.compactSpace_orbit_iff.mpr s.isCompact_image
134 
135 /-- Closed pieces of a locally compact ambient space are locally compact. -/
136 theorem locallyCompact (s : CocompactPiece Γ X) [LocallyCompactSpace X] :
137     LocallyCompactSpace s.toInvariantSubset := s.isClosed_carrier.locallyCompactSpace
138 
139 /-- Compact subsets generate closed cocompact invariant pieces in a proper space. -/
140 def ofCompact [TopologicalSpace Γ] [ProperSMul Γ X]
141     (K : Set X) (hK : IsCompact K) : CocompactPiece Γ X where
142   carrier := Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) ⁻¹'
143     (Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) '' K)
144   smul_mem g x hx := by
145     change Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) (g • x) ∈
146       Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) '' K
147     have he (x : X) : Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) (g • x) =
148         Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X) x := Quotient.sound ⟨g, rfl⟩
149     rw [he]
150     exact hx
151   isClosed_carrier := (hK.image (show Continuous (Quotient.mk (MulAction.orbitRel Γ X)) from continuous_quot_mk)).isClosed.preimage continuous_quot_mk
152   isCompact_image := by
153     rw [Set.image_preimage_eq _ Quotient.mk_surjective]
154     exact hK.image continuous_quot_mk
155 
156 theorem subset_ofCompact [TopologicalSpace Γ] [ProperSMul Γ X]
157     (K : Set X) (hK : IsCompact K) : K ⊆ (ofCompact (Γ := Γ) K hK : Set X) :=
158   fun x hx => ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
159 
160 /-- Every point belongs to a closed cocompact invariant piece. -/
161 theorem covers [TopologicalSpace Γ] [ProperSMul Γ X] (x : X) :
162     ∃ s : CocompactPiece Γ X, x ∈ s :=
163   ⟨ofCompact {x} isCompact_singleton,
164     subset_ofCompact (Γ := Γ) {x} isCompact_singleton (Set.mem_singleton x)⟩
165 
166 end CocompactPiece
167 end BC4lean.ProperActions

EquivariantCW.lean

源码 SHA-256：f40e168898fb1e020c96942dc5543ccd8c969cc3be6ced729d59f8eea8b88030

  1 import BC4lean.ProperOrbitCells
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.CWComplex.Abstract.Basic
  3 import Mathlib.CategoryTheory.Action.Limits
  4 
  5 /-! # Equivariant cell attachments
  6 
  7 This module realizes the orbit boundary inclusions in `Action TopCat Γ`
  8 and specializes Mathlib's relative cell complex structure to them. The cells
  9 have finite isotropy. Properness of arbitrary cellular colimits and the
 10 universal proper-space existence and mapping theorems are separate results;
 11 they are not fields or axioms of the definitions below.
 12 -/
 13 
 14 open CategoryTheory CategoryTheory.Limits
 15 
 16 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 17 
 18 universe u v
 19 
 20 /-- Bundle an action with continuous translations as an action in `TopCat`. -/
 21 def topologicalAction (Γ : Type u) (X : Type v) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace X]
 22     [MulAction Γ X] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] : Action TopCat.{v} Γ where
 23   V := TopCat.of X
 24   ρ :=
 25     { toFun := fun g => TopCat.ofHom ⟨fun x => g • x, continuous_const_smul g⟩
 26       map_one' := by
 27         apply TopCat.hom_ext
 28         apply ContinuousMap.ext
 29         intro x
 30         exact one_smul Γ x
 31       map_mul' := by
 32         intro g h
 33         apply TopCat.hom_ext
 34         apply ContinuousMap.ext
 35         intro x
 36         exact mul_smul g h x }
 37 
 38 namespace EquivariantMap
 39 
 40 variable {Γ : Type u} {X Y Z : Type v} [Group Γ]
 41 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [TopologicalSpace Y] [TopologicalSpace Z]
 42 variable [MulAction Γ X] [MulAction Γ Y] [MulAction Γ Z]
 43 variable [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ Y] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ Z]
 44 
 45 /-- A bundled continuous equivariant map gives a morphism of topological actions. -/
 46 def toActionHom (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) :
 47     topologicalAction Γ X ⟶ topologicalAction Γ Y where
 48   hom := TopCat.ofHom f.toContinuousMap
 49   comm g := by ext x; exact f.map_smul g x
 50 
 51 @[simp] theorem toActionHom_apply (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) (x : X) :
 52     f.toActionHom.hom x = f x := rfl
 53 
 54 @[simp] theorem toActionHom_id :
 55     (id (Γ := Γ) (X := X)).toActionHom = 𝟙 (topologicalAction Γ X) := rfl
 56 
 57 @[simp] theorem toActionHom_comp (g : EquivariantMap Γ Y Z) (f : EquivariantMap Γ X Y) :
 58     (g.comp f).toActionHom = f.toActionHom ≫ g.toActionHom := rfl
 59 
 60 /-- Recover the pointwise equivariant-map API from a categorical morphism. -/
 61 def ofActionHom (f : topologicalAction Γ X ⟶ topologicalAction Γ Y) :
 62     EquivariantMap Γ X Y where
 63   toContinuousMap := f.hom.hom
 64   map_smul' g x := congrArg (fun k : TopCat.of X ⟶ TopCat.of Y => k x) (f.comm g)
 65 
 66 /-- The categorical and pointwise descriptions have exactly the same morphisms. -/
 67 def actionHomEquiv :
 68     EquivariantMap Γ X Y ≃ (topologicalAction Γ X ⟶ topologicalAction Γ Y) where
 69   toFun := toActionHom
 70   invFun := ofActionHom
 71   left_inv f := by ext x; rfl
 72   right_inv f := by apply Action.Hom.ext; rfl
 73 
 74 end EquivariantMap
 75 
 76 variable {Γ : Type u} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ]
 77 
 78 namespace OrbitCell
 79 
 80 /-- Map the parameter of an orbit cell, leaving its coset coordinate unchanged. -/
 81 def map {D E : Type v} [TopologicalSpace D] [TopologicalSpace E]
 82     (H : Subgroup Γ) (f : C(D, E)) : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H D) (OrbitCell H E) where
 83   toFun p := (p.1, f p.2)
 84   continuous_toFun := continuous_fst.prodMk (f.continuous.comp continuous_snd)
 85   map_smul' _ _ := rfl
 86 
 87 omit [DiscreteTopology Γ] in
 88 @[simp] theorem map_apply {D E : Type v} [TopologicalSpace D] [TopologicalSpace E]
 89     (H : Subgroup Γ) (f : C(D, E)) (p : OrbitCell H D) :
 90     map H f p = (p.1, f p.2) := rfl
 91 
 92 /-- The orbit boundary inclusion Γ/H × ∂Dⁿ → Γ/H × Dⁿ. -/
 93 noncomputable def boundaryInclusion (H : Subgroup Γ) (n : ℕ) :
 94     EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n))
 95       (OrbitCell H (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) :=
 96   map H (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n).hom
 97 
 98 omit [DiscreteTopology Γ] in
 99 /-- Orbit boundary inclusions are injective, including in dimension zero. -/
100 theorem boundaryInclusion_injective (H : Subgroup Γ) (n : ℕ) :
101     Function.Injective (boundaryInclusion H n) := by
102   intro x y h
103   change (x.1, (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n) x.2) =
104     (y.1, (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n) y.2) at h
105   apply Prod.ext
106   · exact congrArg (fun p : (Γ ⧸ H) × TopCat.disk.{u} n => p.1) h
107   · exact (TopCat.mono_iff_injective (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n)).mp
108       inferInstance (congrArg Prod.snd h)
109 
110 /-- The disk orbit cell with finite isotropy is topologically proper. -/
111 theorem disk_proper (H : Subgroup Γ) [Finite H] (n : ℕ) :
112     ProperSMul Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) := by
113   let : T2Space (TopCat.disk.{u} n) := by
114     change T2Space (ULift (Metric.closedBall (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) 1))
115     exact Homeomorph.ulift.{u, 0}.symm.t2Space
116   exact proper H _
117 
118 /-- The boundary orbit cell with finite isotropy is topologically proper. -/
119 theorem boundary_proper (H : Subgroup Γ) [Finite H] (n : ℕ) :
120     ProperSMul Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n)) := by
121   let : T2Space (TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n) := by
122     change T2Space (ULift (Metric.sphere (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) 1))
123     exact Homeomorph.ulift.{u, 0}.symm.t2Space
124   exact proper H _
125 
126 end OrbitCell
127 
128 /-- Finite isotropy subgroups indexing the allowed orbit cells. -/
129 def FiniteIsotropy (Γ : Type u) [Group Γ] := {H : Subgroup Γ // Finite H}
130 
131 /-- Generating boundary maps, with dimension indexed by the skeletal stage. -/
132 noncomputable def equivariantBasicCell (n : ℕ) (H : FiniteIsotropy Γ) :
133     topologicalAction Γ (OrbitCell H.val (TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n)) ⟶
134       topologicalAction Γ (OrbitCell H.val (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) :=
135   (OrbitCell.boundaryInclusion H.val n).toActionHom
136 
137 /-- Relative equivariant CW data, attaching n-dimensional finite-isotropy orbit
138 cells at stage n. This definition includes actual pushout and colimit witnesses. -/
139 abbrev RelativeEquivariantCWComplex {X Y : Action TopCat.{u} Γ} (f : X ⟶ Y) :=
140   HomotopicalAlgebra.RelativeCellComplex.{u} (equivariantBasicCell (Γ := Γ)) f
141 
142 /-- An equivariant CW structure starting from the empty action. Finite isotropy
143 is imposed on the cell family, not assumed as a properness conclusion. -/
144 abbrev EquivariantCWComplex (X : Action TopCat.{u} Γ) :=
145   RelativeEquivariantCWComplex (initial.to X)
146 
147 /-- Relative equivariant CW structures include an attachment at every finite stage. -/
148 def RelativeEquivariantCWComplex.attachment {X Y : Action TopCat.{u} Γ} {f : X ⟶ Y}
149     (c : RelativeEquivariantCWComplex f) (n : ℕ) :=
150   c.attachCells n (not_isMax n)
151 
152 /-- Maps out of a cellular object are determined on the initial subspace and cells. -/
153 theorem RelativeEquivariantCWComplex.hom_ext
154     {X Y Z : Action TopCat.{u} Γ} {f : X ⟶ Y}
155     (c : RelativeEquivariantCWComplex f) {φ ψ : Y ⟶ Z}
156     (h₀ : f ≫ φ = f ≫ ψ)
157     (h : ∀ γ : HomotopicalAlgebra.RelativeCellComplex.Cells c, γ.ι ≫ φ = γ.ι ≫ ψ) :
158     φ = ψ :=
159   HomotopicalAlgebra.RelativeCellComplex.hom_ext c h₀ h
160 
161 end BC4lean.ProperActions

OrbitCellMaps.lean

源码 SHA-256：4cb73c5c98de04a4e67abc1b32d8fbd511f8e27e615373a165014cdc4324f58d

  1 import BC4lean.EquivariantCW
  2 
  3 /-! # Maps from orbit cells and fixed-point extension problems
  4 
  5 Continuous equivariant maps Γ/H × D → X are continuous maps D → X^H.
  6 Consequently extension across a parameter map is exactly an ordinary extension
  7 problem in the fixed-point space. This does not assume or prove that those
  8 ordinary extensions exist for contractible targets.
  9 -/
 10 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 11 
 12 universe u v w
 13 
 14 variable {Γ : Type u} {D E : Type v} {X : Type w} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ]
 15 variable [TopologicalSpace D] [TopologicalSpace E] [TopologicalSpace X]
 16 variable [MulAction Γ X] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X]
 17 
 18 /-- Evaluate an equivariant map on the identity-coset slice. -/
 19 def cellEvaluation (H : Subgroup Γ) (f : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H D) X) :
 20     C(D, FixedPointSpace H X) where
 21   toFun d := ⟨f (QuotientGroup.mk 1, d), fun h => by
 22     change (h : Γ) • f (QuotientGroup.mk 1, d) = f (QuotientGroup.mk 1, d)
 23     exact (f.map_smul (h : Γ) (QuotientGroup.mk 1, d)).symm.trans
 24       (congrArg f (Prod.ext (subgroup_fixes_identity_coset H h) rfl))⟩
 25   continuous_toFun :=
 26     (f.continuous.comp (continuous_const.prodMk continuous_id)).subtype_mk _
 27 
 28 /-- Extend a continuous family of fixed points equivariantly over the orbit factor. -/
 29 def cellFromFixed (H : Subgroup Γ) (u : C(D, FixedPointSpace H X)) :
 30     EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H D) X where
 31   toFun p := orbitMap H (u p.2) p.1
 32   continuous_toFun := by
 33     let : DiscreteTopology (Γ ⧸ H) := QuotientGroup.discreteTopology (isOpen_discrete _)
 34     change Continuous (fun p : (Γ ⧸ H) × D => orbitMap H (u p.2) p.1)
 35     apply continuous_prod_of_discrete_left.mpr
 36     intro q
 37     induction q using QuotientGroup.induction_on with
 38     | H g =>
 39       change Continuous (fun d => g • (u d : X))
 40       exact (continuous_const_smul g).comp (continuous_subtype_val.comp u.continuous)
 41   map_smul' g p := (orbitMap H (u p.2)).map_smul g p.1
 42 
 43 /-- The orbit/fixed-point correspondence with a topological parameter. -/
 44 def orbitCellMapEquiv (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 45     EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H D) X ≃ C(D, FixedPointSpace H X) where
 46   toFun := cellEvaluation H
 47   invFun := cellFromFixed H
 48   left_inv f := by
 49     ext p
 50     rcases p with ⟨q, d⟩
 51     induction q using QuotientGroup.induction_on with
 52     | H g =>
 53       change g • f (QuotientGroup.mk 1, d) = f (QuotientGroup.mk g, d)
 54       apply (f.map_smul g (QuotientGroup.mk 1, d)).symm.trans
 55       apply congrArg f
 56       change (g • (QuotientGroup.mk 1 : Γ ⧸ H), d) = (QuotientGroup.mk g, d)
 57       simp only [MulAction.Quotient.smul_mk, smul_eq_mul, mul_one]
 58   right_inv u := by
 59     ext d
 60     exact one_smul Γ (u d : X)
 61 
 62 omit [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X] [DiscreteTopology Γ] in
 63 /-- Evaluation commutes with restriction along any continuous parameter map. -/
 64 theorem cellEvaluation_comp (H : Subgroup Γ) (i : C(D, E))
 65     (f : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H E) X) :
 66     cellEvaluation H (f.comp (OrbitCell.map H i)) = (cellEvaluation H f).comp i := by
 67   ext d
 68   rfl
 69 
 70 /-- An equivariant cell extension exists exactly when its fixed-point slice extends. -/
 71 theorem orbitCell_extension_iff (H : Subgroup Γ) (i : C(D, E))
 72     (f : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H D) X) :
 73     (∃ F : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H E) X, F.comp (OrbitCell.map H i) = f) ↔
 74       ∃ u : C(E, FixedPointSpace H X), u.comp i = cellEvaluation H f := by
 75   constructor
 76   · rintro ⟨F, hF⟩
 77     exact ⟨cellEvaluation H F, (cellEvaluation_comp H i F).symm.trans (congrArg (cellEvaluation H) hF)⟩
 78   · rintro ⟨u, hu⟩
 79     refine ⟨cellFromFixed H u, ?_⟩
 80     apply (orbitCellMapEquiv H).injective
 81     change cellEvaluation H ((cellFromFixed H u).comp (OrbitCell.map H i)) = cellEvaluation H f
 82     rw [cellEvaluation_comp]
 83     have he : cellEvaluation H (cellFromFixed H u) = u := (orbitCellMapEquiv H).apply_symm_apply u
 84     rw [he, hu]
 85 
 86 end BC4lean.ProperActions

DiskExtension.lean

源码 SHA-256：57fde0d5e6c94ac4e1e1cc48174e410fd808f9332008ef5b682f8e58fa95efe3

  1 import Mathlib.Topology.Homotopy.Contractible
  2 import Mathlib.Topology.Category.TopCat.Sphere
  3 
  4 /-!
  5 # Extension from the boundary of a disk
  6 
  7 A null-homotopic map from a unit sphere extends over the closed unit ball.
  8 The proof descends the null-homotopy through the radial quotient map.
  9 -/
 10 
 11 noncomputable section
 12 
 13 open Metric
 14 open scoped unitInterval
 15 
 16 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 17 
 18 universe u
 19 
 20 variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]
 21 
 22 /-- Inclusion of the unit sphere into the closed unit ball. -/
 23 def sphereInclusion : C(sphere (0 : E) 1, closedBall (0 : E) 1) :=
 24   ⟨fun x => ⟨x.1, le_of_eq x.2⟩, continuous_subtype_val.subtype_mk _⟩
 25 
 26 /-- The radial map from the cylinder on the unit sphere to the unit ball. -/
 27 def radialMap : C(I × sphere (0 : E) 1, closedBall (0 : E) 1) := by
 28   refine ⟨fun p => ⟨(p.1 : ℝ) • p.2.1, ?_⟩, ?_⟩
 29   · rw [mem_closedBall, dist_zero_right, norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs,
 30       abs_of_nonneg p.1.2.1, norm_eq_of_mem_sphere, mul_one]
 31     exact p.1.2.2
 32   · exact ((continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_fst).smul
 33       (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_snd)).subtype_mk _
 34 
 35 /-- The radial coordinate is recovered by taking the norm. -/
 36 theorem radialMap_norm (p : I × sphere (0 : E) 1) :
 37     ‖(radialMap p : E)‖ = (p.1 : ℝ) := by
 38   have hs : ‖p.2.1‖ = 1 := by simpa only [mem_sphere, dist_zero_right] using p.2.2
 39   simp [radialMap, norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg p.1.2.1, hs]
 40 
 41 /-- If the sphere is nonempty, every point of the ball has radial coordinates. -/
 42 theorem radialMap_surjective [Nonempty (sphere (0 : E) 1)] :
 43     Function.Surjective (radialMap (E := E)) := by
 44   intro x
 45   by_cases hx : x.1 = 0
 46   · obtain ⟨s⟩ := ‹Nonempty (sphere (0 : E) 1)›
 47     exact ⟨(0, s), Subtype.ext (by simp [radialMap, hx])⟩
 48   · have hn : ‖x.1‖ ≠ 0 := norm_ne_zero_iff.mpr hx
 49     let s : sphere (0 : E) 1 := ⟨‖x.1‖⁻¹ • x.1, by
 50       simp [norm_smul, hn]⟩
 51     refine ⟨(⟨‖x.1‖, norm_nonneg _, by simpa only [mem_closedBall, dist_zero_right] using x.2⟩, s), ?_⟩
 52     apply Subtype.ext
 53     change ‖x.1‖ • (‖x.1‖⁻¹ • x.1) = x.1
 54     simp [smul_smul, hn]
 55 
 56 /-- The radial cylinder is a quotient presentation of the ball. -/
 57 theorem radialMap_isQuotientMap [ProperSpace E] [Nonempty (sphere (0 : E) 1)] :
 58     Topology.IsQuotientMap (radialMap (E := E)) :=
 59   .of_surjective_continuous radialMap_surjective radialMap.continuous
 60 
 61 variable {Y : Type*} [TopologicalSpace Y]
 62 
 63 /-- A homotopy starting at a constant is constant on the radial fibers. -/
 64 theorem homotopy_factorsThrough_radialMap {y : Y} {f : C(sphere (0 : E) 1, Y)}
 65     (H : (ContinuousMap.const _ y).Homotopy f) :
 66     Function.FactorsThrough H (radialMap (E := E)) := by
 67   intro p q hpq
 68   have ht : p.1 = q.1 := Subtype.ext (by
 69     simpa only [radialMap_norm] using congrArg (fun x : closedBall (0 : E) 1 => ‖x.1‖) hpq)
 70   by_cases hzero : p.1 = 0
 71   · have hzq : q.1 = 0 := ht.symm.trans hzero
 72     change H (p.1, p.2) = H (q.1, q.2)
 73     rw [hzero, hzq, H.apply_zero, H.apply_zero]
 74     rfl
 75   · have hn : (p.1 : ℝ) ≠ 0 := fun h => hzero (Subtype.ext h)
 76     have hs : p.2 = q.2 := by
 77       apply Subtype.ext
 78       have h := congrArg (fun x : closedBall (0 : E) 1 => x.1) hpq
 79       change (p.1 : ℝ) • p.2.1 = (q.1 : ℝ) • q.2.1 at h
 80       rw [← ht] at h
 81       exact (smul_right_injective E hn) h
 82     exact congrArg H (Prod.ext ht hs)
 83 
 84 /-- A null-homotopic sphere map extends over the closed ball. -/
 85 theorem nullhomotopic_extends_ball [ProperSpace E]
 86     (f : C(sphere (0 : E) 1, Y)) (hf : f.Nullhomotopic) :
 87     ∃ F : C(closedBall (0 : E) 1, Y), F.comp sphereInclusion = f := by
 88   classical
 89   obtain ⟨y, ⟨H⟩⟩ := hf
 90   cases isEmpty_or_nonempty (sphere (0 : E) 1) with
 91   | inl h =>
 92     exact ⟨ContinuousMap.const _ y, ContinuousMap.ext (fun x => isEmptyElim x)⟩
 93   | inr h =>
 94     let q := radialMap_isQuotientMap (E := E)
 95     let F := q.lift H.symm.toContinuousMap (homotopy_factorsThrough_radialMap H.symm)
 96     refine ⟨F, ContinuousMap.ext fun s => ?_⟩
 97     have h := ContinuousMap.congr_fun (q.lift_comp H.symm.toContinuousMap
 98       (homotopy_factorsThrough_radialMap H.symm)) (1, s)
 99     change F (radialMap (1, s)) = H.symm (1, s) at h
100     rw [H.symm.apply_one] at h
101     have he : radialMap (1, s) = sphereInclusion s := Subtype.ext (one_smul ℝ s.1)
102     rw [he] at h
103     exact h
104 
105 /-- Every sphere map into a contractible space extends over the ball. -/
106 theorem contractible_extends_ball [ProperSpace E] [ContractibleSpace Y]
107     (f : C(sphere (0 : E) 1, Y)) :
108     ∃ F : C(closedBall (0 : E) 1, Y), F.comp sphereInclusion = f := by
109   apply nullhomotopic_extends_ball f
110   simpa using (id_nullhomotopic Y).comp_left f
111 
112 /-- Every map from the boundary of a Euclidean disk into a contractible space extends. -/
113 theorem contractible_extends_disk {Y : Type*} [TopologicalSpace Y] [ContractibleSpace Y]
114     (n : ℕ) (f : C(TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n, Y)) :
115     ∃ F : C(TopCat.disk.{u} n, Y), F.comp (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n).hom = f := by
116   let s : TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n ≃ₜ sphere (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) 1 :=
117     Homeomorph.ulift
118   let d : TopCat.disk.{u} n ≃ₜ closedBall (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) 1 :=
119     Homeomorph.ulift
120   obtain ⟨F, hF⟩ := contractible_extends_ball (f.comp ⟨s.symm, s.symm.continuous⟩)
121   refine ⟨F.comp ⟨d, d.continuous⟩, ContinuousMap.ext fun x => ?_⟩
122   exact ContinuousMap.congr_fun hF x.down
123 
124 end BC4lean.ProperActions

OrbitCellExtension.lean

源码 SHA-256：d96b38c4b837e7ccddd84d23d4cbb602f44629a8ff472e8cc86f1db10306d2ea

  1 import BC4lean.DiskExtension
  2 import BC4lean.OrbitCellMaps
  3 import BC4lean.FixedPointCriterion
  4 
  5 /-!
  6 # Extension over equivariant disk cells
  7 
  8 Contractibility of the H-fixed points gives extension over a Γ/H-cell.
  9 This is the single-cell extension step, not yet the full cellular mapping theorem.
 10 -/
 11 
 12 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 13 
 14 universe u v
 15 
 16 variable {Γ : Type u} {X : Type v} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ]
 17 variable [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X]
 18 
 19 /-- A map on the boundary of an orbit cell extends when the fixed-point target is contractible. -/
 20 theorem orbitCell_extends_of_contractible (H : Subgroup Γ)
 21     [ContractibleSpace (FixedPointSpace H X)] (n : ℕ)
 22     (f : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n)) X) :
 23     ∃ F : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) X,
 24       F.comp (OrbitCell.boundaryInclusion H n) = f := by
 25   apply (orbitCell_extension_iff H (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n).hom f).mpr
 26   exact contractible_extends_disk n (cellEvaluation H f)
 27 
 28 /-- The fixed-point criterion supplies extension for each finite-isotropy orbit cell. -/
 29 theorem FixedPointCriterion.orbitCell_extends (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X)
 30     (H : Subgroup Γ) [Finite H] (n : ℕ)
 31     (f : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n)) X) :
 32     ∃ F : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) X,
 33       F.comp (OrbitCell.boundaryInclusion H n) = f := by
 34   let := h.1 H inferInstance
 35   exact orbitCell_extends_of_contractible H n f
 36 
 37 end BC4lean.ProperActions

CellularExtension.lean

源码 SHA-256：6b3b2f58b2bdebef65374362441418dfcc95116f4c5159d53b124bc49dd87a0e

  1 import BC4lean.OrbitCellExtension
  2 import Mathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallObject.TransfiniteCompositionLifting
  3 
  4 /-!
  5 # Extension over equivariant CW complexes
  6 
  7 The single-cell extension property is preserved by coproducts, pushouts and
  8 sequential colimits. Thus maps into a target with contractible finite-subgroup
  9 fixed points extend over a relative equivariant CW complex.
 10 -/
 11 
 12 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 13 
 14 open CategoryTheory CategoryTheory.Limits
 15 
 16 universe u
 17 
 18 variable {Γ : Type u} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ]
 19 variable {X : Type u} [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X]
 20 
 21 /-- Each generating orbit cell lifts against the map from the target to a point. -/
 22 theorem basicCell_hasLiftingProperty (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X)
 23     (n : ℕ) (H : FiniteIsotropy Γ) :
 24     HasLiftingProperty (equivariantBasicCell n H) (terminal.from (topologicalAction Γ X)) := by
 25   constructor
 26   intro a b sq
 27   let : Finite H.val := H.property
 28   obtain ⟨F, hF⟩ := h.orbitCell_extends H.val n (EquivariantMap.ofActionHom a)
 29   refine ⟨⟨{ l := F.toActionHom, fac_left := ?_, fac_right := ?_ }⟩⟩
 30   · have hf := congrArg EquivariantMap.toActionHom hF
 31     exact hf.trans (EquivariantMap.actionHomEquiv.apply_symm_apply a)
 32   · exact terminalIsTerminal.hom_ext _ _
 33 
 34 /-- The cellwise lifting property persists across the full relative CW structure. -/
 35 theorem RelativeEquivariantCWComplex.hasLiftingProperty
 36     {A B : Action TopCat.{u} Γ} {i : A ⟶ B}
 37     (c : RelativeEquivariantCWComplex i) (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X) :
 38     HasLiftingProperty i (terminal.from (topologicalAction Γ X)) := by
 39   let W := MorphismProperty.ofHoms (fun p : ℕ × FiniteIsotropy Γ =>
 40     equivariantBasicCell p.1 p.2)
 41   have hc : ∀ s : c.Cells, W (equivariantBasicCell s.j s.i) :=
 42     fun s => MorphismProperty.ofHoms.mk (s.j, s.i)
 43   have hr : W.rlp (terminal.from (topologicalAction Γ X)) := by
 44     intro A B f hf
 45     cases hf with
 46     | mk p => exact basicCell_hasLiftingProperty h p.1 p.2
 47   exact W.transfiniteCompositionsOfShape_pushouts_coproducts_le_llp_rlp ℕ i
 48     ⟨c.transfiniteCompositionOfShape' hc⟩ _ hr
 49 
 50 /-- Maps into a target satisfying the fixed-point criterion extend over relative CW complexes. -/
 51 theorem RelativeEquivariantCWComplex.extend
 52     {A B : Action TopCat.{u} Γ} {i : A ⟶ B}
 53     (c : RelativeEquivariantCWComplex i) (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X)
 54     (f : A ⟶ topologicalAction Γ X) :
 55     ∃ F : B ⟶ topologicalAction Γ X, i ≫ F = f := by
 56   let := c.hasLiftingProperty h
 57   let sq : CommSq f i (terminal.from (topologicalAction Γ X)) (terminal.from B) :=
 58     ⟨terminalIsTerminal.hom_ext _ _⟩
 59   exact ⟨sq.lift, sq.fac_left⟩
 60 
 61 /-- Every equivariant CW complex admits a map into a target satisfying the fixed-point criterion. -/
 62 theorem EquivariantCWComplex.nonempty_map
 63     {A : Action TopCat.{u} Γ} (c : EquivariantCWComplex A) (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X) :
 64     Nonempty (A ⟶ topologicalAction Γ X) := by
 65   obtain ⟨F, _⟩ := c.extend h (initial.to _)
 66   exact ⟨F⟩
 67 
 68 end BC4lean.ProperActions

CylinderBoundary.lean

源码 SHA-256：2dec4351a723bee828d46047c77d07e282e2e40794f61f481d7a44a6dc8e13e0

  1 import BC4lean.DiskExtension
  2 
  3 /-! # A quotient presentation of the boundary of a disk cylinder
  4 
  5 The product norm on ℝ × E is the max norm. Its unit sphere consists of the
  6 two disk faces and the cylinder on the unit sphere of E.
  7 -/
  8 
  9 noncomputable section
 10 open Metric
 11 open scoped unitInterval
 12 
 13 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 14 
 15 variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
 16 
 17 /-- Affine coordinate identifying the unit interval with [-1,1]. -/
 18 def signedTime (t : I) : ℝ := 2 * (t : ℝ) - 1
 19 
 20 @[simp] theorem signedTime_zero : signedTime 0 = -1 := by norm_num [signedTime]
 21 @[simp] theorem signedTime_one : signedTime 1 = 1 := by norm_num [signedTime]
 22 
 23 theorem abs_signedTime_le (t : I) : |signedTime t| ≤ 1 := by
 24   rw [abs_le]
 25   have h := t.2
 26   dsimp [signedTime]
 27   constructor <;> linarith [h.1, h.2]
 28 
 29 theorem signedTime_injective : Function.Injective signedTime := by
 30   intro t u h
 31   apply Subtype.ext
 32   dsimp [signedTime] at h
 33   linarith
 34 
 35 /-- The cylinder parametrized as the max-norm ball. -/
 36 def cylinderBall : C(I × closedBall (0 : E) 1, closedBall (0 : ℝ × E) 1) := by
 37   refine ⟨fun p => ⟨(signedTime p.1, p.2.1), ?_⟩, ?_⟩
 38   · rw [mem_closedBall, dist_zero_right, Prod.norm_mk, Real.norm_eq_abs, max_le_iff]
 39     exact ⟨abs_signedTime_le _, by simpa only [mem_closedBall, dist_zero_right] using p.2.2⟩
 40   · exact (((continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_fst).const_mul 2 |>.sub continuous_const).prodMk
 41       (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_snd)).subtype_mk _
 42 
 43 /-- The lower disk face of the cylinder boundary. -/
 44 def cylinderFaceZero : C(closedBall (0 : E) 1, sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1) := by
 45   refine ⟨fun x => ⟨(-1, x.1), ?_⟩, ?_⟩
 46   · have hx : ‖x.1‖ ≤ 1 := by simpa only [mem_closedBall, dist_zero_right] using x.2
 47     simp only [mem_sphere, dist_zero_right, Prod.norm_mk, norm_neg, norm_one, max_eq_left hx]
 48   · exact (continuous_const.prodMk continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
 49 
 50 /-- The upper disk face of the cylinder boundary. -/
 51 def cylinderFaceOne : C(closedBall (0 : E) 1, sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1) := by
 52   refine ⟨fun x => ⟨(1, x.1), ?_⟩, ?_⟩
 53   · have hx : ‖x.1‖ ≤ 1 := by simpa only [mem_closedBall, dist_zero_right] using x.2
 54     simp only [mem_sphere, dist_zero_right, Prod.norm_mk, norm_one, max_eq_left hx]
 55   · exact (continuous_const.prodMk continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
 56 
 57 /-- The cylindrical side of the boundary. -/
 58 def cylinderSide : C(I × sphere (0 : E) 1, sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1) := by
 59   refine ⟨fun p => ⟨(signedTime p.1, p.2.1), ?_⟩, ?_⟩
 60   · simp only [mem_sphere, dist_zero_right, Prod.norm_mk, Real.norm_eq_abs,
 61       norm_eq_of_mem_sphere, max_eq_right (abs_signedTime_le p.1)]
 62   · exact (((continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_fst).const_mul 2 |>.sub continuous_const).prodMk
 63       (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_snd)).subtype_mk _
 64 
 65 /-- Disjoint union of the two faces and the cylindrical side. -/
 66 abbrev CylinderBoundaryPieces (E : Type*) [NormedAddCommGroup E] :=
 67   closedBall (0 : E) 1 ⊕ (closedBall (0 : E) 1 ⊕ (I × sphere (0 : E) 1))
 68 
 69 /-- Glue the three boundary pieces into the max-norm unit sphere. -/
 70 def cylinderBoundaryQuotient : C(CylinderBoundaryPieces E, sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1) :=
 71   ⟨Sum.elim cylinderFaceZero (Sum.elim cylinderFaceOne cylinderSide),
 72     continuous_sum_dom.mpr ⟨cylinderFaceZero.continuous,
 73       continuous_sum_dom.mpr ⟨cylinderFaceOne.continuous, cylinderSide.continuous⟩⟩⟩
 74 
 75 /-- The three pieces cover the sphere, including in dimension zero. -/
 76 theorem cylinderBoundaryQuotient_surjective :
 77     Function.Surjective (cylinderBoundaryQuotient (E := E)) := by
 78   intro x
 79   have hx : max |x.1.1| ‖x.1.2‖ = 1 := by
 80     simpa only [mem_sphere, dist_zero_right, Prod.norm_def, Real.norm_eq_abs] using x.2
 81   have hb : ‖x.1.2‖ ≤ 1 := (le_max_right _ _).trans (le_of_eq hx)
 82   let b : closedBall (0 : E) 1 := ⟨x.1.2, by simpa only [mem_closedBall, dist_zero_right] using hb⟩
 83   by_cases h0 : x.1.1 = -1
 84   · exact ⟨Sum.inl b, Subtype.ext (Prod.ext h0.symm rfl)⟩
 85   by_cases h1 : x.1.1 = 1
 86   · exact ⟨Sum.inr (Sum.inl b), Subtype.ext (Prod.ext h1.symm rfl)⟩
 87   have hr : |x.1.1| ≤ 1 := (le_max_left _ _).trans (le_of_eq hx)
 88   have hn : ‖x.1.2‖ = 1 := by
 89     rcases max_eq_iff.mp hx with h | h
 90     · rcases (abs_eq (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 1)).mp h.1 with h | h
 91       · exact False.elim (h1 h)
 92       · exact False.elim (h0 h)
 93     · exact h.1
 94   let t : I := ⟨(x.1.1 + 1) / 2, by constructor <;> linarith [abs_le.mp hr]⟩
 95   let s : sphere (0 : E) 1 := ⟨x.1.2, by simpa only [mem_sphere, dist_zero_right] using hn⟩
 96   refine ⟨Sum.inr (Sum.inr (t,s)), Subtype.ext (Prod.ext ?_ rfl)⟩
 97   change 2 * ((x.1.1 + 1) / 2) - 1 = x.1.1
 98   ring
 99 
100 /-- Compactness of the pieces makes the boundary presentation a quotient map. -/
101 theorem cylinderBoundaryQuotient_isQuotientMap [ProperSpace E] :
102     Topology.IsQuotientMap (cylinderBoundaryQuotient (E := E)) :=
103   .of_surjective_continuous cylinderBoundaryQuotient_surjective cylinderBoundaryQuotient.continuous
104 
105 end BC4lean.ProperActions

DiskHomotopyExtension.lean

源码 SHA-256：916cd5746a16fcdfc63cbe7543180bc15dad91ac2377d45578e12866f74f4bec

  1 import BC4lean.CylinderBoundary
  2 
  3 /-! # Extending prescribed disk endpoint maps and a boundary homotopy -/
  4 
  5 noncomputable section
  6 open Metric
  7 open scoped unitInterval
  8 
  9 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 10 
 11 variable {E Y : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [TopologicalSpace Y]
 12 variable (f₀ f₁ : C(closedBall (0 : E) 1, Y))
 13 variable (H : (f₀.comp sphereInclusion).Homotopy (f₁.comp sphereInclusion))
 14 
 15 /-- Values prescribed on the two disk faces and their common cylindrical side. -/
 16 def cylinderBoundaryData : C(CylinderBoundaryPieces E, Y) :=
 17   ⟨Sum.elim f₀ (Sum.elim f₁ H),
 18     continuous_sum_dom.mpr ⟨f₀.continuous,
 19       continuous_sum_dom.mpr ⟨f₁.continuous, H.continuous⟩⟩⟩
 20 
 21 private theorem faceZero_side_agree (x : closedBall (0 : E) 1) (p : I × sphere (0 : E) 1)
 22     (h : cylinderFaceZero x = cylinderSide p) : f₀ x = H p := by
 23   have ht : (0 : I) = p.1 := signedTime_injective (by
 24     rw [signedTime_zero]
 25     exact congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.1) h)
 26   have hx : x = sphereInclusion p.2 :=
 27     Subtype.ext (congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.2) h)
 28   change f₀ x = H (p.1, p.2)
 29   rw [← ht, H.apply_zero]
 30   exact congrArg f₀ hx
 31 
 32 private theorem faceOne_side_agree (x : closedBall (0 : E) 1) (p : I × sphere (0 : E) 1)
 33     (h : cylinderFaceOne x = cylinderSide p) : f₁ x = H p := by
 34   have ht : (1 : I) = p.1 := signedTime_injective (by
 35     rw [signedTime_one]
 36     exact congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.1) h)
 37   have hx : x = sphereInclusion p.2 :=
 38     Subtype.ext (congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.2) h)
 39   change f₁ x = H (p.1, p.2)
 40   rw [← ht, H.apply_one]
 41   exact congrArg f₁ hx
 42 
 43 /-- The endpoint identities are exactly the compatibility conditions for boundary gluing. -/
 44 theorem cylinderBoundaryData_factorsThrough :
 45     Function.FactorsThrough (cylinderBoundaryData f₀ f₁ H) cylinderBoundaryQuotient := by
 46   intro p q h
 47   rcases p with x | x
 48   · rcases q with y | y
 49     · exact congrArg f₀ (Subtype.ext (congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.2) h))
 50     · rcases y with y | y
 51       · have ht := congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.1) h
 52         change (-1 : ℝ) = 1 at ht
 53         norm_num at ht
 54       · exact faceZero_side_agree f₀ f₁ H x y h
 55   · rcases x with x | x
 56     · rcases q with y | y
 57       · have ht := congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.1) h
 58         change (1 : ℝ) = -1 at ht
 59         norm_num at ht
 60       · rcases y with y | y
 61         · exact congrArg f₁ (Subtype.ext (congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.2) h))
 62         · exact faceOne_side_agree f₀ f₁ H x y h
 63     · rcases q with y | y
 64       · exact (faceZero_side_agree f₀ f₁ H y x h.symm).symm
 65       · rcases y with y | y
 66         · exact (faceOne_side_agree f₀ f₁ H y x h.symm).symm
 67         · apply congrArg H
 68           exact Prod.ext
 69             (signedTime_injective (congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.1) h))
 70             (Subtype.ext (congrArg (fun z : sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1 => z.1.2) h))
 71 
 72 /-- Glue endpoint maps and a boundary homotopy into a map on the cylinder boundary. -/
 73 def gluedCylinderBoundary [ProperSpace E] : C(sphere (0 : ℝ × E) 1, Y) :=
 74   cylinderBoundaryQuotient_isQuotientMap.lift (cylinderBoundaryData f₀ f₁ H)
 75     (cylinderBoundaryData_factorsThrough f₀ f₁ H)
 76 
 77 @[simp] theorem gluedCylinderBoundary_on_piece [ProperSpace E] (p : CylinderBoundaryPieces E) :
 78     gluedCylinderBoundary f₀ f₁ H (cylinderBoundaryQuotient p) =
 79       cylinderBoundaryData f₀ f₁ H p :=
 80   ContinuousMap.congr_fun (cylinderBoundaryQuotient_isQuotientMap.lift_comp
 81     (cylinderBoundaryData f₀ f₁ H) (cylinderBoundaryData_factorsThrough f₀ f₁ H)) p
 82 
 83 variable [NormedSpace ℝ E]
 84 
 85 /-- Into a contractible target, a sphere homotopy extends over the ball with both endpoints fixed. -/
 86 theorem contractible_extends_ball_homotopy [ProperSpace E] [ContractibleSpace Y] :
 87     ∃ F : f₀.Homotopy f₁, ∀ t s, F (t, sphereInclusion s) = H (t, s) := by
 88   obtain ⟨F, hF⟩ := contractible_extends_ball (gluedCylinderBoundary f₀ f₁ H)
 89   have he (p : CylinderBoundaryPieces E) :
 90       F (sphereInclusion (cylinderBoundaryQuotient p)) = cylinderBoundaryData f₀ f₁ H p :=
 91     (ContinuousMap.congr_fun hF (cylinderBoundaryQuotient p)).trans
 92       (gluedCylinderBoundary_on_piece f₀ f₁ H p)
 93   refine ⟨{ toContinuousMap := F.comp cylinderBall,
 94             map_zero_left := ?_, map_one_left := ?_ }, ?_⟩
 95   · intro x
 96     change F (cylinderBall (0,x)) = f₀ x
 97     calc
 98       F (cylinderBall (0,x)) = F (sphereInclusion (cylinderBoundaryQuotient (Sum.inl x))) := by
 99         apply congrArg F
100         apply Subtype.ext
101         exact Prod.ext signedTime_zero rfl
102       _ = _ := he (Sum.inl x)
103   · intro x
104     change F (cylinderBall (1,x)) = f₁ x
105     calc
106       F (cylinderBall (1,x)) = F (sphereInclusion (cylinderBoundaryQuotient (Sum.inr (Sum.inl x)))) := by
107         apply congrArg F
108         apply Subtype.ext
109         exact Prod.ext signedTime_one rfl
110       _ = _ := he (Sum.inr (Sum.inl x))
111   · intro t s
112     exact he (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (t,s)))
113 
114 end BC4lean.ProperActions

  1 import BC4lean.DiskHomotopyExtension
  2 
  3 /-! # Path lifting across the standard disk boundary inclusion -/
  4

  6 open scoped unitInterval
  7 
  8 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
