 11 
 12 /-- A prescribed boundary homotopy extends over a standard disk, with endpoints unchanged. -/
 13 theorem contractible_extends_disk_homotopy {Y : Type*} [TopologicalSpace Y]
 14     [ContractibleSpace Y] (n : ℕ) (f₀ f₁ : C(TopCat.disk.{u} n, Y))
 15     (H : (f₀.comp (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n).hom).Homotopy
 16       (f₁.comp (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n).hom)) :
 17     ∃ F : f₀.Homotopy f₁, ∀ t s, F (t, (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n) s) = H (t,s) := by
 18   let b : C(Metric.closedBall (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) 1, TopCat.disk.{u} n) :=
 19     ⟨ULift.up, continuous_uliftUp⟩
 20   let s : C(Metric.sphere (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) 1, TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n) :=
 21     ⟨ULift.up, continuous_uliftUp⟩
 22   let h : ((f₀.comp b).comp sphereInclusion).Homotopy ((f₁.comp b).comp sphereInclusion) :=
 23     H.compContinuousMap s
 24   obtain ⟨F, hF⟩ := contractible_extends_ball_homotopy (f₀.comp b) (f₁.comp b) h
 25   let d : C(TopCat.disk.{u} n, Metric.closedBall (0 : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin n)) 1) :=
 26     ⟨ULift.down, continuous_uliftDown⟩
 27   exact ⟨F.compContinuousMap d, fun t x => hF t x.down⟩
 28 
 29 /-- Endpoint-preserving extension of a continuous family of boundary paths. -/
 30 theorem contractible_disk_path_lifting {Y : Type*} [TopologicalSpace Y]
 31     [ContractibleSpace Y] (n : ℕ)
 32     (a : C(TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n, C(I,Y))) (f₀ f₁ : C(TopCat.disk.{u} n, Y))
 33     (h₀ : ∀ s, a s 0 = f₀ ((TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n) s))
 34     (h₁ : ∀ s, a s 1 = f₁ ((TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n) s)) :
 35     ∃ F : C(TopCat.disk.{u} n, C(I,Y)),
 36       (∀ s, F ((TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n) s) = a s) ∧
 37       (∀ x, F x 0 = f₀ x) ∧ (∀ x, F x 1 = f₁ x) := by
 38   let H : (f₀.comp (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n).hom).Homotopy
 39       (f₁.comp (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n).hom) :=
 40     { toContinuousMap := a.uncurry.comp ContinuousMap.prodSwap,
 41       map_zero_left := h₀, map_one_left := h₁ }
 42   obtain ⟨G, hG⟩ := contractible_extends_disk_homotopy n f₀ f₁ H
 43   refine ⟨(G.toContinuousMap.comp ContinuousMap.prodSwap).curry, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
 44   · intro s
 45     exact ContinuousMap.ext (fun t => hG t s)
 46   · exact G.apply_zero
 47   · exact G.apply_one
 48 
 49 end BC4lean.ProperActions

EquivariantPathLifting.lean

源码 SHA-256：b554da811b2a95fb6ba3aeff73ecc81428400d2112c2fbed62d3c3e05bd62464

  1 import BC4lean.DiskPathLifting
  2 import BC4lean.CellularExtension
  3 import Mathlib.Topology.ContinuousMap.Algebra
  4 
  5 /-! # Equivariant path evaluation and lifting across orbit cells -/
  6 
  7 noncomputable section
  8 open scoped unitInterval
  9 
 10 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions
 11 
 12 universe u v
 13 
 14 variable {Γ : Type u} {X : Type v} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace X]
 15 variable [MulAction Γ X] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X]
 16 
 17 /-- Evaluation of an equivariant path at a fixed time. -/
 18 def pathEvaluation (t : I) : EquivariantMap Γ C(I,X) X where
 19   toFun p := p t
 20   continuous_toFun := continuous_eval_const t
 21   map_smul' _ _ := rfl
 22 
 23 /-- A path fixed by H is a continuous path in the H-fixed-point space. -/
 24 def fixedPathMap (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 25     C(FixedPointSpace H C(I,X), C(I,FixedPointSpace H X)) where
 26   toFun p :=
 27     ⟨fun t => ⟨p.1 t, fun h => congrArg (fun q : C(I,X) => q t) (p.2 h)⟩,
 28       p.1.continuous.subtype_mk _⟩
 29   continuous_toFun := ContinuousMap.continuous_of_continuous_uncurry _
 30     (((continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_fst).eval continuous_snd).subtype_mk _)
 31 
 32 /-- A path in the fixed-point space gives an H-fixed path. -/
 33 def pathFixedMap (H : Subgroup Γ) :
 34     C(C(I,FixedPointSpace H X), FixedPointSpace H C(I,X)) where
 35   toFun p := ⟨⟨fun t => (p t).1, continuous_subtype_val.comp p.continuous⟩,
 36     fun h => ContinuousMap.ext (fun t => (p t).2 h)⟩
 37   continuous_toFun :=
 38     (ContinuousMap.continuous_postcomp ⟨Subtype.val, continuous_subtype_val⟩).subtype_mk _
 39 
 40 @[simp] theorem pathFixedMap_fixedPathMap (H : Subgroup Γ) (p : FixedPointSpace H C(I,X)) :
 41     pathFixedMap H (fixedPathMap H p) = p := by
 42   apply Subtype.ext
 43   exact ContinuousMap.ext (fun _ => rfl)
 44 
 45 @[simp] theorem fixedPathMap_pathFixedMap (H : Subgroup Γ) (p : C(I,FixedPointSpace H X)) :
 46     fixedPathMap H (pathFixedMap H p) = p := by
 47   exact ContinuousMap.ext (fun _ => rfl)
 48 
 49 variable [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ]
 50 
 51 /-- Lift a family of boundary paths with prescribed disk endpoints across an orbit cell. -/
 52 theorem orbitCell_path_lifting (H : Subgroup Γ) [ContractibleSpace (FixedPointSpace H X)]
 53     (n : ℕ) (a : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.diskBoundary.{u} n)) C(I,X))
 54     (f₀ f₁ : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) X)
 55     (h₀ : (pathEvaluation 0).comp a = f₀.comp (OrbitCell.boundaryInclusion H n))
 56     (h₁ : (pathEvaluation 1).comp a = f₁.comp (OrbitCell.boundaryInclusion H n)) :
 57     ∃ F : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) C(I,X),
 58       F.comp (OrbitCell.boundaryInclusion H n) = a ∧
 59       (pathEvaluation 0).comp F = f₀ ∧ (pathEvaluation 1).comp F = f₁ := by
 60   let a' := (fixedPathMap H).comp (cellEvaluation H a)
 61   obtain ⟨v, hv, hzero, hone⟩ := contractible_disk_path_lifting n a'
 62     (cellEvaluation H f₀) (cellEvaluation H f₁)
 63     (fun s => Subtype.ext (congrArg (fun f => f (QuotientGroup.mk 1,s)) h₀))
 64     (fun s => Subtype.ext (congrArg (fun f => f (QuotientGroup.mk 1,s)) h₁))
 65   let F := cellFromFixed H ((pathFixedMap H).comp v)
 66   have he : cellEvaluation H F = (pathFixedMap H).comp v :=
 67     (orbitCellMapEquiv H).apply_symm_apply _
 68   refine ⟨F, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
 69   · apply (orbitCellMapEquiv H).injective
 70     change cellEvaluation H (F.comp (OrbitCell.map H (TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n).hom)) =
 71       cellEvaluation H a
 72     rw [cellEvaluation_comp, he]
 73     apply ContinuousMap.ext
 74     intro s
 75     change pathFixedMap H (v ((TopCat.diskBoundaryInclusion n) s)) = cellEvaluation H a s
 76     rw [hv]
 77     exact pathFixedMap_fixedPathMap H _
 78   · apply (orbitCellMapEquiv H).injective
 79     apply ContinuousMap.ext
 80     intro x
 81     apply Subtype.ext
 82     have hx := congrArg (fun p : FixedPointSpace H C(I,X) => p.1 0)
 83       (ContinuousMap.congr_fun he x)
 84     exact hx.trans (congrArg Subtype.val (hzero x))
 85   · apply (orbitCellMapEquiv H).injective
 86     apply ContinuousMap.ext
 87     intro x
 88     apply Subtype.ext
 89     have hx := congrArg (fun p : FixedPointSpace H C(I,X) => p.1 1)
 90       (ContinuousMap.congr_fun he x)
 91     exact hx.trans (congrArg Subtype.val (hone x))
 92 
 93 end BC4lean.ProperActions

CellularHomotopy.lean

  1 import BC4lean.EquivariantPathLifting
  2 
  3 /-! # Homotopy uniqueness for maps from equivariant CW complexes -/
  4 
  5 noncomputable section
  6 open CategoryTheory CategoryTheory.Limits
  7 open scoped unitInterval
  8 
  9 namespace BC4lean.ProperActions

 11 universe u
 12 
 13 variable {Γ : Type u} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [DiscreteTopology Γ]
 14 variable {X : Type u} [TopologicalSpace X] [MulAction Γ X] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ X]
 15 
 16 /-- The equivariant map recording both endpoints of a path. -/
 17 def pathEndpointPair : EquivariantMap Γ C(I,X) (X × X) where
 18   toFun p := (p 0, p 1)
 19   continuous_toFun := (continuous_eval_const 0).prodMk (continuous_eval_const 1)
 20   map_smul' _ _ := rfl
 21 
 22 /-- Contractible fixed-point targets lift orbit cells against path endpoint evaluation. -/
 23 theorem basicCell_path_hasLiftingProperty (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X)
 24     (n : ℕ) (H : FiniteIsotropy Γ) :
 25     HasLiftingProperty (equivariantBasicCell n H) (pathEndpointPair (Γ := Γ) (X := X)).toActionHom := by
 26   constructor
 27   intro a b sq
 28   let : ContractibleSpace (FixedPointSpace H.val X) := h.1 H.val H.property
 29   let a' := EquivariantMap.ofActionHom a
 30   let b' := EquivariantMap.ofActionHom b
 31   let f₀ : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H.val (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) X :=
 32     { toFun := fun x => (b' x).1,
 33       continuous_toFun := continuous_fst.comp b'.continuous,
 34       map_smul' := fun g x => congrArg Prod.fst (b'.map_smul g x) }
 35   let f₁ : EquivariantMap Γ (OrbitCell H.val (TopCat.disk.{u} n)) X :=
 36     { toFun := fun x => (b' x).2,
 37       continuous_toFun := continuous_snd.comp b'.continuous,
 38       map_smul' := fun g x => congrArg Prod.snd (b'.map_smul g x) }
 39   obtain ⟨F, hi, h₀, h₁⟩ := orbitCell_path_lifting H.val n a' f₀ f₁
 40     (EquivariantMap.ext fun x => congrArg (fun k => (k.hom x).1) sq.w)
 41     (EquivariantMap.ext fun x => congrArg (fun k => (k.hom x).2) sq.w)
 42   refine ⟨⟨{ l := F.toActionHom, fac_left := ?_, fac_right := ?_ }⟩⟩
 43   · exact (congrArg EquivariantMap.toActionHom hi).trans
 44       (EquivariantMap.actionHomEquiv.apply_symm_apply a)
 45   · apply Action.Hom.ext
 46     apply TopCat.hom_ext
 47     apply ContinuousMap.ext
 48     intro x
 49     exact Prod.ext (congrArg (fun f => f x) h₀) (congrArg (fun f => f x) h₁)
 50 
 51 /-- Path endpoint lifting extends from cells to every relative equivariant CW structure. -/
 52 theorem RelativeEquivariantCWComplex.path_hasLiftingProperty
 53     {A B : Action TopCat.{u} Γ} {i : A ⟶ B}
 54     (c : RelativeEquivariantCWComplex i) (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X) :
 55     HasLiftingProperty i (pathEndpointPair (Γ := Γ) (X := X)).toActionHom := by
 56   let W := MorphismProperty.ofHoms (fun p : ℕ × FiniteIsotropy Γ => equivariantBasicCell p.1 p.2)
 57   have hc : ∀ s : c.Cells, W (equivariantBasicCell s.j s.i) :=
 58     fun s => MorphismProperty.ofHoms.mk (s.j, s.i)
 59   have hr : W.rlp (pathEndpointPair (Γ := Γ) (X := X)).toActionHom := by
 60     intro A B f hf
 61     cases hf with
 62     | mk p => exact basicCell_path_hasLiftingProperty h p.1 p.2
 63   exact W.transfiniteCompositionsOfShape_pushouts_coproducts_le_llp_rlp ℕ i
 64     ⟨c.transfiniteCompositionOfShape' hc⟩ _ hr
 65 
 66 variable {A : Type u} [TopologicalSpace A] [MulAction Γ A] [ContinuousConstSMul Γ A]
 67 
 68 /-- Any two maps from an equivariant CW complex into a fixed-point-criterion target are homotopic. -/
 69 theorem EquivariantCWComplex.homotopic (c : EquivariantCWComplex (topologicalAction Γ A))
 70     (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X) (f g : EquivariantMap Γ A X) : EquivariantlyHomotopic f g := by
 71   let b : EquivariantMap Γ A (X × X) :=
 72     { toFun := fun x => (f x, g x), continuous_toFun := f.continuous.prodMk g.continuous,
 73       map_smul' := fun a x => Prod.ext (f.map_smul a x) (g.map_smul a x) }
 74   let := c.path_hasLiftingProperty h
 75   let sq : CommSq (initial.to (topologicalAction Γ C(I,X)))
 76       (initial.to (topologicalAction Γ A)) (pathEndpointPair (Γ := Γ) (X := X)).toActionHom
 77       b.toActionHom := ⟨initialIsInitial.hom_ext _ _⟩
 78   let F := EquivariantMap.ofActionHom sq.lift
 79   have he (x : A) : (F x 0, F x 1) = (f x, g x) :=
 80     congrArg (fun k => k.hom x) sq.fac_right
 81   refine ⟨{ toHomotopy :=
 82     { toFun := fun p => F p.2 p.1,
 83       continuous_toFun := (F.continuous.comp continuous_snd).eval continuous_fst,
 84       map_zero_left := fun x => congrArg Prod.fst (he x),
 85       map_one_left := fun x => congrArg Prod.snd (he x) }, prop' := ?_ }⟩
 86   intro t a x
 87   exact congrArg (fun p : C(I,X) => p t) (F.map_smul a x)
 88 
 89 /-- The fixed-point criterion implies the homotopy-terminal mapping property for CW sources. -/
 90 theorem EquivariantCWComplex.homotopyTerminal (c : EquivariantCWComplex (topologicalAction Γ A))
 91     (h : FixedPointCriterion Γ X) : HomotopyTerminalFor Γ A X := by
 92   obtain ⟨f⟩ := c.nonempty_map h
 93   exact ⟨⟨EquivariantMap.ofActionHom f⟩, c.homotopic h⟩
 94 
 95 /-- Two equivariant CW spaces satisfying the fixed-point criterion are equivariantly homotopy equivalent. -/
 96 theorem EquivariantCWComplex.fixedPointCriterion_unique
 97     (cA : EquivariantCWComplex (topologicalAction Γ A))
 98     (cX : EquivariantCWComplex (topologicalAction Γ X))
 99     (hA : FixedPointCriterion Γ A) (hX : FixedPointCriterion Γ X) :
100     Nonempty (EquivariantHomotopyEquiv Γ A X) :=
101   homotopyTerminal_unique (cA.homotopyTerminal hX) (cX.homotopyTerminal hA)
102     (cA.homotopyTerminal hA) (cX.homotopyTerminal hX)
103 
104 end BC4lean.ProperActions
